--- File: AGENTS.md ---
0001	# Repository Guidelines for Pump
0002	
0003	## Setup
0004	
0005	1. Use **Python 3.9+**.
0006	2. Create a virtual environment `pump_env` (via conda or `python -m venv`).
0007	3. Install Python dependencies:
0008	   ```bash
0009	   pip install -r requirements.txt
0010	   ```
0011	4. Start the PostgreSQL database and web player with the start script:
0012	   ```bash
0013	   ./start_pump.sh
0014	   ```
0015	   The script requires Docker and Docker Compose to be available. It launches a
0016	   PostgreSQL container on port **45432**.
0017	5. Alternatively, ensure a PostgreSQL server is running with the credentials in
0018	   `pump.conf` (copy `pump.conf.example` on first run) and start the app with:
0019	   ```bash
0020	   python web_player.py
0021	   ```
0022	6. To inspect the SQLite music feature database, run:
0023	   ```bash
0024	   python check_db.py
0025	   ```
0026	
0027	## Contributing
0028	
0029	- Format Python code with **black** before committing.
0030	- Keep functions concise and include docstrings.
0031	- Document new configuration options in `README.md`.
0032	
0033	## Testing
0034	
0035	- Verify the server starts without errors:
0036	  ```bash
0037	  python web_player.py > /tmp/server.log 2>&1 &
0038	  sleep 3
0039	  pkill -f web_player.py
0040	  ```
0041	  Inspect `/tmp/server.log` for issues.
0042	- If you modify database logic, run `python check_db.py` to ensure the SQLite
0043	  database can be queried.
0044	
0045	## Pull Request Notes
0046	
0047	- Summarize your changes at a high level.
0048	- List modified files.
0049	- Mention whether you ran the server start test.

--- File: README.md ---
0001	![logo copy 2](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/d531b729-c97b-4de2-92e5-f631f4630227)
0002	
0003	PUMP is a local music player application with advanced audio analysis features, playlist generation, and music metadata services integration.
0004	
0005	## System Requirements
0006	
0007	- Python 3.9+
0008	- Git
0009	- Anaconda or Miniconda (recommended)
0010	- Audio libraries dependencies (see installation)
0011	
0012	## Installation on Linux
0013	
0014	### 1. Clone the Repository
0015	
0016	```bash
0017	git clone https://github.com/icewall905/pump.git
0018	cd pump
0019	```
0020	
0021	### 2. Install System Dependencies
0022	
0023	#### Debian/Ubuntu:
0024	
0025	```bash
0026	sudo apt update
0027	sudo apt install -y python3-dev python3-pip ffmpeg libasound2-dev portaudio19-dev libportaudio2 libportaudiocpp0 libsndfile1-dev git
0028	```
0029	
0030	#### Fedora/RHEL/CentOS:
0031	
0032	```bash
0033	sudo dnf install -y python3-devel ffmpeg portaudio-devel libsndfile-devel git
0034	```
0035	
0036	#### Arch Linux:
0037	
0038	```bash
0039	sudo pacman -S python python-pip ffmpeg portaudio libsndfile git
0040	```
0041	
0042	### 3. Set Up a Python Environment
0043	
0044	#### Using Conda (recommended):
0045	
0046	```bash
0047	# Install Miniconda if you don't have it
0048	wget https://repo.anaconda.com/miniconda/Miniconda3-latest-Linux-x86_64.sh
0049	bash Miniconda3-latest-Linux-x86_64.sh
0050	source ~/.bashrc
0051	
0052	# Create and activate environment
0053	conda create -n pump_env python=3.9
0054	conda activate pump_env
0055	```
0056	
0057	#### Using venv (alternative):
0058	
0059	```bash
0060	python3 -m venv venv
0061	source venv/bin/activate
0062	```
0063	
0064	### 4. Install Python Dependencies
0065	
0066	```bash
0067	pip install -r requirements.txt
0068	```
0069	
0070	If you encounter issues with librosa or other audio analysis libraries, try:
0071	
0072	```bash
0073	pip install --upgrade pip setuptools wheel
0074	pip install --only-binary=:all: numpy
0075	pip install -r requirements.txt
0076	```
0077	
0078	## Running PUMP
0079	
0080	### 1. Using the Start Script (Recommended):
0081	
0082	The start script will check dependencies, create the environment if needed, and start the application:
0083	
0084	```bash
0085	chmod +x start_pump.sh
0086	./start_pump.sh
0087	```
0088	
0089	### 2. Manual Start:
0090	
0091	If you prefer to run the application manually:
0092	
0093	```bash
0094	# Activate your environment if not already active
0095	conda activate pump_env  # or source venv/bin/activate
0096	
0097	# Run the application
0098	python web_player.py
0099	```
0100	
0101	### 3. Access the Web Interface
0102	
0103	Open your browser and navigate to:
0104	```
0105	http://localhost:8080
0106	```
0107	
0108	## Configuration
0109	
0110	On first run, PUMP creates a pump.conf file where you can configure:
0111	
0112	- Server settings (port, host)
0113	- Music library location
0114	- API keys for music services (LastFM, Spotify)
0115	- Cache settings
0116	
0117	Edit this file before starting the application again or use the Settings page in the app.
0118	
0119	## Adding Your Music
0120	
0121	1. Go to Settings in the application
0122	2. Set your music folder path
0123	3. Check "Scan recursively" if you want to include subdirectories
0124	4. Click "Start Analysis"
0125	
0126	The analysis process may take time depending on your library size as it extracts audio features for playlist generation.
0127	
0128	## Features
0129	
0130	- Music library management
0131	- Audio signal analysis
0132	- Smart playlist generation
0133	- Metadata enrichment
0134	- Recently added tracking
0135	- Advanced audio search
0136	
0137	## Performance Tips for Large Libraries
0138	1. Enable “Keep Database in Memory” in Settings → Database Performance
0139	2. Adjust “Database Cache Size” based on available RAM
0140	3. At least 4GB RAM recommended; 8GB+ for 100k tracks
0141	4. Initial analysis can take hours; subsequent scans are faster
0142	
0143	## Troubleshooting
0144	
0145	### Common Issues:
0146	
0147	1. **Audio dependencies errors**: If you encounter issues with audio libraries, try installing system dependencies mentioned above.
0148	
0149	2. **Pygame/PortAudio errors**: Some systems might need additional dependencies:
0150	   ```bash
0151	   sudo apt install -y libportmidi-dev
0152	   pip install pygame
0153	   ```
0154	
0155	3. **Permissions issues**: Ensure you have read access to your music files and write access to the application directory.
0156	
0157	4. **Database errors**: If the application fails to start due to database issues, remove the pump.db file and restart to create a fresh database.
0158	
0159	---
0160	
0161	For more details, please see the [full documentation](https://github.com/icewall905/pump/wiki) or open an issue on GitHub.


--- File: check_db.py ---
0001	import os
0002	from db_operations import optimized_connection
0003	import sqlite3
0004	
0005	def check_database():
0006	    db_path = "music_features.db"
0007	    
0008	    # Check if database file exists
0009	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
0010	        print(f"Database file '{db_path}' does not exist!")
0011	        return
0012	    
0013	    print(f"Database file '{db_path}' exists (size: {os.path.getsize(db_path)} bytes)")
0014	    
0015	    # Connect to the database using optimized_connection context manager
0016	    with optimized_connection(db_path) as conn:
0017	        cursor = conn.cursor()
0018	        
0019	        # Check tables exist
0020	        cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")
0021	        tables = cursor.fetchall()
0022	        print(f"Tables in database: {[table[0] for table in tables]}")
0023	        
0024	        # Check audio_files table
0025	        try:
0026	            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audio_files")
0027	            count = cursor.fetchone()[0]
0028	            print(f"Number of entries in audio_files table: {count}")
0029	            
0030	            if count > 0:
0031	                cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM audio_files LIMIT 5")
0032	                rows = cursor.fetchall()
0033	                print("Sample audio_files entries:")
0034	                for row in rows:
0035	                    print(f"  {row}")
0036	        except sqlite3.OperationalError as e:
0037	            print(f"Error querying audio_files: {e}")
0038	        
0039	        # Check audio_features table
0040	        try:
0041	            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audio_features")
0042	            count = cursor.fetchone()[0]
0043	            print(f"Number of entries in audio_features table: {count}")
0044	            
0045	            if count > 0:
0046	                cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM audio_features LIMIT 5")
0047	                rows = cursor.fetchall()
0048	                print("Sample audio_features entries:")
0049	                for row in rows:
0050	                    print(f"  {row}")
0051	        except sqlite3.OperationalError as e:
0052	            print(f"Error querying audio_features: {e}")
0053	
0054	if __name__ == "__main__":
0055	    check_database()


--- File: check_folder.py ---
0001	import os
0002	
0003	def check_audio_folder(folder_path="90s Happy Hits"):
0004	    """Check if the folder exists and contains audio files"""
0005	    if not os.path.exists(folder_path):
0006	        print(f"ERROR: Folder '{folder_path}' does not exist!")
0007	        return
0008	    
0009	    print(f"Folder '{folder_path}' exists")
0010	    
0011	    # Check audio files
0012	    audio_extensions = ['.mp3', '.wav', '.flac', '.ogg']
0013	    files = os.listdir(folder_path)
0014	    
0015	    print(f"Total files in folder: {len(files)}")
0016	    
0017	    audio_files = [f for f in files if any(f.lower().endswith(ext) for ext in audio_extensions)]
0018	    print(f"Audio files in folder: {len(audio_files)}")
0019	    
0020	    if audio_files:
0021	        print("First few audio files:")
0022	        for file in audio_files[:5]:
0023	            print(f"  {file}")
0024	    else:
0025	        print("No audio files found in the folder!")
0026	
0027	if __name__ == "__main__":
0028	    check_audio_folder()

0001	import os
0002	import configparser
0003	import logging
0004	
0005	logger = logging.getLogger('config')
0006	
0007	def load_config():
0008	    """Load configuration from pump.conf"""
0009	    config = configparser.ConfigParser()
0010	    
0011	    # Default configuration
0012	    default_config = {
0013	        'SERVER': {
0014	            'host': '127.0.0.1',
0015	            'port': '8080',
0016	            'debug': 'false'
0017	        },
0018	        'DATABASE': {
0019	            'host': 'localhost',
0020	            'port': '45432',
0021	            'user': 'pump',
0022	            'password': 'Ge3hgU07bXlBigvTbRSX',
0023	            'dbname': 'pump',
0024	            'min_connections': '1',
0025	            'max_connections': '10'
0026	        },
0027	        'CACHE': {
0028	            'directory': 'cache',
0029	            'max_size_mb': '500'
0030	        },
0031	        'MUSIC': {
0032	            'library': '',
0033	            'recursive': 'true'
0034	        },
0035	        'API': {
0036	            'lastfm_api_key': '',
0037	            'lastfm_api_secret': '',
0038	            'spotify_client_id': '',
0039	            'spotify_client_secret': ''
0040	        },
0041	        'SCANNER': {
0042	            'exclude_dirs': '["node_modules", "__pycache__", ".git", "$RECYCLE.BIN"]'
0043	        }
0044	    }
0045	    
0046	    # Set defaults
0047	    for section, options in default_config.items():
0048	        if not config.has_section(section):
0049	            config.add_section(section)
0050	        for key, value in options.items():
0051	            config.set(section, key, value)
0052	    
0053	    # Load from file if it exists
0054	    config_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'pump.conf')
0055	    if os.path.exists(config_path):
0056	        logger.info(f"Loading configuration from {config_path}")
0057	        config.read(config_path)
0058	    else:
0059	        logger.warning(f"Configuration file not found at {config_path}, using defaults")
0060	        
0061	    return config

--- File: db_operations.py ---
0001	import os
0002	import time
0003	import logging
0004	import configparser
0005	import logging
0006	import psycopg2
0007	from psycopg2 import pool
0008	from psycopg2.extras import DictCursor, execute_values
0009	from contextlib import contextmanager
0010	import json
0011	import re
0012	
0013	
0014	# Initialize logging
0015	logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
0016	logger = logging.getLogger('db_operations')
0017	
0018	# PostgreSQL connection pool
0019	pg_pool = None
0020	
0021	def get_config():
0022	    """Read database configuration from pump.conf"""
0023	    config = configparser.ConfigParser()
0024	    config_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'pump.conf')
0025	    if os.path.exists(config_path):
0026	        config.read(config_path)
0027	    else:
0028	        # Use default configuration
0029	        config['DATABASE'] = {
0030	            'host': 'localhost',
0031	            'port': '45432',
0032	            'user': 'pump',
0033	            'password': 'Ge3hgU07bXlBigvTbRSX',
0034	            'dbname': 'pump',
0035	            'min_connections': '1',
0036	            'max_connections': '10'
0037	        }
0038	    return config
0039	
0040	def initialize_connection_pool():
0041	    """Initialize PostgreSQL connection pool"""
0042	    global pg_pool
0043	    if pg_pool is not None:
0044	        return pg_pool
0045	
0046	    config = get_config()
0047	    db_config = config['DATABASE']
0048	
0049	    try:
0050	        pg_pool = psycopg2.pool.SimpleConnectionPool(
0051	            minconn=int(db_config.get('min_connections', 1)),
0052	            maxconn=int(db_config.get('max_connections', 10)),
0053	            host=db_config.get('host', 'localhost'),
0054	            port=db_config.get('port', '45432'),
0055	            user=db_config.get('user', 'pump'),
0056	            password=db_config.get('password', 'Ge3hgU07bXlBigvTbRSX'),
0057	            dbname=db_config.get('dbname', 'pump')
0058	        )
0059	        logger.info(f"PostgreSQL connection pool initialized on port {db_config.get('port', '45432')}")
0060	        return pg_pool
0061	    except Exception as e:
0062	        logger.error(f"Error initializing PostgreSQL connection pool: {e}")
0063	        raise
0064	
0065	def get_connection():
0066	    """Get a connection from the pool with retry logic"""
0067	    global pg_pool
0068	    if pg_pool is None:
0069	        pg_pool = initialize_connection_pool()
0070	
0071	    max_retries = 5
0072	    retry_delay = 1
0073	
0074	    for attempt in range(max_retries):
0075	        try:
0076	            conn = pg_pool.getconn()
0077	            return conn
0078	        except Exception as e:
0079	            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
0080	                logger.warning(f"Failed to get connection (attempt {attempt+1}/{max_retries}): {e}")
0081	                time.sleep(retry_delay)
0082	                retry_delay *= 2  # Exponential backoff
0083	            else:
0084	                logger.error(f"Failed to get connection after {max_retries} attempts: {e}")
0085	                raise
0086	
0087	    raise Exception("Failed to get database connection")
0088	
0089	def release_connection(conn):
0090	    """Return a connection to the pool with safety checks"""
0091	    global pg_pool
0092	    try:
0093	        if pg_pool is not None and conn is not None:
0094	            pg_pool.putconn(conn)
0095	    except Exception as e:
0096	        logger.error(f"Error returning connection to pool: {e}")
0097	        # Don't re-raise, just log the error to prevent cascading failures
0098	
0099	def execute_query(query, params=None, fetchone=False, commit=False):
0100	    """Execute a query and return results"""
0101	    conn = None
0102	    try:
0103	        # Add check for empty query
0104	        if not query or query.strip() == '':
0105	            logger.error("Attempted to execute an empty query")
0106	            return [] if not fetchone else None
0107	            
0108	        conn = get_connection()
0109	        cursor = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=DictCursor)
0110	        cursor.execute(query, params)
0111	        
0112	        if fetchone:
0113	            result = cursor.fetchone()
0114	        else:
0115	            result = cursor.fetchall()
0116	            
0117	        if commit:
0118	            conn.commit()
0119	            
0120	        return result
0121	    except Exception as e:
0122	        logger.error(f"Error executing query: {e}")
0123	        if commit:
0124	            conn.rollback()
0125	        return [] if not fetchone else None
0126	    finally:
0127	        if conn:
0128	            release_connection(conn)
0129	
0130	def execute_query_dict(query, params=None, fetchone=False, commit=False):
0131	    """Execute a query and return results as dictionaries"""
0132	    conn = None
0133	    try:
0134	        # Add check for empty query
0135	        if not query or query.strip() == '':
0136	            logger.error("Attempted to execute an empty query")
0137	            return [] if not fetchone else None
0138	            
0139	        conn = get_connection()
0140	        cursor = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=DictCursor)
0141	        cursor.execute(query, params)
0142	        
0143	        if fetchone:
0144	            result = cursor.fetchone()
0145	        else:
0146	            result = cursor.fetchall()
0147	            
0148	        if commit:
0149	            conn.commit()
0150	            
0151	        return result
0152	    except Exception as e:
0153	        logger.error(f"Error executing query: {e}")
0154	        if commit:
0155	            conn.rollback()
0156	        return [] if not fetchone else None
0157	    finally:
0158	        if conn:
0159	            release_connection(conn)
0160	
0161	def execute_many(query, params_list, commit=True):
0162	    """Execute many operations in a single transaction"""
0163	    conn = None
0164	    try:
0165	        conn = get_connection()
0166	        with conn.cursor() as cursor:
0167	            execute_values(cursor, query, params_list)
0168	            if commit:
0169	                conn.commit()
0170	            return cursor.rowcount
0171	    except Exception as e:
0172	        if conn:
0173	            conn.rollback()
0174	        logger.error(f"Database error in execute_many: {e}")
0175	        raise
0176	    finally:
0177	        if conn:
0178	            release_connection(conn)
0179	
0180	def initialize_database():
0181	    """Initialize the database with required tables"""
0182	    conn = None
0183	    try:
0184	        conn = get_connection()
0185	        with conn.cursor() as cursor:
0186	            # Create tracks table
0187	            cursor.execute("""
0188	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tracks (
0189	                    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
0190	                    file_path TEXT UNIQUE,
0191	                    title TEXT,
0192	                    artist TEXT,
0193	                    album TEXT,
0194	                    genre TEXT,
0195	                    year INTEGER,
0196	                    duration FLOAT,
0197	                    sample_rate INTEGER,
0198	                    bit_rate INTEGER,
0199	                    channels INTEGER,
0200	                    album_art_url TEXT,
0201	                    date_added TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
0202	                    liked BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
0203	                    analysis_status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
0204	                    metadata_source TEXT,
0205	                    metadata_updated_at TIMESTAMP
0206	                )
0207	            """)
0208	
0209	            # Create audio_features table
0210	            cursor.execute("""
0211	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audio_features (
0212	                    track_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES tracks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
0213	                    tempo FLOAT,
0214	                    key INTEGER,
0215	                    brightness FLOAT,
0216	                    energy FLOAT,
0217	                    danceability FLOAT,
0218	                    acousticness FLOAT,
0219	                    instrumentalness FLOAT,
0220	                    valence FLOAT,
0221	                    loudness FLOAT,
0222	                    mode INTEGER,
0223	                    time_signature INTEGER,
0224	                    analysis_version TEXT
0225	                )
0226	            """)
0227	
0228	            # Create playlists table
0229	            cursor.execute("""
0230	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS playlists (
0231	                    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
0232	                    name TEXT NOT NULL,
0233	                    description TEXT,
0234	                    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
0235	                    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
0236	                )
0237	            """)
0238	
0239	            # Create playlist_items table (note: using playlist_items in all queries)
0240	            cursor.execute("""
0241	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS playlist_items (
0242	                    playlist_id INTEGER REFERENCES playlists(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
0243	                    track_id INTEGER REFERENCES tracks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
0244	                    position INTEGER NOT NULL,
0245	                    PRIMARY KEY (playlist_id, track_id)
0246	                )
0247	            """)
0248	
0249	            # Optionally, create additional tables if they don't exist
0250	            cursor.execute("""
0251	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artist_images (
0252	                    artist TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
0253	                    image_url TEXT,
0254	                    last_updated TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
0255	                )
0256	            """)
0257	            cursor.execute("""
0258	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata_cache (
0259	                    artist TEXT,
0260	                    album TEXT,
0261	                    title TEXT,
0262	                    source TEXT,
0263	                    data TEXT,
0264	                    last_updated TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
0265	                    PRIMARY KEY (artist, album, title, source)
0266	                )
0267	            """)
0268	            cursor.execute("""
0269	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cache_stats (
0270	                    cache_type TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
0271	                    total_entries INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
0272	                    hits INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
0273	                    misses INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
0274	                    last_updated TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
0275	                )
0276	            """)
0277	            cursor.execute("""
0278	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (
0279	                    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
0280	                    value TEXT,
0281	                    last_updated TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
0282	                )
0283	            """)
0284	            conn.commit()
0285	            logger.info("Database initialized")
0286	    except Exception as e:
0287	        if conn:
0288	            conn.rollback()
0289	        logger.error(f"Error initializing database: {e}")
0290	        raise
0291	    finally:
0292	        if conn:
0293	            release_connection(conn)
0294	
0295	def insert_track(track_data):
0296	    """Insert a new track into the database"""
0297	    query = """
0298	        INSERT INTO tracks (
0299	            file_path, title, artist, album, genre, year, duration,
0300	            sample_rate, bit_rate, channels, album_art_url
0301	        ) VALUES (
0302	            %(file_path)s, %(title)s, %(artist)s, %(album)s, %(genre)s, %(year)s, %(duration)s,
0303	            %(sample_rate)s, %(bit_rate)s, %(channels)s, %(album_art_url)s
0304	        ) ON CONFLICT (file_path) DO UPDATE SET
0305	            title = EXCLUDED.title,
0306	            artist = EXCLUDED.artist,
0307	            album = EXCLUDED.album,
0308	            genre = EXCLUDED.genre,
0309	            year = EXCLUDED.year,
0310	            duration = EXCLUDED.duration,
0311	            sample_rate = EXCLUDED.sample_rate,
0312	            bit_rate = EXCLUDED.bit_rate,
0313	            channels = EXCLUDED.channels,
0314	            album_art_url = EXCLUDED.album_art_url
0315	        RETURNING id
0316	    """
0317	    try:
0318	        result = execute_query(query, track_data, fetchone=True, commit=True)
0319	        return result['id'] if result else None
0320	    except Exception as e:
0321	        logger.error(f"Error inserting track: {e}")
0322	        raise

0335	            tempo = EXCLUDED.tempo,
0336	            key = EXCLUDED.key,

0382	def search_tracks(search_term, limit=50, offset=0):
0383	    """Search tracks by title, artist or album"""
0384	    search_pattern = f"%{search_term}%"
0385	    query = """
0386	        SELECT t.*, af.energy, af.danceability, af.valence
0387	        FROM tracks t
0388	        LEFT JOIN audio_features af ON t.id = af.track_id
0389	        WHERE 
0390	            t.title ILIKE %s OR 
0391	            t.artist ILIKE %s OR 
0392	            t.album ILIKE %s
0393	        ORDER BY t.title
0394	        LIMIT %s OFFSET %s
0395	    """
0396	    params = (search_pattern, search_pattern, search_pattern, limit, offset)
0397	    try:
0398	        return execute_query(query, params)
0399	    except Exception as e:
0400	        logger.error(f"Error searching tracks: {e}")
0401	        return []

0403	def get_all_tracks(limit=None, offset=None):
0404	    """Get all tracks with optional pagination"""

0406	        query = """
0407	            SELECT t.*, af.energy, af.danceability, af.valence
0408	            FROM tracks t

0410	            ORDER BY t.artist, t.album, t.title
0411	        """

0414	            query += " LIMIT %s"
0415	            params = (limit,)
0416	            if offset is not None:
0417	                query += " OFFSET %s"
0418	                params = (limit, offset)
0419	        return execute_query(query, params)
0420	    except Exception as e:
0421	        logger.error(f"Error getting all tracks: {e}")
0422	        return []

0469	@contextmanager
0470	def optimized_connection(db_path=None, commit_at_end=True, *args, **kwargs):
0471	    """Context manager for PostgreSQL connections"""
0472	    conn = get_connection()
0473	    try:
0474	        yield conn
0475	        if commit_at_end:
0476	            conn.commit()
0477	    except Exception as e:
0478	        conn.rollback()
0479	        raise
0480	    finally:
0481	        release_connection(conn)

0506	def get_all_playlists():
0507	    """Get all playlists"""
0508	    query = """
0509	        SELECT p.*, COUNT(pi.track_id) as track_count
0510	        FROM playlists p
0511	        LEFT JOIN playlist_items pi ON p.id = pi.playlist_id
0512	        GROUP BY p.id
0513	        ORDER BY p.name
0514	    """
0515	    try:
0516	        return execute_query(query)
0517	    except Exception as e:
0518	        logger.error(f"Error getting all playlists: {e}")
0519	        return []

0521	def add_track_to_playlist(playlist_id, track_id, position=None):
0522	    """Add a track to a playlist"""
0523	    if position is None:
0524	        position_query = """
0525	            SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position), 0) + 1 AS next_position
0526	            FROM playlist_items
0527	            WHERE playlist_id = %s
0528	        """
0529	        result = execute_query(position_query, (playlist_id,), fetchone=True)
0530	        position = result['next_position'] if result else 1
0531	
0532	    query = """
0533	        INSERT INTO playlist_items (playlist_id, track_id, position)
0534	        VALUES (%s, %s, %s)
0535	        ON CONFLICT (playlist_id, track_id) DO UPDATE SET position = EXCLUDED.position
0536	    """
0537	    try:
0538	        execute_query(query, (playlist_id, track_id, position), commit=True)
0539	        return True
0540	    except Exception as e:
0541	        logger.error(f"Error adding track to playlist: {e}")
0542	        return False
0543	
0544	def get_playlist_tracks(playlist_id):
0545	    """Get all tracks in a playlist ordered by position"""
0546	    query = """
0547	        SELECT t.*, pi.position
0548	        FROM playlist_items pi
0549	        JOIN tracks t ON pi.track_id = t.id
0550	        LEFT JOIN audio_features af ON t.id = af.track_id
0551	        WHERE pi.playlist_id = %s
0552	        ORDER BY pi.position
0553	    """
0554	    try:
0555	        return execute_query(query, (playlist_id,))
0556	    except Exception as e:
0557	        logger.error(f"Error getting playlist tracks: {e}")
0558	        return []

0609	def get_all_artists():
0610	    """Get all artists with track counts and image URLs"""
0611	    query = """
0612	        SELECT 
0613	            t.artist,
0614	            COUNT(t.id) AS track_count,
0615	            ai.image_url AS artist_image_url
0616	        FROM tracks t
0617	        LEFT JOIN artist_images ai ON t.artist = ai.artist
0618	        WHERE t.artist IS NOT NULL AND t.artist != ''
0619	        GROUP BY t.artist, ai.image_url
0620	        ORDER BY t.artist
0621	    """
0622	    try:
0623	        return execute_query(query)
0624	    except Exception as e:
0625	        logger.error(f"Error getting all artists: {e}")
0626	        return []

0628	def get_all_albums():
0629	    """Get all albums with track counts and album art URLs"""
0630	    query = """
0631	        SELECT 
0632	            t.album,
0633	            t.artist,
0634	            COUNT(t.id) AS track_count,
0635	            MIN(t.album_art_url) AS album_art_url,
0636	            MIN(t.id) AS sample_track
0637	        FROM tracks t
0638	        WHERE t.album IS NOT NULL AND t.album != ''
0639	        GROUP BY t.album, t.artist
0640	        ORDER BY t.artist, t.album
0641	    """
0642	    try:
0643	        return execute_query(query)
0644	    except Exception as e:
0645	        logger.error(f"Error getting all albums: {e}")
0646	        return []

0686	def get_library_stats():
0687	    """Get statistics about the music library"""
0688	    stats_query = """
0689	        SELECT
0690	            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tracks) AS total_tracks,
0691	            (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT artist) FROM tracks WHERE artist IS NOT NULL AND artist != '') AS total_artists,
0692	            (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT album) FROM tracks WHERE album IS NOT NULL AND album != '') AS total_albums,
0693	            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tracks WHERE liked = TRUE) AS liked_tracks,
0694	            (SELECT SUM(duration) FROM tracks) AS total_duration,
0695	            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tracks WHERE audio_features.track_id IS NOT NULL) AS analyzed_tracks
0696	        FROM tracks
0697	        LEFT JOIN audio_features ON tracks.id = audio_features.track_id
0698	        LIMIT 1
0699	    """
0700	    try:
0701	        result = execute_query(stats_query, fetchone=True)
0702	        if result and result['total_duration']:
0703	            result['total_duration_hours'] = round(result['total_duration'] / 3600, 1)
0704	        return result if result else {}
0705	    except Exception as e:
0706	        logger.error(f"Error getting library stats: {e}")
0707	        return {}
0708	
0709	def save_metadata_cache(artist, album, title, source, data):
0710	    """Save metadata to the cache"""
0711	    query = """
0712	        INSERT INTO metadata_cache (artist, album, title, source, data)
0713	        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
0714	        ON CONFLICT (artist, album, title, source) DO UPDATE SET
0715	            data = EXCLUDED.data,
0716	            last_updated = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
0717	    """
0718	    try:
0719	        execute_query(query, (artist, album, title, source, json.dumps(data)), commit=True)
0720	        return True
0721	    except Exception as e:
0722	        logger.error(f"Error saving metadata cache: {e}")
0723	        return False

0725	def get_metadata_cache(artist, album, title, source):
0726	    """Get metadata from the cache"""
0727	    query = """
0728	        SELECT data
0729	        FROM metadata_cache
0730	        WHERE artist = %s AND album = %s AND title = %s AND source = %s
0731	    """
0732	    try:
0733	        result = execute_query(query, (artist, album, title, source), fetchone=True)
0734	        return json.loads(result['data']) if result else None
0735	    except Exception as e:
0736	        logger.error(f"Error getting metadata cache: {e}")
0737	        return None

0739	def update_cache_stats(cache_type, hit=False):
0740	    """Update cache statistics"""
0741	    query = """
0742	        INSERT INTO cache_stats (cache_type, total_entries, hits, misses)
0743	        VALUES (%s, 1, %s, %s)
0744	        ON CONFLICT (cache_type) DO UPDATE SET
0745	            total_entries = cache_stats.total_entries + 1,
0746	            hits = cache_stats.hits + %s,
0747	            misses = cache_stats.misses + %s,
0748	            last_updated = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
0749	    """
0750	    hit_val = 1 if hit else 0
0751	    miss_val = 0 if hit else 1
0752	    try:
0753	        execute_query(query, (cache_type, hit_val, miss_val, hit_val, miss_val), commit=True)
0754	    except Exception as e:
0755	        logger.error(f"Error updating cache stats: {e}")
0756	
0757	def get_cache_stats():

0767	    """Clear cache entries and reset stats"""

0769	    try:
0770	        conn = get_connection()
0771	        with conn.cursor() as cursor:
0772	            if cache_type:
0773	                cursor.execute("DELETE FROM metadata_cache WHERE source = %s", (cache_type,))
0774	                cursor.execute("UPDATE cache_stats SET total_entries=0, hits=0, misses=0 WHERE cache_type = %s", (cache_type,))
0775	            else:
0776	                cursor.execute("DELETE FROM metadata_cache")
0777	                cursor.execute("UPDATE cache_stats SET total_entries=0, hits=0, misses=0")
0778	            conn.commit()
0779	        return True
0780	    except Exception as e:
0781	        if conn:
0782	            conn.rollback()

0784	        return False
0785	    finally:
0786	        if conn:

0795	            value = EXCLUDED.value,
0796	            last_updated = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
0797	    """

0828	def get_similar_tracks(track_id, limit=20):
0829	    """Find similar tracks based on audio features"""
0830	    features_query = """
0831	        SELECT 
0832	            track_id,
0833	            energy,
0834	            danceability,
0835	            acousticness,
0836	            instrumentalness,
0837	            valence
0838	        FROM audio_features
0839	        WHERE track_id = %s
0840	    """
0841	    try:
0842	        ref_features = execute_query(features_query, (track_id,), fetchone=True)
0843	        if not ref_features:
0844	            return []
0845	
0846	        similar_query = """
0847	            WITH track_distances AS (
0848	                SELECT 
0849	                    t.id,
0850	                    t.title,
0851	                    t.artist,
0852	                    t.album,
0853	                    t.album_art_url,
0854	                    t.liked,
0855	                    SQRT(
0856	                        POWER(af.energy - %s, 2) * 1.0 +
0857	                        POWER(af.danceability - %s, 2) * 1.0 +
0858	                        POWER(af.brightness - %s, 2) * 0.8 +
0859	                        POWER(af.acousticness - %s, 2) * 0.8 +
0860	                        POWER(af.instrumentalness - %s, 2) * 0.8 +
0861	                        POWER(af.valence - %s, 2) * 1.2
0862	                    ) AS distance
0863	                FROM audio_features af
0864	                JOIN tracks t ON af.track_id = t.id
0865	                WHERE af.track_id != %s
0866	            )
0867	            SELECT 
0868	                id, 
0869	                title, 
0870	                artist, 
0871	                album, 
0872	                album_art_url,
0873	                liked,
0874	                distance
0875	            FROM track_distances
0876	            ORDER BY distance ASC
0877	            LIMIT %s
0878	        """
0879	        params = (
0880	            ref_features['energy'], 
0881	            ref_features['danceability'],
0882	            ref_features['brightness'],
0883	            ref_features['acousticness'],
0884	            ref_features['instrumentalness'],
0885	            ref_features['valence'],
0886	            track_id,
0887	            limit
0888	        )
0889	        return execute_query(similar_query, params)
0890	    except Exception as e:
0891	        logger.error(f"Error finding similar tracks: {e}")
0892	        return []
0893	
0894	def save_memory_db_to_disk(in_memory_conn, disk_path=None):
0895	    """
0896	    Compatibility function for PostgreSQL (no-op).
0897	    In PostgreSQL transactions are already persisted, so this is a no-op.
0898	    """
0899	    logger.info("PostgreSQL compatibility: save_memory_db_to_disk called (no-op)")
0900	    if in_memory_conn and hasattr(in_memory_conn, 'cursor'):
0901	        try:
0902	            in_memory_conn.commit()
0903	            release_connection(in_memory_conn)
0904	            logger.info("PostgreSQL connection committed and released")
0905	        except Exception as e:
0906	            logger.error(f"Error releasing PostgreSQL connection: {e}")
0907	    return True

0921	def execute_with_retry(query, params=None, max_retries=3, retry_delay=1, commit=False):
0922	    """Execute a query with retry logic if it fails"""
0923	    conn = None
0924	    retries = 0
0925	
0926	    while retries < max_retries:
0927	        try:
0928	            conn = get_connection()
0929	            cursor = conn.cursor()
0930	            cursor.execute(query, params)
0931	            if commit:
0932	                conn.commit()
0933	            if cursor.description:
0934	                result = cursor.fetchall()
0935	                return result
0936	            return None
0937	        except Exception as e:
0938	            retries += 1
0939	            logger.warning(f"Database operation failed, retry {retries}/{max_retries}: {e}")
0940	            if conn:
0941	                conn.rollback()
0942	            if retries >= max_retries:
0943	                logger.error(f"Max retries reached, operation failed: {e}")
0944	                raise
0945	            time.sleep(retry_delay)
0946	        finally:
0947	            if conn:
0948	                release_connection(conn)
0949	
0950	def execute_query_row(query, params=None, commit=False):
0951	    """Execute a query and return a single row result"""
0952	    conn = None
0953	    try:
0954	        # Add check for empty query
0955	        if not query or query.strip() == '':
0956	            logger.error("Attempted to execute an empty query")
0957	            return None
0958	            
0959	        conn = get_connection()
0960	        cursor = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=DictCursor)
0961	        cursor.execute(query, params)
0962	        
0963	        result = cursor.fetchone()
0964	            
0965	        if commit:
0966	            conn.commit()
0967	            
0968	        return result
0969	    except Exception as e:
0970	        logger.error(f"Error executing query: {e}")
0971	        if commit and conn:
0972	            conn.rollback()
0973	        return None
0974	    finally:
0975	        if conn:
0976	            release_connection(conn)
0977	
0978	def transaction_context():
0979	    """Context manager for transactions"""
0980	    class TransactionContextManager:
0981	        def __enter__(self):
0982	            self.conn = get_connection()
0983	            self.cursor = self.conn.cursor()
0984	            return self.conn, self.cursor
0985	        def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
0986	            if exc_type is None:
0987	                self.conn.commit()
0988	            else:
0989	                self.conn.rollback()
0990	            release_connection(self.conn)
0991	    return TransactionContextManager()
0992	
0993	def sanitize_for_postgres(value):
0994	    """Remove null bytes and other problematic characters from strings for PostgreSQL"""
0995	    if isinstance(value, str):
0996	        return value.replace('\x00', '')
0997	    return value

0999	def execute_write(query, params=None):
1000	    """Execute a write operation (INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE)"""
1001	    conn = None
1002	    try:
1003	        conn = get_connection()
1004	        with conn.cursor() as cursor:
1005	            # Sanitize parameters if they're a dict or list
1006	            if isinstance(params, dict):
1007	                sanitized_params = {k: sanitize_for_postgres(v) for k, v in params.items()}
1008	            elif isinstance(params, (list, tuple)):
1009	                sanitized_params = [sanitize_for_postgres(p) for p in params]
1010	            else:
1011	                sanitized_params = params
1012	                
1013	            cursor.execute(query, sanitized_params)
1014	            conn.commit()
1015	    except Exception as e:
1016	        logger.error(f"Error executing write query: {e}")
1017	        if conn:
1018	            conn.rollback()
1019	        raise
1020	    finally:
1021	        if conn:
1022	            release_connection(conn)
1023	
1024	def reset_database_locks():
1025	    """
1026	    Reset any database locks (PostgreSQL compatibility function)
1027	    """
1028	    conn = None
1029	    try:
1030	        conn = get_connection()
1031	        with conn.cursor() as cursor:
1032	            cursor.execute("""
1033	                SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid) 
1034	                FROM pg_stat_activity 
1035	                WHERE datname = current_database()
1036	                AND state = 'idle in transaction'
1037	                AND (now() - state_change) > interval '30 minutes'
1038	            """)
1039	        conn.commit()
1040	        logger.info("Database locks reset successfully")
1041	        return True
1042	    except Exception as e:
1043	        logger.error(f"Error resetting database locks: {e}")
1044	        if conn:
1045	            conn.rollback()
1046	        return False
1047	    finally:
1048	        if conn:
1049	            release_connection(conn)

1084	def set_db_config(db_path=None, in_memory=False, cache_size_mb=75):
1085	    """
1086	    Set global database configuration variables
1087	    This function now accepts parameters for backward compatibility,
1088	    but ignores them for PostgreSQL since these settings are specific to SQLite
1089	    """
1090	    global pg_pool
1091	    logger.info(f"PostgreSQL compatibility: set_db_config called (ignoring SQLite-specific parameters)")
1092	    if pg_pool is not None:
1093	        try:
1094	            for conn in pg_pool._used:
1095	                try:
1096	                    conn.close()
1097	                except Exception:
1098	                    pass
1099	            pg_pool.closeall()
1100	            pg_pool = None
1101	            logger.info("Closed existing database connection pool")
1102	        except Exception as e:
1103	            logger.error(f"Error closing connection pool: {e}")
1104	    try:
1105	        pg_pool = initialize_connection_pool()
1106	        logger.info("Database configuration updated and connection pool reinitialized")
1107	        return True
1108	    except Exception as e:
1109	        logger.error(f"Failed to reinitialize connection pool: {e}")
1110	        return False

--- File: env_load.sh ---
0001	#!/bin/sh
0002	
0003	# Name of the environment (for conda)
0004	ENV_NAME="myenv"
0005	
0006	if command -v conda >/dev/null 2>&1; then
0007	    echo "Conda found. Using conda environment."
0008	    # Check if the conda environment exists
0009	    if conda info --envs | grep -q "^${ENV_NAME}[[:space:]]"; then
0010	        echo "Activating existing conda environment: ${ENV_NAME}"
0011	        conda activate "${ENV_NAME}"
0012	    else
0013	        echo "Creating new conda environment: ${ENV_NAME}"
0014	        conda create -n "${ENV_NAME}" python -y
0015	        conda activate "${ENV_NAME}"
0016	    fi
0017	else
0018	    echo "Conda not found, falling back to virtual environment."
0019	    # Determine the Python interpreter using command -v
0020	    if command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
0021	        PYTHON=python3
0022	    elif command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1; then
0023	        PYTHON=python
0024	    else
0025	        echo "No Python interpreter found. Exiting."
0026	        exit 1
0027	    fi
0028	    # Create the virtual environment if it does not exist
0029	    if [ ! -d "env" ]; then
0030	        echo "Creating virtual environment in ./env"
0031	        $PYTHON -m venv env
0032	    fi
0033	    echo "Activating virtual environment..."
0034	    . env/bin/activate
0035	fi
0036	
0037	# Install packages from requirements.txt
0038	echo "Installing packages from requirements.txt..."
0039	if pip install -r requirements.txt; then
0040	    echo "All clear!"
0041	else
0042	    echo "Installation failed."
0043	    exit 1
0044	fi

0001	import os
0002	import fnmatch # For simpler pattern matching if pathspec isn't used for some initial checks
0003	from pathlib import Path
0004	import chardet # For robust encoding detection
0005	import pathspec # For .gitignore parsing
0006	
0007	# --- Configuration ---
0008	# Directories to explicitly always ignore (even if not in .gitignore, like .git itself)
0009	ALWAYS_IGNORED_DIRS = {'.git', '.vscode', '__pycache__', 'node_modules', 'venv', '.idea', 'dist', 'build'}
0010	# File extensions to *additionally* treat as binary (add more as needed)
0011	# .gitignore should handle most, but this is a fallback.
0012	BINARY_EXTENSIONS = {
0013	    '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.gif', '.bmp', '.tiff', '.ico',
0014	    '.pdf',
0015	    '.zip', '.tar', '.gz', '.rar', '.7z',
0016	    '.exe', '.dll', '.so', '.o', '.a', '.lib',

0022	    '.DS_Store'
