0033	def is_binary_string_heuristic(bytes_content, threshold=0.3):
0034	    """
0035	    Heuristic to check if a byte string likely contains binary data.
0036	    Checks for a significant number of non-printable ASCII characters
0037	    or null bytes.
0038	    """
0039	    if not bytes_content:
0040	        return False
0041	    # Check for null bytes, a strong indicator of binary files
0042	    if b'\x00' in bytes_content:
0043	        return True
0044	
0045	    # Check for a high proportion of non-text characters
0046	    # Common text characters (ASCII range, plus tab, LF, CR, FF, ESC)
0047	    textchars = bytearray({7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 13, 27} | set(range(0x20, 0x7f)))
0048	    non_text_char_count = sum(1 for byte in bytes_content if byte not in textchars)
0049	
0050	    if len(bytes_content) == 0: # Should not happen if not bytes_content is checked
0051	        return False
0052	    # If more than `threshold`% are non-text characters, consider it binary
0053	    return (non_text_char_count / len(bytes_content)) > threshold
0054	
0055	def get_gitignore_rules(directory):
0056	    """
0057	    Loads .gitignore rules from a specific directory.
0058	    Returns a PathSpec object.
0059	    """
0060	    gitignore_file = Path(directory) / ".gitignore"
0061	    rules = []
0062	    if gitignore_file.is_file():
0063	        try:
0064	            with open(gitignore_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
0065	                rules.extend(f.read().splitlines())
0066	        except Exception as e:
0067	            print(f"Warning: Could not read .gitignore in {directory}: {e}")
0068	    return pathspec.PathSpec.from_lines('gitwildmatch', rules) if rules else None
0069	
0070	
0071	def get_combined_gitignore_rules(start_dir, current_path):
0072	    """
0073	    Traverses up from current_path to start_dir, accumulating .gitignore rules.
0074	    Lower .gitignore files (closer to the file) take precedence for negations,
0075	    but patterns are generally additive. Pathspec handles most of this complexity.
0076	    """
0077	    all_rules = []
0078	    path = Path(current_path)
0079	    if path.is_file():
0080	        path = path.parent
0081	
0082	    # Iterate from current directory up to the root of the processing (start_dir)
0083	    while path.resolve() != Path(start_dir).parent.resolve() and path.exists() : # path >= Path(start_dir):
0084	        gitignore_file = path / ".gitignore"
0085	        if gitignore_file.is_file():
0086	            try:
0087	                with open(gitignore_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
0088	                    # Pathspec needs paths relative to the .gitignore file's location
0089	                    # However, for matching, we provide paths relative to the root.
0090	                    # The library handles this by taking the .gitignore file's location into account.
0091	                    # We just collect all lines.
0092	                    lines = [line for line in f.read().splitlines() if line.strip() and not line.strip().startswith('#')]
0093	                    all_rules.extend(lines) # Add rules from this .gitignore
0094	            except Exception as e:
0095	                print(f"Warning: Could not read or process .gitignore in {path}: {e}")
0096	        if path == Path(start_dir): # Stop once we processed .gitignore in start_dir
0097	            break
0098	        if path.parent == path: # Reached root of filesystem
0099	            break
0100	        path = path.parent
0101	
0102	    # PathSpec.from_lines expects patterns. It will handle the precedence and combination.
0103	    return pathspec.PathSpec.from_lines('gitwildmatch', all_rules) if all_rules else None
0104	
0105	def get_project_structure(root_dir_path, all_collected_files):
0106	    """
0107	    Generates a string representation of the project's directory structure
0108	    based on the files that will actually be included.
0109	    """
0110	    structure_str = "Project Structure (Files to be Included):\n"
0111	    structure_str += f"Root: {root_dir_path.name}/\n"
0112	
0113	    # Create a directory tree structure
0114	    tree = {}
0115	    for rel_path_str in all_collected_files:
0116	        parts = Path(rel_path_str).parts
0117	        current_level = tree
0118	        for part in parts:
0119	            if part not in current_level:
0120	                current_level[part] = {}
0121	            current_level = current_level[part]
0122	
0123	    def generate_tree_lines(current_level, indent_level=0):
0124	        lines = []
0125	        prefix = '    ' * indent_level
0126	        items = sorted(current_level.keys())
0127	        for i, name in enumerate(items):
0128	            connector = '└── ' if i == len(items) - 1 else '├── '
0129	            is_dir = bool(current_level[name]) # True if it's a dict (directory), False otherwise
0130	            lines.append(f"{prefix}{connector}{name}{'/' if is_dir else ''}")
0131	            if is_dir:
0132	                lines.extend(generate_tree_lines(current_level[name], indent_level + 1))
0133	        return lines
0134	
0135	    structure_str += "\n".join(generate_tree_lines(tree, 0)) # Start indent from root
0136	    structure_str += "\n\n" + "="*80 + "\n\n"
0137	    return structure_str
0138	
0139	
0140	def process_project(root_dir, output_file_path):
0141	    """
0142	    Processes the project directory respecting .gitignore and writes content.
0143	    """
0144	    root_dir_path = Path(root_dir).resolve()
0145	    collected_files_content = [] # Store (relative_path, content_type, content_or_message)
0146	
0147	    # --- First Pass: Collect all files respecting .gitignore and other rules ---
0148	    print("Phase 1: Scanning project and applying .gitignore rules...")
0149	    files_to_process = [] # List of (absolute_path, relative_path)
0150	
0151	    # Load root .gitignore if present (for initial directory pruning)
0152	    # More specific .gitignores will be checked during traversal
0153	    root_spec = get_gitignore_rules(root_dir_path)
0154	
0155	    for current_root_str, dirs, files in os.walk(root_dir_path, topdown=True):
0156	        current_root_path = Path(current_root_str)
0157	        relative_current_root = current_root_path.relative_to(root_dir_path)
0158	
0159	        # Combine rules from all .gitignores from root_dir_path up to current_root_path's parent
0160	        # This ensures correct handling of nested .gitignore files
0161	        # For matching files/dirs *within* current_root_path, we use spec from current_root_path itself (and parents)
0162	        effective_spec = get_combined_gitignore_rules(root_dir_path, current_root_path)
0163	
0164	        # Filter directories
0165	        original_dirs = list(dirs) # copy before modification
0166	        dirs[:] = [d for d in original_dirs if d not in ALWAYS_IGNORED_DIRS]
0167	        if effective_spec:
0168	            # Pathspec needs paths relative to the location of the .gitignore files it parsed from,
0169	            # or, more simply, give it paths relative to the 'root' you are matching against.
0170	            # Here, we give it paths relative to root_dir_path for directories.
0171	            # pathspec expects paths with '/' as separator
0172	            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if not effective_spec.match_file(str(relative_current_root / d).replace(os.sep, '/'))]
0173	        dirs.sort() # For consistent order
0174	
0175	        # Filter files
0176	        files.sort() # For consistent order
0177	        for filename in files:
0178	            file_path = current_root_path / filename
0179	            relative_path = file_path.relative_to(root_dir_path)
0180	            relative_path_str = str(relative_path).replace(os.sep, '/') # Use POSIX paths for matching
0181	
0182	            if filename in ALWAYS_IGNORED_FILES or any(fnmatch.fnmatch(filename, p) for p in ALWAYS_IGNORED_FILES): # for potential patterns in ALWAYS_IGNORED_FILES
0183	                 print(f"Skipping (always ignored file): {relative_path_str}")
0184	                 continue
0185	
0186	            if effective_spec and effective_spec.match_file(relative_path_str):
0187	                print(f"Skipping (ignored by .gitignore): {relative_path_str}")
0188	                continue
0189	
0190	            files_to_process.append((file_path, relative_path))
0191	
0192	
0193	    # --- Second Pass: Process collected files and write to output ---
0194	    print(f"\nPhase 2: Processing {len(files_to_process)} identified files...")
0195	    with open(output_file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace') as outfile:
0196	        # 1. Write Project Structure based on files that WILL be included
0197	        outfile.write(get_project_structure(root_dir_path, [str(rp) for _, rp in files_to_process]))
0198	        outfile.write("File Contents:\n")
0199	        outfile.write("="*80 + "\n\n")
0200	
0201	        # 2. Include contents of .gitignore files first
0202	        gitignore_files_processed = set()
0203	        for current_root_str, _, _ in os.walk(root_dir_path, topdown=True): # Fresh walk to find all .gitignores
0204	            current_root_path = Path(current_root_str)
0205	            if any(part in ALWAYS_IGNORED_DIRS for part in current_root_path.relative_to(root_dir_path).parts):
0206	                continue # Skip .gitignores in always ignored dirs like .git/
0207	
0208	            gitignore_path = current_root_path / ".gitignore"
0209	            if gitignore_path.is_file() and gitignore_path not in gitignore_files_processed:
0210	                relative_gitignore_path = gitignore_path.relative_to(root_dir_path)
0211	                outfile.write(f"--- File: {str(relative_gitignore_path).replace(os.sep, '/')} (.gitignore) ---\n")
0212	                try:
0213	                    with open(gitignore_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as gf:
0214	                        lines = gf.read().splitlines()
0215	                        if not lines:
0216	                            outfile.write("[Empty .gitignore file]\n")
0217	                        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
0218	                            outfile.write(f"{i+1:04d}\t{line}\n")
0219	                        outfile.write("\n\n")
0220	                    gitignore_files_processed.add(gitignore_path)
0221	                except Exception as e:
0222	                    outfile.write(f"[Error reading .gitignore file: {e}]\n\n\n")
0223	                    print(f"Error reading .gitignore {relative_gitignore_path}: {e}")
0224	
0225	
0226	        # 3. Process and write content of other files
0227	        for file_path, relative_path in files_to_process:
0228	            relative_path_str = str(relative_path).replace(os.sep, '/') # For display
0229	            outfile.write(f"--- File: {relative_path_str} ---\n")
0230	            _, extension = os.path.splitext(file_path.name.lower())
0231	
0232	            # Check for binary extensions (additional safety)
0233	            if extension in BINARY_EXTENSIONS:
0234	                outfile.write("[Binary file (by extension) - content not included]\n\n\n")
0235	                print(f"Info: File '{relative_path_str}' identified as binary by extension.")
0236	                continue
0237	
0238	            # Check file size
0239	            try:
0240	                file_size = file_path.stat().st_size
0241	                if file_size > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES:
0242	                    outfile.write(f"[File too large ({file_size / (1024*1024):.2f} MB) - content not included]\n\n\n")
0243	                    print(f"Skipping (too large): {relative_path_str}")
0244	                    continue
0245	                if file_size == 0:
0246	                    outfile.write("[Empty file]\n\n\n")
0247	                    print(f"Info: File '{relative_path_str}' is empty.")
0248	                    continue
0249	            except OSError as e:
0250	                outfile.write(f"[Error accessing file size: {e} - content not included]\n\n\n")
0251	                print(f"Error accessing file size for {relative_path_str}: {e}")
0252	                continue
0253	
0254	            # Read and process the file content
0255	            try:
0256	                with open(file_path, 'rb') as f: # Read as binary first for detection
0257	                    raw_content = f.read()
0258	
0259	                # Try to detect encoding
0260	                detected = chardet.detect(raw_content)
0261	                encoding = detected['encoding'] if detected['encoding'] and detected['confidence'] > 0.5 else 'utf-8'
0262	
0263	                # Heuristic check for binary content
0264	                if is_binary_string_heuristic(raw_content):
0265	                    # Double check if it's an extension we might sometimes want (e.g. .svg, .json if large and weird)
0266	                    # For now, trust the heuristic if it triggers
0267	                    outfile.write("[Likely binary file (content heuristic) - content not included]\n\n\n")
0268	                    print(f"Skipping (likely binary by heuristic): {relative_path_str} (Detected encoding: {encoding} with confidence {detected['confidence']})")
0269	                    continue
0270	
0271	                try:
0272	                    content = raw_content.decode(encoding, errors='replace')
0273	                except UnicodeDecodeError:
0274	                    # Fallback for stubborn cases
0275	                    content = raw_content.decode('latin-1', errors='replace')
0276	                    print(f"Warning: File {relative_path_str} decoded as 'latin-1' after '{encoding}' failed.")
0277	
0278	
0279	                lines = content.splitlines()
0280	                if not lines and file_size > 0 : # File has size but no lines after split (e.g. binary that passed heuristic or just no newlines)
0281	                    outfile.write("[File has content but no standard line breaks, or is binary and passed heuristics - partial/raw dump attempt below if small]\n")
0282	                    # For very small such files, a snippet might be okay, otherwise skip.
0283	                    if len(raw_content) < 200: # Small enough to potentially be a snippet
0284	                         outfile.write(f"Raw (first 200 bytes, possibly problematic): {raw_content[:200].decode('latin-1', errors='replace')}\n")
0285	                    else:
0286	                         outfile.write("[Content not displayed due to unusual format and size]\n")
0287	                elif not lines and file_size == 0:
0288	                    # Already handled by file_size == 0 check, but as a safeguard:
0289	                    outfile.write("[Empty file]\n")
0290	                else:
0291	                    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
0292	                        outfile.write(f"{i+1:04d}\t{line}\n")
0293	                outfile.write("\n\n")
0294	
0295	            except FileNotFoundError:
0296	                # Should not happen due to pre-collection, but good to have
0297	                outfile.write("[File not found during processing - might be a broken symlink or removed]\n\n\n")
0298	                print(f"File not found: {file_path}")
0299	            except IOError as e:
0300	                outfile.write(f"[IOError reading file: {e} - content not included]\n\n\n")
0301	                print(f"IOError reading {relative_path_str}: {e}")
0302	            except Exception as e:
0303	                outfile.write(f"[Unexpected error processing file: {e} - content not included]\n\n\n")
0304	                print(f"Unexpected error with {relative_path_str}: {e}")
0305	
0306	        print(f"\nProject content successfully written to {output_file_path}")
0307	        print(f"Please review '{output_file_path}' for any sensitive information before sharing.")
0308	        print("Consider zipping the output file if it's very large.")
0309	
0310	if __name__ == "__main__":
0311	    project_directory = os.getcwd() # Assumes the script is in the root of the project
0312	    # project_directory = "/path/to/your/repo" # Or set a specific path here for testing
0313	
0314	    output_file = Path(project_directory) / OUTPUT_FILE
0315	
0316	    print(f"Starting to process project in: {project_directory}")
0317	    print(f"Output will be saved to: {output_file}")
0318	    print(f"Always ignored Dirs: {ALWAYS_IGNORED_DIRS}")
0319	    print(f"Always ignored Files: {ALWAYS_IGNORED_FILES}")
0320	    print(f"Additional binary extensions: {BINARY_EXTENSIONS}")
0321	    print("---")
0322	
0323	    try:
0324	        process_project(project_directory, output_file)
0325	    except ImportError as e:
0326	        if 'pathspec' in str(e):
0327	            print("ERROR: The 'pathspec' library is not installed. Please install it:")
0328	            print("  pip install pathspec")
0329	        elif 'chardet' in str(e):
0330	            print("ERROR: The 'chardet' library is not installed. Please install it:")
0331	            print("  pip install chardet")
0332	        else:
0333	            print(f"ERROR: An import error occurred: {e}")
0334	        print("Script cannot continue without this dependency.")
0335	    except Exception as e:
0336	        print(f"An unexpected critical error occurred: {e}")


--- File: find_dupes.py ---
0001	import re
0002	
0003	# Open your web_player.py file
0004	with open('web_player.py', 'r') as f:
0005	    content = f.read()
0006	
0007	# Find all route definitions
0008	route_pattern = r'@app\.route\([\'"]([^\'"]+)[\'"]\)'
0009	routes = re.findall(route_pattern, content)
0010	
0011	# Find duplicates
0012	duplicates = {}
0013	for route in routes:
0014	    if route in duplicates:
0015	        duplicates[route] += 1
0016	    else:
0017	        duplicates[route] = 1
0018	
0019	# Print duplicated routes
0020	print("Duplicated routes:")
0021	for route, count in duplicates.items():
0022	    if count > 1:
0023	        print(f"'{route}' appears {count} times")


--- File: lastfm_service.py ---
0001	import requests
0002	import logging
0003	import json
0004	import os
0005	import time
0006	import hashlib
0007	import configparser
0008	
0009	class LastFMService:
0010	    """Service for fetching metadata from Last.fm API"""
0011	    
0012	    def __init__(self, api_key=None, api_secret=None, cache_dir='lastfm_cache'):
0013	        # If no API key provided, try to load from config
0014	        if not api_key or not api_secret:
0015	            self._load_config_keys()
0016	        else:
0017	            self.api_key = api_key
0018	            self.api_secret = api_secret
0019	            
0020	        self.base_url = "http://ws.audioscrobbler.com/2.0/"
0021	        self.cache_dir = cache_dir
0022	        
0023	        # Create cache directory if it doesn't exist
0024	        if not os.path.exists(cache_dir):
0025	            os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
0026	            
0027	        self.logger = logging.getLogger('lastfm_service')
0028	        
0029	        # Log API key status for debugging
0030	        if self.api_key:
0031	            self.logger.info(f"LastFM service initialized with API key: {self.api_key[:5]}...")
0032	        else:
0033	            self.logger.warning("LastFM service initialized without API key")
0034	    
0035	    def _load_config_keys(self):
0036	        """Load API keys from pump.conf file"""
0037	        try:
0038	            config = configparser.ConfigParser()
0039	            config_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'pump.conf')
0040	            if os.path.exists(config_path):
0041	                config.read(config_path)
0042	                if config.has_section('lastfm'):
0043	                    self.api_key = config.get('lastfm', 'api_key', fallback='')
0044	                    self.api_secret = config.get('lastfm', 'api_secret', fallback='')
0045	                    return
0046	            
0047	            # Fallback to hardcoded keys if needed
0048	            self.api_key = 'b8b4d3c72ac643dbd9e069c6474a0b0b'
0049	            self.api_secret = 'de0459d5ee5c5dd9f838735774d41f9e'
0050	        except Exception as e:
0051	            # Default keys if there's any error
0052	            self.api_key = 'b8b4d3c72ac643dbd9e069c6474a0b0b'
0053	            self.api_secret = 'de0459d5ee5c5dd9f838735774d41f9e'
0054	            logging.error(f"Error loading LastFM config: {e}, using default keys")
0055	
0056	    def get_artist_info(self, artist_name):
0057	        """Get artist info including images from Last.fm"""
0058	        if not self.api_key or not artist_name:
0059	            self.logger.warning(f"Missing API key or artist name")
0060	            return None
0061	            
0062	        # Check cache first
0063	        cache_file = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, f"artist_{hashlib.md5(artist_name.encode()).hexdigest()}.json")
0064	        if os.path.exists(cache_file):
0065	            try:
0066	                with open(cache_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
0067	                    cached_data = json.load(f)
0068	                    self.logger.info(f"Using cached Last.fm data for: {artist_name}")
0069	                    return cached_data
0070	            except Exception as e:
0071	                self.logger.error(f"Error reading cache file: {e}")
0072	                # Continue to API request if cache read fails
0073	    
0074	        # Make API request
0075	        try:
0076	            params = {
0077	                'method': 'artist.getinfo',
0078	                'artist': artist_name,
0079	                'api_key': self.api_key,
0080	                'format': 'json'
0081	            }
0082	            
0083	            self.logger.info(f"Making LastFM API request for artist: {artist_name}")
0084	            
0085	            response = requests.get(self.base_url, params=params, timeout=10)
0086	            
0087	            if response.status_code == 200:
0088	                try:
0089	                    data = response.json()
0090	                    
0091	                    # Check if the response contains artist data
0092	                    if 'artist' in data:
0093	                        self.logger.info(f"Received Last.fm data for {artist_name}")
0094	                        # Save to cache
0095	                        try:
0096	                            with open(cache_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
0097	                                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
0098	                        except Exception as cache_err:
0099	                            self.logger.error(f"Error writing cache: {cache_err}")
0100	                        return data
0101	                    else:
0102	                        self.logger.warning(f"No artist data found for {artist_name}")
0103	                        if 'error' in data:
0104	                            self.logger.error(f"Last.fm API error: {data.get('message', 'Unknown error')}")
0105	                except ValueError as e:
0106	                    self.logger.error(f"Error parsing Last.fm response: {e}")
0107	            else:
0108	                self.logger.error(f"Last.fm request failed with status {response.status_code}: {response.text}")
0109	                
0110	        except Exception as e:
0111	            self.logger.error(f"Exception fetching artist info: {e}")
0112	        
0113	        return None
0114	    
0115	    def get_artist_image_url(self, artist_name, cache_dir=None):
0116	        """Extract the best available artist image URL or download to cache"""
0117	        # Try standard artist info first
0118	        artist_info = self.get_artist_info(artist_name)
0119	        default_image = "https://lastfm.freetls.fastly.net/i/u/300x300/2a96cbd8b46e442fc41c2b86b821562f.png"
0120	        
0121	        try:
0122	            if artist_info and 'artist' in artist_info:
0123	                # Get image array
0124	                images = artist_info['artist'].get('image', [])
0125	                
0126	                # Find a non-default image by checking the largest ones first
0127	                for img in reversed(images):
0128	                    image_url = img.get('#text', '')
0129	                    if image_url and not image_url.endswith('2a96cbd8b46e442fc41c2b86b821562f.png'):
0130	                        self.logger.info(f"Found good image for {artist_name}: {image_url}")
0131	                        
0132	                        # If we have a cache directory, download the image
0133	                        if cache_dir:
0134	                            try:
0135	                                # Create a hash of the URL for the filename
0136	                                url_hash = hashlib.md5(image_url.encode()).hexdigest()
0137	                                cache_path = os.path.join(cache_dir, f"artist_{url_hash}.jpg")
0138	                                
0139	                                # If already cached, return the path
0140	                                if os.path.exists(cache_path):
0141	                                    self.logger.debug(f"Artist image already in cache: {cache_path}")
0142	                                    return cache_path
0143	                                
0144	                                # Create cache directory if it doesn't exist
0145	                                if not os.path.exists(cache_dir):
0146	                                    self.logger.info(f"Creating artist image cache directory: {cache_dir}")
0147	                                    os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
0148	                                
0149	                                # Otherwise download and save it
0150	                                response = requests.get(image_url, timeout=10)
0151	                                if response.status_code == 200:
0152	                                    # Save the image to cache
0153	                                    with open(cache_path, 'wb') as f:
0154	                                        f.write(response.content)
0155	                                    self.logger.info(f"Saved artist image to {cache_path}")
0156	                                    return cache_path
0157	                            except Exception as e:
0158	                                self.logger.error(f"Error caching artist image: {e}")
0159	                                # Fall back to returning the URL if caching fails
0160	                        
0161	                        return image_url
0162	                
0163	                # If we get here, we only found default images, return the largest one
0164	                if images and images[-1].get('#text'):
0165	                    return images[-1].get('#text')
0166	                
0167	            # If we couldn't find anything good, try the similar artists
0168	            similar_artists = self.get_similar_artists(artist_name)
0169	            if similar_artists and len(similar_artists) > 0:
0170	                # Check the first similar artist's image
0171	                similar_artist = similar_artists[0].get('name')
0172	                if similar_artist:
0173	                    self.logger.info(f"Trying image from similar artist: {similar_artist}")
0174	                    return self.get_artist_image_url(similar_artist, cache_dir)  # Recursive call
0175	            
0176	            return None  # No suitable image found
0177	        
0178	        except Exception as e:
0179	            self.logger.error(f"Error extracting artist image: {e}")
0180	            return None
0181	
0182	    def get_artist_image_from_albums(self, artist_name):
0183	        """Get artist image from their top albums as fallback"""
0184	        if not self.api_key or not artist_name:
0185	            return None
0186	            
0187	        try:
0188	            params = {
0189	                'method': 'artist.getTopAlbums',
0190	                'artist': artist_name,
0191	                'api_key': self.api_key,
0192	                'format': 'json',
0193	                'limit': 5  # Check top 5 albums
0194	            }
0195	            
0196	            response = requests.get(self.base_url, params=params, timeout=10)
0197	            
0198	            if response.status_code == 200:
0199	                data = response.json()
0200	                
0201	                if 'topalbums' in data and 'album' in data['topalbums']:
0202	                    albums = data['topalbums']['album']
0203	                    
0204	                    # Try to get an album image
0205	                    for album in albums:
0206	                        images = album.get('image', [])
0207	                        for img in images:
0208	                            if img.get('size') in ['extralarge', 'large'] and img.get('#text'):
0209	                                if not img.get('#text').endswith('2a96cbd8b46e442fc41c2b86b821562f.png'):
0210	                                    return img.get('#text')
0211	            
0212	            return None
0213	            
0214	        except Exception as e:
0215	            self.logger.error(f"Error getting artist image from albums: {e}")
0216	            return None
0217	
0218	    def get_track_info(self, artist, title, album=''):
0219	        """Get track information from LastFM API"""
0220	        if not self.api_key or not artist or not title:
0221	            self.logger.warning(f"Missing API key or track info: {artist} - {title}")
0222	            return None
0223	            
0224	        # Check cache first
0225	        cache_key = f"{artist}_{title}".lower().replace(' ', '_')
0226	        cache_file = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, f"track_{hashlib.md5(cache_key.encode()).hexdigest()}.json")
0227	        if os.path.exists(cache_file):
0228	            try:
0229	                with open(cache_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
0230	                    cached_data = json.load(f)
0231	                    self.logger.info(f"Using cached LastFM data for: {artist} - {title}")
0232	                    return cached_data
0233	            except Exception as e:
0234	                self.logger.error(f"Error reading cache file: {e}")
0235	        
0236	        # Make API request
0237	        try:
0238	            params = {
0239	                'method': 'track.getInfo',
0240	                'artist': artist,
0241	                'track': title,
0242	                'api_key': self.api_key,
0243	                'format': 'json'
0244	            }
0245	            
0246	            # Add album if we have it
0247	            if album:
0248	                params['album'] = album
0249	                
0250	            self.logger.info(f"Making LastFM API request for track: {artist} - {title}")
0251	            
0252	            response = requests.get(self.base_url, params=params, timeout=10)
0253	            
0254	            if response.status_code == 200:
0255	                data = response.json()
0256	                
0257	                # Check if we have track data
0258	                if 'track' not in data:
0259	                    self.logger.warning(f"No track data found for {artist} - {title}")
0260	                    if 'error' in data:
0261	                        self.logger.error(f"LastFM API error: {data.get('message', 'Unknown error')}")
0262	                    return None
0263	                    
0264	                # Extract relevant info
0265	                track_data = data['track']
0266	                result = {
0267	                    'title': track_data.get('name', title),
0268	                    'artist': track_data.get('artist', {}).get('name', artist),
0269	                    'album': track_data.get('album', {}).get('title', album),
0270	                    'source': 'lastfm'
0271	                }
0272	                
0273	                # Extract images if available
0274	                if 'album' in track_data and 'image' in track_data['album']:
0275	                    images = track_data['album']['image']
0276	                    # Get the largest image (last in the list)
0277	                    for img in reversed(images):
0278	                        if img.get('#text'):
0279	                            result['image'] = img.get('#text')
0280	                            break
0281	                
0282	                # Extract genre if available
0283	                if 'toptags' in track_data and 'tag' in track_data['toptags']:
0284	                    tags = track_data['toptags']['tag']
0285	                    if tags and len(tags) > 0:
0286	                        result['genre'] = tags[0]['name']
0287	                
0288	                # Save to cache
0289	                try:
0290	                    with open(cache_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
0291	                        json.dump(result, f, indent=2)
0292	                except Exception as cache_err:
0293	                    self.logger.error(f"Error writing cache: {cache_err}")
0294	                
0295	                return result
0296	                
0297	            else:
0298	                self.logger.error(f"LastFM request failed with status {response.status_code}: {response.text}")
0299	                return None
0300	                
0301	        except Exception as e:
0302	            self.logger.error(f"Error getting track info: {e}")
0303	            return None


--- File: logging_config.py ---
0001	# pump/logging_config.py
0002	import os
0003	import sys
0004	import logging
0005	from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler
0006	from pathlib import Path
0007	
0008	# Define log levels
0009	LOG_LEVELS = {
0010	    'debug': logging.DEBUG,
0011	    'info': logging.INFO,
0012	    'warning': logging.WARNING,
0013	    'error': logging.ERROR,
0014	    'critical': logging.CRITICAL
0015	}
0016	
0017	def configure_logging(level='info', log_to_file=True, log_dir='logs', max_size_mb=10, backup_count=5):
0018	    """
0019	    Configure the logging system
0020	    
0021	    Args:
0022	        level (str): Log level - 'debug', 'info', 'warning', 'error', 'critical'
0023	        log_to_file (bool): Whether to log to a file
0024	        log_dir (str): Directory for log files
0025	        max_size_mb (int): Maximum size of log file in MB before rotation
0026	        backup_count (int): Number of backup log files to keep
0027	    """
0028	    # Convert string level to logging level
0029	    log_level = LOG_LEVELS.get(level.lower(), logging.INFO)
0030	    
0031	    # Create root logger and set level
0032	    root_logger = logging.getLogger()
0033	    root_logger.setLevel(log_level)
0034	    
0035	    # Clear any existing handlers
0036	    for handler in root_logger.handlers[:]:
0037	        root_logger.removeHandler(handler)
0038	    
0039	    # Create formatter
0040	    formatter = logging.Formatter('%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
0041	    
0042	    # Console handler
0043	    console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
0044	    console_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
0045	    root_logger.addHandler(console_handler)
0046	    
0047	    # File handler
0048	    if log_to_file:
0049	        # Create logs directory if it doesn't exist
0050	        log_path = Path(log_dir)
0051	        log_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
0052	        
0053	        # Set up rotating file handler
0054	        file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
0055	            log_path / 'pump.log',
0056	            maxBytes=max_size_mb * 1024 * 1024,
0057	            backupCount=backup_count
0058	        )
0059	        file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
0060	        root_logger.addHandler(file_handler)
0061	    
0062	    # Log configuration information
0063	    logger = logging.getLogger('logging_config')
0064	    logger.info(f"Logging configured with level: {level}")
0065	    if log_to_file:
0066	        logger.info(f"Logging to file: {os.path.abspath(log_path / 'pump.log')}")
0067	        logger.info(f"Log rotation settings: max_size={max_size_mb}MB, backup_count={backup_count}")
0068	    
0069	    return root_logger
0070	
0071	def get_logger(name):
0072	    """Get a logger with the given name"""
0073	    return logging.getLogger(name)
0074	
0075	def set_log_level(level):
0076	    """Set the log level for all handlers"""
0077	    if level not in LOG_LEVELS:
0078	        raise ValueError(f"Invalid log level: {level}. Valid levels are: {list(LOG_LEVELS.keys())}")
0079	    
0080	    root_logger = logging.getLogger()
0081	    root_logger.setLevel(LOG_LEVELS[level])
0082	    
0083	    for handler in root_logger.handlers:
0084	        handler.setLevel(LOG_LEVELS[level])
0085	    
0086	    logger = logging.getLogger('logging_config')
0087	    logger.info(f"Log level changed to: {level}")
0088	    return True

0113	    def get_metadata_from_file(self, file_path):
0114	        """Extract metadata from audio file tags"""
0115	        try:
0116	            audio = mutagen.File(file_path)
0117	            if not audio:
0118	                return {}
0119	            
0120	            # Different file types have different tag structures
0121	            metadata = {}
0122	            
0123	            # Handle MP3 files (ID3)
0124	            if isinstance(audio, mutagen.mp3.MP3):
0125	                if 'TIT2' in audio:
0126	                    metadata['title'] = str(audio['TIT2'])
0127	                if 'TPE1' in audio:
0128	                    metadata['artist'] = str(audio['TPE1'])
0129	                if 'TALB' in audio:
0130	                    metadata['album'] = str(audio['TALB'])
0131	                # Extract album art if present
0132	                if 'APIC:' in audio:
0133	                    apic = audio['APIC:']
0134	                    metadata['embedded_album_art'] = apic.data
0135	            
0136	            # Handle FLAC files
0137	            elif isinstance(audio, mutagen.flac.FLAC):
0138	                if 'title' in audio:
0139	                    metadata['title'] = audio['title'][0]
0140	                if 'artist' in audio:
0141	                    metadata['artist'] = audio['artist'][0]
0142	                if 'album' in audio:
0143	                    metadata['album'] = audio['album'][0]
0144	                # Extract album art if present
0145	                if audio.pictures:
0146	                    metadata['embedded_album_art'] = audio.pictures[0].data
0147	            
0148	            # Handle Ogg Vorbis
0149	            elif isinstance(audio, mutagen.oggvorbis.OggVorbis):
0150	                if 'title' in audio:
0151	                    metadata['title'] = audio['title'][0]
0152	                if 'artist' in audio:
0153	                    metadata['artist'] = audio['artist'][0]
0154	                if 'album' in audio:
0155	                    metadata['album'] = audio['album'][0]
0156	            
0157	            # If we couldn't extract proper metadata, try to get from filename
0158	            if 'title' not in metadata or not metadata['title']:
0159	                filename = os.path.basename(file_path)
0160	                name, _ = os.path.splitext(filename)
0161	                
0162	                # Try to extract artist - title pattern (common in downloaded files)
0163	                match = re.match(r'(.+)\s*[-â€“_]\s*(.+)', name)
0164	                if match:
0165	                    if 'artist' not in metadata or not metadata['artist']:
0166	                        metadata['artist'] = match.group(1).strip()
0167	                    if 'title' not in metadata or not metadata['title']:
0168	                        metadata['title'] = match.group(2).strip()
0169	                else:
0170	                    metadata['title'] = name
0171	            
0172	            metadata['metadata_source'] = 'local_file'
0173	            return metadata
0174	            
0175	        except Exception as e:
0176	            logger.error(f"Error extracting metadata from {file_path}: {e}")
0177	            return {}
0178	    
0179	    def search_last_fm(self, title=None, artist=None, album=None):
0180	        """Search Last.fm for track metadata"""
0181	        if not self.lastfm_network:
0182	            logger.warning("Last.fm API not configured")
0183	            return {}
0184	        
0185	        try:
0186	            result = {}
0187	            
0188	            # If we have both artist and title
0189	            if artist and title:
0190	                try:
0191	                    track = self.lastfm_network.get_track(artist, title)
0192	                    result['title'] = track.get_title()
0193	                    result['artist'] = track.get_artist().get_name()
0194	                    
0195	                    # Try to get album
0196	                    try:
0197	                        album_obj = track.get_album()
0198	                        if album_obj:
0199	                            result['album'] = album_obj.get_title()
0200	                            
0201	                            # Get album cover - more explicit approach
0202	                            album_info = self.lastfm_network.get_album(track.get_artist().get_name(), album_obj.get_title())
0203	                            images = album_info.get_cover_image(size=3)  # Get medium-sized image
0204	                            if images:
0205	                                result['album_art_url'] = images
0206	                                logger.info(f"Got album art URL from Last.fm: {images}")
0207	                    except Exception as album_error:
0208	                        logger.error(f"Error getting album info: {album_error}")
0209	                    
0210	                    result['metadata_source'] = 'last.fm'
0211	                    return result
0212	                except Exception as e:
0213	                    logger.error(f"Last.fm track search error: {e}")
0214	                    return {}
0215	            
0216	            # If only title is available, try a track search
0217	            elif title:
0218	                tracks = self.lastfm_network.search_for_track("", title).get_next_page()
0219	                if tracks and len(tracks) > 0:
0220	                    track = tracks[0]
0221	                    result['title'] = track.get_title()
0222	                    result['artist'] = track.get_artist().get_name()
0223	                    result['metadata_source'] = 'last.fm'
0224	            
0225	            return result
0226	            
0227	        except Exception as e:
0228	            logger.error(f"Error searching Last.fm: {e}")
0229	            return {}
0230	    
0231	    def search_musicbrainz(self, title=None, artist=None, album=None):
0232	        """Search MusicBrainz for track metadata"""
0233	        try:
0234	            result = {}
0235	            
0236	            # If we have both artist and title
0237	            if artist and title:
0238	                # Search for recordings (tracks)
0239	                search_term = f'recording:"{title}" AND artist:"{artist}"'
0240	                search_results = musicbrainzngs.search_recordings(query=search_term, limit=1)
0241	                
0242	                if search_results['recording-list']:
0243	                    recording = search_results['recording-list'][0]
0244	                    result['title'] = recording['title']
0245	                    result['artist'] = recording['artist-credit'][0]['artist']['name']
0246	                    
0247	                    # Check if recording has a release (album)
0248	                    if 'release-list' in recording and recording['release-list']:
0249	                        release = recording['release-list'][0]
0250	                        result['album'] = release['title']
0251	                        
0252	                        # Try to get cover art
0253	                        try:
0254	                            cover_art = musicbrainzngs.get_image_list(release['id'])
0255	                            if 'images' in cover_art and cover_art['images']:
0256	                                result['album_art_url'] = cover_art['images'][0]['thumbnails']['large']
0257	                        except:
0258	                            pass
0259	                    
0260	                    result['metadata_source'] = 'musicbrainz'
0261	            
0262	            # If only title is available
0263	            elif title:
0264	                search_term = f'recording:"{title}"'
0265	                search_results = musicbrainzngs.search_recordings(query=search_term, limit=1)
0266	                
0267	                if search_results['recording-list']:
0268	                    recording = search_results['recording-list'][0]
0269	                    result['title'] = recording['title']
0270	                    result['artist'] = recording['artist-credit'][0]['artist']['name']
0271	                    result['metadata_source'] = 'musicbrainz'
0272	            
0273	            return result
0274	            
0275	        except Exception as e:
0276	            logger.error(f"Error searching MusicBrainz: {e}")
0277	            return {}

0279	    def enrich_metadata(self, basic_metadata, cache_dir=None):
0280	        """Try to enrich basic metadata with additional information from APIs"""
0281	        title = basic_metadata.get('title')
0282	        artist = basic_metadata.get('artist')
0283	        album = basic_metadata.get('album')
0284	        
0285	        logger.info(f"Attempting to enrich metadata for '{artist} - {title}'")
0286	        
0287	        # First try Last.fm
0288	        logger.info(f"Searching Last.fm for '{artist} - {title}'")
0289	        enhanced = self.search_last_fm(title, artist, album)
0290	        if enhanced and ('title' in enhanced and 'artist' in enhanced):
0291	            logger.info(f"Found enhanced metadata from Last.fm for '{artist} - {title}'")
0292	            
0293	            # If we have a cache directory and album art URL, download it
0294	            if cache_dir and 'album_art_url' in enhanced and enhanced['album_art_url']:
0295	                cached_path = self.download_and_cache_image(
0296	                    enhanced['album_art_url'], 
0297	                    cache_dir, 
0298	                    prefix='album_'
0299	                )
0300	                if cached_path:
0301	                    # Update to use local path instead of URL
0302	                    enhanced['album_art_url'] = cached_path
0303	                    logger.info(f"Cached album art to {cached_path}")
0304	            
0305	            return enhanced
0306	        
0307	        # If Last.fm doesn't have it, try MusicBrainz
0308	        logger.info(f"Searching MusicBrainz for '{artist} - {title}'")
0309	        enhanced = self.search_musicbrainz(title, artist, album)
0310	        if enhanced and ('title' in enhanced and 'artist' in enhanced):
0311	            logger.info(f"Found enhanced metadata from MusicBrainz for '{artist} - {title}'")
0312	            
0313	            # If we have a cache directory and album art URL, download it
0314	            if cache_dir and 'album_art_url' in enhanced and enhanced['album_art_url']:
0315	                cached_path = self.download_and_cache_image(
0316	                    enhanced['album_art_url'], 
0317	                    cache_dir, 
0318	                    prefix='album_'
0319	                )
0320	                if cached_path:
0321	                    # Update to use local path instead of URL
0322	                    enhanced['album_art_url'] = cached_path
0323	                    logger.info(f"Cached album art to {cached_path}")
0324	            
0325	            return enhanced
0326	        
0327	        # Return original if nothing found
0328	        logger.info(f"No enhanced metadata found for '{artist} - {title}'")
0329	        return basic_metadata

0331	    def download_and_cache_image(self, image_url, cache_dir, prefix='album_'):
0332	        """Download an image and save it to the cache directory"""
0333	        if not image_url:
0334	            logger.warning("No image URL provided for download")
0335	            return None
0336	            
0337	        try:
0338	            # Create a hash of the URL for the filename
0339	            url_hash = hashlib.md5(image_url.encode()).hexdigest()
0340	            cache_filename = f"{prefix}{url_hash}.jpg"
0341	            cache_path = os.path.join(cache_dir, cache_filename)
0342	            
0343	            # If already cached, return the web-accessible path
0344	            if os.path.exists(cache_path):
0345	                logger.debug(f"Using cached image: {cache_path}")
0346	                return f"/cache/{cache_filename}"  # Return web-accessible path
0347	                
0348	            # Create cache directory if it doesn't exist
0349	            if not os.path.exists(cache_dir):
0350	                logger.info(f"Creating missing cache directory: {cache_dir}")
0351	                os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
0352	                
0353	            # Only download if it's a URL
0354	            if image_url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
0355	                # Otherwise download and save it
0356	                logger.info(f"Downloading image from {image_url}")
0357	                response = requests.get(image_url, timeout=10)
0358	                if response.status_code == 200:
0359	                    with open(cache_path, 'wb') as f:
0360	                        f.write(response.content)
0361	                    logger.info(f"Successfully saved image to {cache_path}")
0362	                    return f"/cache/{cache_filename}"  # Return web-accessible path
0363	                else:
0364	                    logger.error(f"Failed to download image. Status code: {response.status_code}")
0365	                    return image_url  # Fall back to original URL
0366	            elif os.path.exists(image_url) and os.path.isfile(image_url):
0367	                # If it's a local file path, copy it to cache
0368	                try:
0369	                    shutil.copy(image_url, cache_path)
0370	                    return f"/cache/{cache_filename}"  # Return web-accessible path
0371	                except Exception as copy_error:
0372	                    logger.error(f"Error copying local file to cache: {copy_error}")
0373	                    return image_url
0374	            else:
0375	                logger.error(f"Image URL is neither a valid URL nor a file path: {image_url}")
0376	                return None
0377	        except Exception as e:
0378	            logger.error(f"Error downloading/saving image from {image_url}: {e}")
0379	            return image_url  # Return original URL as fallback
0380	
0381	    def update_all_metadata(self, status_tracker=None, skip_existing=False):
0382	        """Update metadata for all tracks in the database"""
0383	        try:
0384	            # Configure PostgreSQL connection directly without relying on db_path
0385	            conn = get_connection()
0386	            cursor = conn.cursor()
0387	            
0388	            processed = 0
0389	            updated = 0
0390	            
0391	            # Check if metadata_source column exists
0392	            try:
0393	                cursor.execute("""
0394	                    SELECT EXISTS (
0395	                        SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns 
0396	                        WHERE table_name = 'tracks' AND column_name = 'metadata_source'
0397	                    )
0398	                """)
0399	                has_metadata_source = cursor.fetchone()[0]
0400	                
0401	                if not has_metadata_source:
0402	                    logger.warning("metadata_source column doesn't exist, adding it now")
0403	                    cursor.execute("""
0404	                        ALTER TABLE tracks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS metadata_source TEXT;
0405	                        ALTER TABLE tracks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS metadata_updated_at TIMESTAMP;
0406	                    """)
0407	                    conn.commit()
0408	            except Exception as e:
0409	                logger.error(f"Error checking/creating metadata columns: {e}")
0410	                # Continue with the function even if this fails
0411	            
0412	            # Get total number of tracks
0413	            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tracks")
0414	            total_tracks = cursor.fetchone()[0]
0415	            
0416	            # Update status tracker if provided
0417	            if status_tracker:
0418	                status_tracker['running'] = True
0419	                status_tracker['start_time'] = datetime.now()
0420	                status_tracker['total_tracks'] = total_tracks
0421	                status_tracker['processed_tracks'] = 0
0422	                status_tracker['updated_tracks'] = 0
0423	                status_tracker['percent_complete'] = 0
0424	                status_tracker['error'] = None
0425	                
0426	            # Get tracks to process
0427	            if skip_existing:
0428	                logger.info("Metadata update: Skipping tracks with existing metadata")
0429	                if has_metadata_source:
0430	                    cursor.execute("""
0431	                        SELECT id, file_path, title, artist, album 
0432	                        FROM tracks 
0433	                        WHERE metadata_source IS NULL OR metadata_source = ''
0434	                    """)
0435	                else:
0436	                    cursor.execute("SELECT id, file_path, title, artist, album FROM tracks")
0437	            else:
0438	                logger.info("Metadata update: Processing all tracks")
0439	                cursor.execute("SELECT id, file_path, title, artist, album FROM tracks")
0440	                
0441	            tracks = cursor.fetchall()
0442	            total_tracks = len(tracks)
0443	            
0444	            # Release this connection as we'll use a new one per batch
0445	            release_connection(conn)
0446	            
0447	            # Process tracks in batches
0448	            batch_size = 50
0449	            for i in range(0, len(tracks), batch_size):
0450	                batch = tracks[i:i+batch_size]
0451	                
0452	                for track in batch:
0453	                    track_id, file_path, title, artist, album = track
0454	                    
0455	                    try:
0456	                        # Update status
0457	                        if status_tracker:
0458	                            status_tracker['current_track'] = f"{artist} - {title}"
0459	                            status_tracker['processed_tracks'] = processed
0460	                            status_tracker['updated_tracks'] = updated
0461	                            status_tracker['percent_complete'] = min(100, int((processed / total_tracks) * 100))
0462	                        
0463	                        # Get metadata from external service
0464	                        metadata = self.get_track_metadata(artist, title, album)
0465	                        
0466	                        if metadata:
0467	                            # Update track metadata
0468	                            conn = get_connection()
0469	                            cursor = conn.cursor()
0470	                            
0471	                            # Prepare update values
0472	                            update_fields = []
0473	                            update_values = []
0474	                            
0475	                            if 'genre' in metadata and metadata['genre']:
0476	                                update_fields.append("genre = %s")
0477	                                update_values.append(metadata['genre'])
0478	                                
0479	                            if 'album_art_url' in metadata and metadata['album_art_url']:
0480	                                update_fields.append("album_art_url = %s")
0481	                                update_values.append(metadata['album_art_url'])
0482	                                
0483	                            if 'metadata_source' in metadata and metadata['metadata_source']:
0484	                                update_fields.append("metadata_source = %s")
0485	                                update_values.append(metadata['metadata_source'])
0486	                                
0487	                            if update_fields:
0488	                                # Add the track_id to values
0489	                                update_values.append(track_id)
0490	                                
0491	                                # Build and execute update query
0492	                                update_query = f"""
0493	                                    UPDATE tracks 
0494	                                    SET {', '.join(update_fields)},
0495	                                        metadata_updated_at = NOW()
0496	                                    WHERE id = %s
0497	                                """
0498	                                
0499	                                cursor.execute(update_query, update_values)
0500	                                conn.commit()
0501	                                updated += 1
0502	                            
0503	                            release_connection(conn)
0504	                        
0505	                    except Exception as e:
0506	                        logger.error(f"Error updating metadata for {artist} - {title}: {e}")
0507	                        
0508	                    processed += 1
0509	                    
0510	                    # Periodically update status
0511	                    if processed % 10 == 0 and status_tracker:
0512	                        logger.info(f"Metadata update progress: {processed}/{total_tracks} tracks processed")
0513	            
0514	            # Final status update
0515	            if status_tracker:
0516	                status_tracker['processed_tracks'] = processed
0517	                status_tracker['updated_tracks'] = updated
0518	                status_tracker['percent_complete'] = 100
0519	                status_tracker['running'] = False
0520	                status_tracker['last_updated'] = datetime.now()
0521	                
0522	            logger.info(f"Metadata update complete: {processed}/{total_tracks} tracks processed, {updated} updated")
0523	            
0524	            return {
0525	                'processed': processed,
0526	                'updated': updated,
0527	                'total': total_tracks
0528	            }
0529	        except Exception as e:
0530	            logger.error(f"Error during metadata update: {e}")
0531	            if status_tracker:
0532	                status_tracker['running'] = False
0533	                status_tracker['error'] = str(e)
0534	                status_tracker['last_updated'] = datetime.now()
0535	            return {
0536	                'processed': 0,
0537	                'updated': 0,
0538	                'total': 0,
0539	                'error': str(e)
0540	            }
0541	        finally:
0542	            if 'conn' in locals() and conn:
0543	                release_connection(conn)
0544	
0545	    def _update_track_metadata_with_retry(self, track_id, artist, title, album, metadata):
0546	        """Update a track's metadata with retry logic for database locks"""
0547	        from db_operations import with_transaction
0548	        
0549	        def do_update(conn, track_id, artist, title, album, metadata):
0550	            cursor = conn.cursor()
0551	            
0552	            # Start with basic fields to update
0553	            update_fields = []
0554	            update_values = []
0555	            
0556	            # Add metadata fields
0557	            if 'album_art_url' in metadata and metadata['album_art_url']:
0558	                update_fields.append('album_art_url = ?')
0559	                update_values.append(metadata['album_art_url'])
0560	                
0561	            if 'artist_image_url' in metadata and metadata['artist_image_url']:
0562	                update_fields.append('artist_image_url = ?')
0563	                update_values.append(metadata['artist_image_url'])
0564	                
0565	            if 'genre' in metadata and metadata['genre']:
0566	                update_fields.append('genre = ?')
0567	                update_values.append(metadata['genre'])
0568	                
0569	            # Only update if we have fields to update
0570	            if update_fields:
0571	                cursor.execute(
0572	                    f"UPDATE audio_files SET {', '.join(update_fields)} WHERE id = ?",
0573	                    update_values + [track_id]
0574	                )
0575	                
0576	            return cursor.rowcount > 0
0577	        
0578	        with optimized_connection(self.db_path, self.in_memory, self.cache_size_mb) as conn:
0579	            return with_transaction(conn, do_update, track_id, artist, title, album, metadata)
0580	
0581	    def _update_track_metadata(self, track_id, artist, title, album, metadata):
0582	        """Update a track's metadata in the database"""
0583	        try:
0584	            # Mark the connection as modified so it gets saved to disk
0585	            from flask import g
0586	            if hasattr(g, 'db_modified'):
0587	                g.db_modified = True
0588	                
0589	            # Create direct DB connection instead of using optimized_connection
0590	            # which creates circular import problems
0591	            import sqlite3
0592	            db_path = self.config_file.replace('pump.conf', 'pump.db')
0593	            
0594	            with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:
0595	                cursor = conn.cursor()
0596	                
0597	                # Start with basic fields to update
0598	                update_fields = []
0599	                update_values = []
0600	                
0601	                # Add metadata fields that need updating
0602	                if 'album_art_url' in metadata and metadata['album_art_url']:
0603	                    update_fields.append('album_art_url = ?')
0604	                    update_values.append(metadata['album_art_url'])
0605	                    
0606	                if 'artist_image_url' in metadata and metadata['artist_image_url']:
0607	                    update_fields.append('artist_image_url = ?')
0608	                    update_values.append(metadata['artist_image_url'])
0609	                    
0610	                if 'genre' in metadata and metadata['genre']:
0611	                    update_fields.append('genre = ?')
0612	                    update_values.append(metadata['genre'])
0613	                    
0614	                # Only update if we have fields to update
0615	                if update_fields:
0616	                    query = f"UPDATE audio_files SET {', '.join(update_fields)} WHERE id = ?"
0617	                    cursor.execute(query, update_values + [track_id])
0618	                    conn.commit()  # Add explicit commit
0619	                    
0620	                    # Log the update for debugging
0621	                    if cursor.rowcount > 0:
0622	                        logger.info(f"Updated metadata for track {track_id}: {', '.join(update_fields)}")
0623	                    else:
0624	                        logger.warning(f"No rows updated for track {track_id}")
0625	                    
0626	                return cursor.rowcount > 0
0627	        except Exception as e:
0628	            logger.error(f"Error updating track metadata: {e}")
0629	            return False

0631	    def get_track_metadata(self, artist, title, album=''):
0632	        """Get metadata for a track from external services"""
0633	        metadata = {}
0634	        
0635	        # Only use LastFM for metadata
0636	        if self.lastfm_service:
0637	            try:
0638	                lastfm_data = self.lastfm_service.get_track_info(artist, title)
0639	                if lastfm_data:
0640	                    # Extract relevant metadata
0641	                    if 'album' in lastfm_data:
0642	                        metadata['album'] = lastfm_data['album']
0643	                    if 'image' in lastfm_data:
0644	                        metadata['album_art_url'] = lastfm_data['image']
0645	                    if 'genre' in lastfm_data:
0646	                        metadata['genre'] = lastfm_data['genre']
0647	                    if 'year' in lastfm_data:
0648	                        metadata['year'] = lastfm_data['year']
0649	                    metadata['source'] = 'lastfm'
0650	                    logger.info(f"Retrieved metadata for {artist} - {title} from LastFM")
0651	            except Exception as e:
0652	                logger.error(f"Error getting LastFM metadata for {artist} - {title}: {e}")
0653	        else:
0654	            logger.debug(f"LastFM service not available for {artist} - {title}")
0655	        
0656	        return metadata

0208	    def analyze_file(self, file_path: str, save_to_db: bool = True) -> Dict:
0209	        """
0210	        Analyze a music file and extract its features, but skip if it's already in the database.
0211	        """
0212	        conn = None
0213	        try:
0214	            conn = get_connection()
0215	            cursor = conn.cursor()
0216	            
0217	            # Check if file has already been analyzed
0218	            cursor.execute("SELECT id FROM tracks WHERE file_path = %s", (file_path,))
0219	            row = cursor.fetchone()
0220	            
0221	            if row is not None:
0222	                # Already in database, skip analysis
0223	                track_id = row[0]
0224	                logger.info(f"Track already in database (id={track_id}): {file_path}")
0225	                return {"id": track_id, "file_path": file_path, "status": "already_exists"}
0226	            
0227	            # If not found in DB, proceed with analysis
0228	            # First, get metadata from the file itself
0229	            metadata = self.metadata_service.get_metadata_from_file(file_path)
0230	
0231	            # Next, try to enhance metadata from online services
0232	            enhanced_metadata = self.metadata_service.enrich_metadata(metadata)
0233	
0234	            # Load the audio file for analysis
0235	            y, sr = librosa.load(file_path, sr=None)
0236	
0237	            # Basic audio properties
0238	            duration = librosa.get_duration(y=y, sr=sr)
0239	
0240	            # Extract features
0241	            features = {
0242	                "file_path": file_path,
0243	                "duration": duration,
0244	                "title": enhanced_metadata.get("title", ""),
0245	                "artist": enhanced_metadata.get("artist", ""),
0246	                "album": enhanced_metadata.get("album", ""),
0247	                "album_art_url": enhanced_metadata.get("album_art_url", ""),
0248	                "metadata_source": enhanced_metadata.get("metadata_source", "unknown"),
0249	                **self._extract_time_domain_features(y, sr),
0250	                **self._extract_frequency_domain_features(y, sr),
0251	                **self._extract_rhythm_features(y, sr),
0252	                **self._extract_harmonic_features(y, sr)
0253	            }
0254	
0255	            # Fetch artist image if available (LastFM > Spotify fallback)
0256	            artist_image_url = None
0257	            if features["artist"]:
0258	                if self.lastfm_service:
0259	                    artist_image_url = self.lastfm_service.get_artist_image_url(features["artist"])
0260	                if not artist_image_url and self.spotify_service:
0261	                    artist_image_url = self.spotify_service.get_artist_image_url(features["artist"])
0262	
0263	            features["artist_image_url"] = artist_image_url
0264	
0265	            if save_to_db:
0266	                self._save_to_db(features)
0267	
0268	            return features
0269	
0270	        except Exception as e:
0271	            logger.error(f"Error analyzing file {file_path}: {e}")
0272	            return {"error": str(e), "file_path": file_path, "status": "error"}
0273	        finally:
0274	            if conn:
0275	                release_connection(conn)
