--- File: spotify_service.py ---
0001	import requests
0002	import base64
0003	import json
0004	import os
0005	import time
0006	import logging
0007	import hashlib
0008	
0009	class SpotifyService:
0010	    def __init__(self, client_id=None, client_secret=None, cache_dir='spotify_cache'):
0011	        self.client_id = client_id
0012	        self.client_secret = client_secret
0013	        self.token = None
0014	        self.token_expiry = 0
0015	        self.cache_dir = cache_dir
0016	        
0017	        if not os.path.exists(cache_dir):
0018	            os.makedirs(cache_dir)
0019	            
0020	        self.logger = logging.getLogger('spotify_service')
0021	    
0022	    def get_token(self):
0023	        """Get or refresh Spotify API token"""
0024	        current_time = time.time()
0025	        
0026	        # Return existing token if it's still valid
0027	        if self.token and current_time < self.token_expiry - 60:
0028	            return self.token
0029	            
0030	        if not self.client_id or not self.client_secret:
0031	            self.logger.error("Missing Spotify client credentials")
0032	            return None
0033	            
0034	        try:
0035	            auth_header = base64.b64encode(f"{self.client_id}:{self.client_secret}".encode()).decode()
0036	            
0037	            headers = {
0038	                'Authorization': f'Basic {auth_header}',
0039	                'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'
0040	            }
0041	            
0042	            data = {'grant_type': 'client_credentials'}
0043	            
0044	            response = requests.post('https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token', 
0045	                                    headers=headers, 
0046	                                    data=data)
0047	            
0048	            if response.status_code == 200:
0049	                token_data = response.json()
0050	                self.token = token_data.get('access_token')
0051	                expires_in = token_data.get('expires_in', 3600)
0052	                self.token_expiry = current_time + expires_in
0053	                return self.token
0054	            else:
0055	                self.logger.error(f"Failed to get Spotify token: {response.text}")
0056	                return None
0057	                
0058	        except Exception as e:
0059	            self.logger.error(f"Exception getting Spotify token: {e}")
0060	            return None
0061	    
0062	    def search_artist(self, artist_name):
0063	        """Search for an artist on Spotify"""
0064	        token = self.get_token()
0065	        if not token:
0066	            return None
0067	        
0068	        # Check cache first
0069	        cache_file = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, f"artist_{hashlib.md5(artist_name.encode()).hexdigest()}.json")
0070	        if os.path.exists(cache_file):
0071	            # Use cache if less than 30 days old
0072	            if time.time() - os.path.getmtime(cache_file) < 30 * 24 * 60 * 60:
0073	                try:
0074	                    with open(cache_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
0075	                        return json.load(f)
0076	                except Exception as e:
0077	                    self.logger.error(f"Error reading artist cache: {e}")
0078	        
0079	        # Clean artist name - remove potentially problematic characters or split on common separators
0080	        clean_artist = artist_name
0081	        for separator in ["feat.", "ft.", "featuring", "with", "vs", "x", "&"]:
0082	            if separator in clean_artist.lower():
0083	                clean_artist = clean_artist.split(separator, 1)[0].strip()
0084	        
0085	        try:
0086	            headers = {'Authorization': f'Bearer {token}'}
0087	            params = {
0088	                'q': clean_artist,
0089	                'type': 'artist',
0090	                'limit': 1
0091	            }
0092	            
0093	            self.logger.info(f"Searching Spotify for artist: {clean_artist}")
0094	            response = requests.get('https://api.spotify.com/v1/search',
0095	                                   headers=headers,
0096	                                   params=params)
0097	            
0098	            if response.status_code == 200:
0099	                data = response.json()
0100	                artists = data.get('artists', {}).get('items', [])
0101	                
0102	                if artists:
0103	                    artist = artists[0]
0104	                    # Save to cache
0105	                    try:
0106	                        with open(cache_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
0107	                            json.dump(artist, f)
0108	                    except Exception as e:
0109	                        self.logger.error(f"Error writing artist cache: {e}")
0110	                        
0111	                    return artist
0112	                    
0113	                self.logger.warning(f"No artist found on Spotify for: {clean_artist}")
0114	                return None
0115	            else:
0116	                self.logger.error(f"Error searching Spotify: {response.status_code} - {response.text}")
0117	                return None
0118	                
0119	        except Exception as e:
0120	            self.logger.error(f"Exception searching Spotify: {e}")
0121	            return None
0122	    
0123	    # Update get_artist_image_url to support caching
0124	    def get_artist_image_url(self, artist_name, cache_dir=None):
0125	        """Get artist image URL from Spotify or download to cache"""
0126	        artist = self.search_artist(artist_name)
0127	        if not artist:
0128	            return None
0129	            
0130	        # Get images array, sorted by size (largest first)
0131	        images = artist.get('images', [])
0132	        if images:
0133	            # Get the largest image URL
0134	            image_url = images[0].get('url')
0135	            self.logger.info(f"Found Spotify image for {artist_name}: {image_url}")
0136	            
0137	            # If we have a cache directory, download the image
0138	            if cache_dir and image_url:
0139	                try:
0140	                    # Create a hash of the URL for the filename
0141	                    url_hash = hashlib.md5(image_url.encode()).hexdigest()
0142	                    cache_path = os.path.join(cache_dir, f"artist_{url_hash}.jpg")
0143	                    
0144	                    # If already cached, return the path
0145	                    if os.path.exists(cache_path):
0146	                        self.logger.debug(f"Artist image already in cache: {cache_path}")
0147	                        return cache_path
0148	                    
0149	                    # Otherwise download and save it
0150	                    response = requests.get(image_url, timeout=10)
0151	                    if response.status_code == 200:
0152	                        # Save the image to cache
0153	                        with open(cache_path, 'wb') as f:
0154	                            f.write(response.content)
0155	                        self.logger.info(f"Saved artist image to {cache_path}")
0156	                        return cache_path
0157	                except Exception as e:
0158	                    self.logger.error(f"Error caching artist image: {e}")
0159	                    # Fall back to returning the URL if caching fails
0160	                    
0161	            return image_url
0162	        
0163	        self.logger.warning(f"No images found for artist {artist_name} on Spotify")    
0164	        return None


--- File: start_pump.sh ---
0001	#!/bin/bash
0002	
0003	ENV_NAME="pump_env"  # Name your environment
0004	LOG_DIR="logs"       # Directory for log files
0005	
0006	# Directory where the script is located
0007	SCRIPT_DIR="$( cd "$( dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" )" && pwd )"
0008	cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
0009	
0010	# Function to display colored messages
0011	print_message() {
0012	    local color=$1
0013	    local message=$2
0014	    case $color in
0015	        "green") echo -e "\e[32m$message\e[0m" ;;
0016	        "yellow") echo -e "\e[33m$message\e[0m" ;;
0017	        "red") echo -e "\e[31m$message\e[0m" ;;
0018	        *) echo "$message" ;;
0019	    esac
0020	}
0021	
0022	# Check if running with sudo (needed for apt installs)
0023	check_sudo() {
0024	    if [ "$EUID" -ne 0 ]; then
0025	        print_message "yellow" "Note: For system dependencies, you might be asked for your password."
0026	    fi
0027	}
0028	
0029	# Setup environment based on available tools (conda or venv)
0030	if command -v conda &> /dev/null; then
0031	    print_message "green" "Conda detected. Using conda environment..."
0032	    
0033	    # Initialize conda for shell if needed
0034	    conda_init_status=$(conda info --json | grep -c "not initialized")
0035	    if [ $conda_init_status -gt 0 ]; then
0036	        print_message "yellow" "Initializing conda for your shell..."
0037	        conda init "$(basename "$SHELL")"
0038	        print_message "yellow" "Please restart your terminal and run this script again."
0039	        exit 1
0040	    fi
0041	
0042	    # Check if our environment exists
0043	    if ! conda env list | grep -q "^${ENV_NAME}"; then
0044	        print_message "green" "Creating conda environment '${ENV_NAME}'..."
0045	        conda create -y -n $ENV_NAME python=3.9
0046	    fi
0047	
0048	    # Activate the conda environment

0149	# Function to check if module is available with better error handling
0150	check_module() {
0151	    module_name="$1"
0152	    
0153	    # Special handling for psycopg2
0154	    if [ "$module_name" = "psycopg2" ]; then
0155	        # First, try to import the module
0156	        python -c "import psycopg2; print(f'psycopg2 version: {psycopg2.__version__}')" 2>/dev/null
0157	        
0158	        if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
0159	            print_message "green" "✓ $module_name is available"
0160	            return 0
0161	        else
0162	            print_message "yellow" "⚠️ psycopg2 import failed. Trying alternatives..."
0163	            
0164	            # Detailed diagnostics for psycopg2
0165	            print_message "yellow" "--- psycopg2 diagnostics ---"
0166	            
0167	            # Check if binary version might work
0168	            python -c "import psycopg2.binary" 2>/dev/null
0169	            if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
0170	                print_message "green" "✓ psycopg2.binary module works"
0171	            else
0172	                print_message "red" "✗ psycopg2.binary also fails"
0173	            fi
0174	            
0175	            # Try with conda if available
0176	            if command -v conda &> /dev/null; then
0177	                print_message "yellow" "Trying conda install..."
0178	                conda install -y -c conda-forge psycopg2
0179	                
0180	                # Check again
0181	                python -c "import psycopg2" 2>/dev/null
0182	                if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
0183	                    print_message "green" "✓ psycopg2 was successfully installed via conda"
0184	                    return 0
0185	                fi
0186	            fi
0187	            
0188	            # Special handling for MacOS
0189	            if [[ "$OSTYPE" == "darwin"* ]]; then
0190	                print_message "yellow" "MacOS detected. Trying environment variable workarounds..."
0191	                
0192	                # Try setting environment variables that sometimes help on Mac
0193	                export LDFLAGS="-L/usr/local/opt/openssl/lib"
0194	                export CPPFLAGS="-I/usr/local/opt/openssl/include"
0195	                
0196	                pip install --no-cache-dir --no-binary :all: psycopg2-binary
0197	                
0198	                # Try one more time with the new installation
0199	                python -c "import psycopg2" 2>/dev/null
0200	                if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
0201	                    print_message "green" "✓ psycopg2 was successfully installed with special flags"
0202	                    return 0
0203	                else
0204	                    print_message "red" "✗ All psycopg2 installation attempts failed."
0205	                    print_message "yellow" "Please try manually: pip install --no-binary :all: psycopg2-binary"
0206	                    print_message "yellow" "If that fails, you may need more specific system dependencies."
0207	                    return 1
0208	                fi
0209	            fi
0210	            
0211	            print_message "red" "✗ Failed to install psycopg2"
0212	            return 1
0213	        fi
0214	    else
0215	        # Standard module checking
0216	        python -c "import $module_name" 2>/dev/null
0217	        
0218	        if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
0219	            print_message "green" "✓ $module_name is available"
0220	            return 0
0221	        else
0222	            print_message "yellow" "⚠️ $module_name is not available - installing..."
0223	            pip install $module_name
0224	            
0225	            python -c "import $module_name" 2>/dev/null
0226	            if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
0227	                print_message "green" "✓ $module_name is now available"
0228	                return 0
0229	            else
0230	                print_message "red" "✗ Failed to install $module_name"
0231	                return 1
0232	            fi
0233	        fi
0234	    fi
0235	}

0262	import os
0263	import sys
0264	import logging
0265	from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler
0266	from pathlib import Path
0267	
0268	# Define log levels
0269	LOG_LEVELS = {
0270	    'debug': logging.DEBUG,
0271	    'info': logging.INFO,
0272	    'warning': logging.WARNING,
0273	    'error': logging.ERROR,
0274	    'critical': logging.CRITICAL
0275	}
0276	
0277	def configure_logging(level='info', log_to_file=True, log_dir='logs', max_size_mb=10, backup_count=5):
0278	    \"""
0279	    Configure the logging system
0280	    
0281	    Args:
0282	        level (str): Log level - 'debug', 'info', 'warning', 'error', 'critical'
0283	        log_to_file (bool): Whether to log to a file
0284	        log_dir (str): Directory for log files
0285	        max_size_mb (int): Maximum size of log file in MB before rotation
0286	        backup_count (int): Number of backup log files to keep
0287	    \"""
0288	    # Convert string level to logging level
0289	    log_level = LOG_LEVELS.get(level.lower(), logging.INFO)
0290	    
0291	    # Create root logger and set level
0292	    root_logger = logging.getLogger()
0293	    root_logger.setLevel(log_level)
0294	    
0295	    # Clear any existing handlers
0296	    for handler in root_logger.handlers[:]:
0297	        root_logger.removeHandler(handler)
0298	    
0299	    # Create formatter
0300	    formatter = logging.Formatter('%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
0301	    
0302	    # Console handler
0303	    console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
0304	    console_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
0305	    root_logger.addHandler(console_handler)
0306	    
0307	    # File handler
0308	    if log_to_file:
0309	        # Create logs directory if it doesn't exist
0310	        log_path = Path(log_dir)
0311	        log_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
0312	        
0313	        # Set up rotating file handler
0314	        file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
0315	            log_path / 'pump.log',
0316	            maxBytes=max_size_mb * 1024 * 1024,
0317	            backupCount=backup_count
0318	        )
0319	        file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
0320	        root_logger.addHandler(file_handler)
0321	    
0322	    # Log configuration information
0323	    logger = logging.getLogger('logging_config')
0324	    logger.info(f"Logging configured with level: {level}")
0325	    if log_to_file:
0326	        logger.info(f"Logging to file: {os.path.abspath(log_path / 'pump.log')}")
0327	        logger.info(f"Log rotation settings: max_size={max_size_mb}MB, backup_count={backup_count}")
0328	    
0329	    return root_logger
0330	
0331	def get_logger(name):
0332	    \"""Get a logger with the given name\"""
0333	    return logging.getLogger(name)
0334	
0335	def set_log_level(level):
0336	    \"""Set the log level for all handlers\"""
0337	    if level not in LOG_LEVELS:
0338	        raise ValueError(f"Invalid log level: {level}. Valid levels are: {list(LOG_LEVELS.keys())}")
0339	    
0340	    root_logger = logging.getLogger()
0341	    root_logger.setLevel(LOG_LEVELS[level])
0342	    
0343	    logger = logging.getLogger('logging_config')
0344	    logger.info(f"Log level changed to: {level}")
0345	EOF
0346	fi

0369	# Function to install Docker on various systems
0370	install_docker() {
0371	    print_message "yellow" "Installing Docker..."
0372	    
0373	    # Check OS type
0374	    if command -v apt-get &> /dev/null; then
0375	        # Debian/Ubuntu
0376	        print_message "green" "Detected Debian/Ubuntu system"
0377	        sudo apt-get update
0378	        sudo apt-get install -y ca-certificates curl gnupg
0379	        
0380	        # Add Docker's official GPG key
0381	        sudo install -m 0755 -d /etc/apt/keyrings
0382	        curl -fsSL https://download.docker.com/linux/ubuntu/gpg | sudo gpg --dearmor -o /etc/apt/keyrings/docker.gpg
0383	        sudo chmod a+r /etc/apt/keyrings/docker.gpg
0384	        
0385	        # Add Docker repository
0386	        echo \
0387	        "deb [arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) signed-by=/etc/apt/keyrings/docker.gpg] https://download.docker.com/linux/ubuntu \
0388	        $(. /etc/os-release && echo "$VERSION_CODENAME") stable" | \
0389	        sudo tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/docker.list > /dev/null
0390	        
0391	        # Install Docker packages
0392	        sudo apt-get update
0393	        sudo apt-get install -y docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin
0394	        
0395	        # Add current user to docker group to avoid using sudo
0396	        sudo usermod -aG docker $USER
0397	        print_message "yellow" "User added to docker group. You might need to log out and back in for this to take effect."
0398	    
0399	    elif command -v dnf &> /dev/null; then
0400	        # Fedora/RHEL/CentOS
0401	        print_message "green" "Detected Fedora/RHEL/CentOS system"
0402	        sudo dnf -y install dnf-plugins-core
0403	        sudo dnf config-manager --add-repo https://download.docker.com/linux/fedora/docker-ce.repo
0404	        sudo dnf install -y docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin
0405	        
0406	        # Start and enable Docker service
0407	        sudo systemctl start docker
0408	        sudo systemctl enable docker
0409	        
0410	        # Add current user to docker group
0411	        sudo usermod -aG docker $USER
0412	        print_message "yellow" "User added to docker group. You might need to log out and back in for this to take effect."
0413	    
0414	    elif command -v yum &> /dev/null; then
0415	        # Older RHEL/CentOS
0416	        print_message "green" "Detected older RHEL/CentOS system"
0417	        sudo yum install -y yum-utils
0418	        sudo yum-config-manager --add-repo https://download.docker.com/linux/centos/docker-ce.repo
0419	        sudo yum install -y docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin
0420	        
0421	        # Start and enable Docker service
0422	        sudo systemctl start docker
0423	        sudo systemctl enable docker
0424	        
0425	        # Add current user to docker group
0426	        sudo usermod -aG docker $USER
0427	        print_message "yellow" "User added to docker group. You might need to log out and back in for this to take effect."
0428	    
0429	    elif [[ "$OSTYPE" == "darwin"* ]]; then
0430	        # MacOS
0431	        print_message "green" "Detected MacOS system"
0432	        if command -v brew &> /dev/null; then
0433	            brew install --cask docker
0434	            print_message "yellow" "Docker Desktop installed. Please start Docker Desktop from your Applications folder."
0435	        else
0436	            print_message "red" "Homebrew not found. Please install Docker Desktop manually from https://www.docker.com/products/docker-desktop"
0437	        fi
0438	    
0439	    else
0440	        print_message "red" "Unsupported operating system. Please install Docker manually from https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/"
0441	        return 1
0442	    fi
0443	    
0444	    return 0
0445	}
0446	
0447	# Function to install Docker Compose if needed
0448	install_docker_compose() {
0449	    print_message "yellow" "Installing Docker Compose..."
0450	    
0451	    # For systems that don't have the Docker Compose plugin
0452	    if ! command -v docker-compose &> /dev/null && ! docker compose version &> /dev/null; then
0453	        if [[ "$OSTYPE" == "darwin"* ]]; then
0454	            if command -v brew &> /dev/null; then
0455	                brew install docker-compose
0456	            else
0457	                print_message "red" "Homebrew not found. Please install Docker Compose manually."
0458	                return 1
0459	            fi
0460	        else
0461	            # Linux systems
0462	            COMPOSE_VERSION=$(curl -s https://api.github.com/repos/docker/compose/releases/latest | grep 'tag_name' | cut -d\" -f4)
0463	            sudo curl -L "https://github.com/docker/compose/releases/download/${COMPOSE_VERSION}/docker-compose-$(uname -s)-$(uname -m)" -o /usr/local/bin/docker-compose
0464	            sudo chmod +x /usr/local/bin/docker-compose
0465	            print_message "green" "Docker Compose installed successfully."
0466	        fi
0467	    else
0468	        print_message "green" "Docker Compose already installed."
0469	    fi
0470	    
0471	    return 0
0472	}
0473	
0474	# Check if Docker is installed, install if not
0475	print_message "green" "Checking for Docker installation..."
0476	if ! command -v docker &> /dev/null; then
0477	    print_message "yellow" "Docker not found. Installing Docker..."
0478	    install_docker
0479	    
0480	    # Check if Docker was successfully installed
0481	    if ! command -v docker &> /dev/null; then
0482	        print_message "red" "Failed to install Docker. Please install it manually."
0483	        print_message "yellow" "Visit https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/ for installation instructions."
0484	        exit 1
0485	    fi
0486	else
0487	    print_message "green" "Docker is already installed."
0488	fi
0489	
0490	# Check which docker compose command is available
0491	DOCKER_COMPOSE_CMD="docker compose"
0492	if ! command -v docker &> /dev/null || ! docker compose version &> /dev/null; then
0493	    if command -v docker-compose &> /dev/null; then
0494	        print_message "yellow" "Using docker-compose instead of docker compose"
0495	        DOCKER_COMPOSE_CMD="docker-compose"
0496	    else
0497	        print_message "yellow" "Docker Compose not found. Installing Docker Compose..."
0498	        install_docker_compose
0499	        
0500	        # Check again which variant is available after installation
0501	        if docker compose version &> /dev/null; then
0502	            DOCKER_COMPOSE_CMD="docker compose"
0503	        elif command -v docker-compose &> /dev/null; then
0504	            DOCKER_COMPOSE_CMD="docker-compose"
0505	        else
0506	            print_message "red" "Failed to install Docker Compose. Please install it manually."
0507	            exit 1
0508	        fi
0509	    fi
0510	fi
0511	
0512	# Start PostgreSQL with Docker Compose
0513	print_message "green" "Starting PostgreSQL database on port 45432..."
0514	$DOCKER_COMPOSE_CMD up -d
0515	
0516	# Wait a moment for PostgreSQL to fully initialize
0517	print_message "yellow" "Waiting for PostgreSQL to initialize..."
0518	sleep 3
0519	
0520	# Start the web player
0521	print_message "green" "Starting Pump Web Player..."
0522	python web_player.py
0523	
0524	# If the server exits, wait for user input before closing
0525	echo ""
0526	print_message "yellow" "Server stopped. Press any key to close this window..."
0527	read -n 1

0189	# Set a watchdog timer to force exit if Ctrl+C fails
0190	def setup_exit_watchdog():
0191	    def handler(signum, frame):
0192	        print("\nForce quitting (watchdog triggered)")
0193	    # Register the watchdog to run on SIGINT
0194	    def sigint_watchdog(signum, frame):
0195	        print("\nCtrl+C detected, shutting down...")
0196	        threading.Thread(target=watchdog_exit, daemon=True).start()
0197	        # Let the normal handlers run
0198	        clean_shutdown(signum, frame)
0199	        
0200	    # Override SIGINT handler
0201	    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, sigint_watchdog)

0235	# Add this function to check if analysis is already running
0236	def is_analysis_running():
0237	    """Check if an analysis is already running based on status"""
0238	    # First, check the status object
0239	    if ANALYSIS_STATUS['running']:
0240	        return True
0241	        
0242	    # Second, check if the lock file exists
0243	    lock_file = os.path.join(LOCK_FILE_DIR, '.analysis_lock')
0244	    if os.path.exists(lock_file):
0245	        try:
0246	            with open(lock_file, 'r') as f:
0247	                pid = int(f.read().strip())
0248	            
0249	            # Try to check if process exists
0250	            try:
0251	                os.kill(pid, 0)  # This will raise an exception if process doesn't exist
0252	                return True
0253	            except OSError:
0254	                # Process doesn't exist, remove stale lock
0255	                os.remove(lock_file)
0256	                return False
0257	        except:
0258	            # Invalid lock file, remove it
0259	            os.remove(lock_file)
0260	            return False
0261	            
0262	    return False

0264	def check_database_stats():
0265	    """Check database statistics for PostgreSQL"""
0266	    try:
0267	        # Get track count
0268	        result = execute_query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tracks")
0269	        track_count = result[0][0] if result else 0
0270	        
0271	        # Get size information using PostgreSQL system tables
0272	        result = execute_query("""
0273	            SELECT pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size(current_database())) 
0274	            FROM pg_database 
0275	            WHERE datname = current_database()
0276	        """)
0277	        db_size = result[0][0] if result else "Unknown"
0278	        
0279	        logger.info(f"Database stats: PostgreSQL, Size: {db_size}, Audio files: {track_count}")
0280	        return {
0281	            'db_size': db_size,
0282	            'track_count': track_count
0283	        }
0284	    except Exception as e:
0285	        logger.error(f"Error checking database stats: {e}")
0286	        return None

0375	# Add this function to coordinate database saves using your existing functions
0376	def throttled_save_to_disk(force=False):
0377	    """Throttled version of save_memory_db_to_disk with better error handling"""
0378	    global DB_SAVE_IN_PROGRESS, LAST_SAVE_TIME, main_thread_conn
0379	    
0380	    # Only proceed if we're using in-memory mode
0381	    if not DB_IN_MEMORY:
0382	        return False
0383	        
0384	    # Ensure we have a valid connection
0385	    if main_thread_conn is None:
0386	        logger.warning("Cannot save database: main_thread_conn is None")
0387	        return False
0388	        
0389	    # Verify connection is still valid
0390	    try:
0391	        main_thread_conn.execute("SELECT 1")
0392	    except sqlite3.Error:
0393	        logger.error("Cannot save database: main_thread_conn is closed or invalid")
0394	        # Try to recreate the connection
0395	        try:
0396	            from db_operations import get_optimized_connection
0397	            main_thread_conn = get_optimized_connection(
0398	                DB_PATH, in_memory=True, cache_size_mb=DB_CACHE_SIZE_MB, check_same_thread=False
0399	            )
0400	            logger.info("Main thread connection recreated successfully")
0401	        except Exception as conn_error:
0402	            logger.error(f"Failed to recreate main thread connection: {conn_error}")
0403	            return False
0404	    
0405	    # Check if a save is already in progress
0406	    if DB_SAVE_IN_PROGRESS:
0407	        logger.debug("Skipping save - another save is already in progress")
0408	        return False
0409	        
0410	    # Check if we've saved recently (unless forced)
0411	    current_time = time.time()
0412	    if not force and (current_time - LAST_SAVE_TIME) < MIN_SAVE_INTERVAL:
0413	        logger.debug("Skipping save - throttled (last save was less than 60 seconds ago)")
0414	        return False
0415	    
0416	    # Try to acquire lock without blocking
0417	    if not DB_SAVE_LOCK.acquire(blocking=False):
0418	        logger.debug("Skipping save - couldn't acquire lock")
0419	        return False
0420	        
0421	    try:
0422	        DB_SAVE_IN_PROGRESS = True
0423	        logger.info("Saving in-memory database to disk (throttled)...")
0424	        
0425	        # Use your existing function from db_operations
0426	        success = save_memory_db_to_disk(main_thread_conn, DB_PATH)
0427	        
0428	        if (success):
0429	            LAST_SAVE_TIME = current_time
0430	            logger.info("Throttled database save completed successfully")
0431	        else:
0432	            logger.warning("Throttled database save failed")
0433	            
0434	        return success
0435	            
0436	    except Exception as e:
0437	        logger.error(f"Error in throttled database save: {e}")
0438	        return False
0439	    finally:
0440	        DB_SAVE_IN_PROGRESS = False
0441	        DB_SAVE_LOCK.release()
0442	
0443	def db_write_worker():
0444	    """Worker thread that processes database write operations serially"""
0445	    global DB_WRITE_RUNNING
0446	    
0447	    DB_WRITE_RUNNING = True
0448	    logger.info("Database write worker started")
0449	    
0450	    try:
0451	        while DB_WRITE_RUNNING:
0452	            try:
0453	                # Get next write operation with timeout
0454	                operation = DB_WRITE_QUEUE.get(timeout=5)
0455	                
0456	                if (operation is None):  # None is a signal to stop
0457	                    logger.info("Received stop signal for DB write worker")
0458	                    break
0459	                    
0460	                # Rest of function remains the same...
0461	                    
0462	                # Unpack the operation
0463	                sql, params, callback = operation
0464	                
0465	                # Use the enhanced function with retries
0466	                from db_operations import execute_with_retry
0467	                result = execute_with_retry(
0468	                    DB_PATH, 
0469	                    sql, 
0470	                    params, 
0471	                    in_memory=DB_IN_MEMORY,
0472	                    cache_size_mb=DB_CACHE_SIZE_MB
0473	                )
0474	                
0475	                # If there's a callback with results
0476	                if callback:
0477	                    callback(result)
0478	                    
0479	                # Mark task as done
0480	                DB_WRITE_QUEUE.task_done()
0481	                
0482	                # Occasionally save memory DB to disk if needed
0483	                if random.random() < 0.01 and DB_IN_MEMORY:  # 1% chance per operation
0484	                    throttled_save_to_disk()
0485	                
0486	            except queue.Empty:
0487	                # Queue timeout, just continue waiting
0488	                pass
0489	                
0490	    except Exception as e:
0491	        logger.error(f"Error in DB write worker: {e}")
0492	    finally:
0493	        DB_WRITE_RUNNING = False
0494	        logger.info("Database write worker stopped")
0495	
0496	
0497	# Start DB write worker
0498	def start_db_write_worker():
0499	    global DB_WRITE_THREAD, DB_WRITE_RUNNING
0500	    
0501	    # Don't start if already running
0502	    if DB_WRITE_THREAD is not None and DB_WRITE_THREAD.is_alive():
0503	        return
0504	        
0505	    DB_WRITE_RUNNING = True
0506	    DB_WRITE_THREAD = threading.Thread(target=db_write_worker)
0507	    DB_WRITE_THREAD.daemon = True  # Make sure it's a daemon thread
0508	    DB_WRITE_THREAD.start()
0509	    logger.info("Started database write worker thread")
0510	
0511	# Call near the end of initialization
0512	start_db_write_worker()

0607	def ensure_single_instance(process_name):
0608	    """Ensure only one instance of a process is running"""
0609	    # Use LOCK_FILE_DIR instead of DB_PATH directory
0610	    lock_file = os.path.join(LOCK_FILE_DIR, f'.{process_name}_lock')
0611	    
0612	    if os.path.exists(lock_file):
0613	        try:
0614	            with open(lock_file, 'r') as f:
0615	                pid = int(f.read().strip())
0616	            
0617	            # Try to check if process exists
0618	            try:
0619	                os.kill(pid, 0)  # This will raise an exception if process doesn't exist
0620	                logger.warning(f"Another {process_name} process is already running")
0621	                return False
0622	            except OSError:
0623	                # Process doesn't exist, remove stale lock
0624	                os.remove(lock_file)
0625	        except:
0626	            # Invalid lock file, remove it
0627	            if os.path.exists(lock_file):
0628	                os.remove(lock_file)
0629	    
0630	    # Create new lock file
0631	    with open(lock_file, 'w') as f:
0632	        f.write(str(os.getpid()))
0633	    
0634	    return True
0635	
0636	def save_db_before_exit(signum=None, frame=None):
0637	    """Save in-memory database to disk before application exits"""
0638	    global _is_shutting_down, main_thread_conn
0639	    
0640	    # Prevent multiple shutdown handlers from running
0641	    if _is_shutting_down:
0642	        return
0643	    
0644	    _is_shutting_down = True
0645	    
0646	    if DB_IN_MEMORY and main_thread_conn:
0647	        try:
0648	            logger.info("Saving in-memory database to disk before exit...")
0649	            # Use throttled_save_to_disk with force=True to ensure it runs
0650	            throttled_save_to_disk(force=True)
0651	            logger.info("Database saved successfully before exit")
0652	        except Exception as e:
0653	            logger.error(f"Error saving database before exit: {e}")
0654	    
0655	    # Force exit after short delay if we get stuck
0656	    if signum in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
0657	        def force_exit():
0658	            sys.exit(0)
0659	        
0660	        # Schedule force exit in 2 seconds if still running
0661	        import threading
0662	        threading.Timer(2.0, force_exit).start()
0663	
0664	# Register exit handlers
0665	atexit.register(save_db_before_exit)
0666	signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, save_db_before_exit)
0667	signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, save_db_before_exit)

0675	@app.route('/search')
0676	def search():
0677	    """Search for tracks in the database"""
0678	    query = request.args.get('query', '')
0679	    
0680	    if not query:
0681	        return jsonify([])
0682	    
0683	    try:
0684	        # Update for PostgreSQL compatibility
0685	        tracks = execute_query_dict(
0686	            """SELECT id, file_path, title, artist, album, album_art_url, duration, liked
0687	               FROM tracks 
0688	               WHERE title ILIKE %s OR artist ILIKE %s OR album ILIKE %s 
0689	               ORDER BY artist, album, title
0690	               LIMIT %s""",
0691	            (f'%{query}%', f'%{query}%', f'%{query}%', MAX_SEARCH_RESULTS)
0692	        )
0693	        
0694	        logger.info(f"Search for '{query}' returned {len(tracks)} results")
0695	        return jsonify(tracks)
0696	    
0697	    except Exception as e:
0698	        logger.error(f"Error searching tracks: {e}")
0699	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500

0702	# Replace your current save_db_before_exit function with this improved version
0703	def clean_shutdown(signum=None, frame=None):
0704	    """Improved shutdown handler with timeout and forced exit"""
0705	    global _is_shutting_down, main_thread_conn, DB_WRITE_RUNNING
0706	    
0707	    # Prevent multiple shutdown handlers from running simultaneously
0708	    if _is_shutting_down:
0709	        logger.info("Shutdown already in progress, forcing exit")
0710	        os._exit(0)  # Force immediate exit if called twice
0711	    
0712	    _is_shutting_down = True
0713	    logger.info("Application shutting down gracefully...")
0714	    
0715	    try:
0716	        # Stop background threads first
0717	        DB_WRITE_RUNNING = False
0718	        
0719	        # Signal the queue to stop by adding None
0720	        try:
0721	            DB_WRITE_QUEUE.put(None)  # Signal worker to stop
0722	        except:
0723	            pass
0724	            
0725	        # Only try to save if we have a valid in-memory database
0726	        if DB_IN_MEMORY and main_thread_conn:
0727	            try:
0728	                # Verify connection is still valid before trying to save
0729	                # Use cursor for PostgreSQL connections instead of direct execute
0730	                try:
0731	                    # Check if this is a PostgreSQL connection
0732	                    if hasattr(main_thread_conn, 'cursor'):
0733	                        # PostgreSQL connection - use cursor
0734	                        cursor = main_thread_conn.cursor()
0735	                        cursor.execute("SELECT 1")
0736	                        cursor.close()
0737	                    else:
0738	                        # SQLite connection - direct execute
0739	                        main_thread_conn.execute("SELECT 1")
0740	                        
0741	                    logger.info("Saving in-memory database to disk before exit...")
0742	                    save_memory_db_to_disk(main_thread_conn, DB_PATH)
0743	                    logger.info("Database saved successfully")
0744	                except Exception as conn_err:
0745	                    logger.warning(f"Database connection error: {conn_err}")
0746	            except Exception as e:
0747	                logger.error(f"Error during database shutdown: {e}")
0748	    except Exception as e:
0749	        logger.error(f"Error during shutdown: {e}")
0750	    
0751	    # Force exit after short delay if we're handling a signal
0752	    if signum is not None:
0753	        logger.info("Forcing exit in 3 seconds if still running...")
0754	        
0755	        def force_exit():
0756	            logger.info("Forced exit triggered")
0757	            os._exit(0)  # Use os._exit which can't be caught or blocked
0758	        
0759	        # Schedule force exit with shorter timeout (3 seconds)
0760	        t = threading.Timer(3.0, force_exit)
0761	        t.daemon = True  # Make sure the timer itself doesn't block shutdown
0762	        t.start()
0763	
0764	# Update your signal handlers
0765	atexit.register(clean_shutdown)
0766	signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, clean_shutdown)
0767	signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, clean_shutdown)
0768	
0769	def update_analysis_progress(files_processed, total_files, current_file, error=None):
0770	    """Update the global analysis status with current progress"""
0771	    global ANALYSIS_STATUS
0772	    
0773	    # Calculate percent complete
0774	    percent = (files_processed / total_files) * 100 if total_files > 0 else 0
0775	    percent = round(percent, 1)
0776	    
0777	    # Update the global status dictionary
0778	    ANALYSIS_STATUS['files_processed'] = files_processed
0779	    ANALYSIS_STATUS['total_files'] = total_files
0780	    ANALYSIS_STATUS['current_file'] = current_file
0781	    ANALYSIS_STATUS['percent_complete'] = percent
0782	    ANALYSIS_STATUS['last_updated'] = datetime.now()
0783	    
0784	    if error:
0785	        ANALYSIS_STATUS['error'] = str(error)
0786	        logger.error(f"Analysis error: {error}")
0787	
0788	
0789	def analyze_directory_worker(folder_path, recursive):
0790	    """Worker thread that performs analysis with thread-safe DB access"""
0791	    
0792	    # Acquire lock to ensure only one analysis runs at a time
0793	    if not ANALYSIS_LOCK.acquire(blocking=False):
0794	        logger.warning("Another analysis is already running, skipping...")
0795	        return
0796	        
0797	    try:
0798	        # Reset the global status
0799	        global ANALYSIS_STATUS
0800	        ANALYSIS_STATUS = {
0801	            'running': True,
0802	            'start_time': datetime.now(),
0803	            'files_processed': 0,
0804	            'total_files': 0,
0805	            'current_file': '',
0806	            'percent_complete': 0,
0807	            'last_updated': datetime.now(),
0808	            'error': None
0809	        }
0810	        
0811	        logger.info(f"Starting analysis of {folder_path} (recursive={recursive})")
0812	        
0813	        try:
0814	            # Import necessary modules here to avoid circular imports
0815	            import numpy as np
0816	            import librosa
0817	            
0818	            # Create a dummy y variable in case it's needed
0819	            y = None
0820	            
0821	            # Now call the analyzer
0822	            result = analyzer.analyze_directory(folder_path, recursive=recursive)
0823	            
0824	            # Update ANALYSIS_STATUS with result
0825	            if result is None:
0826	                logger.warning("Analysis returned no result - folder may be empty or contain no audio files")
0827	                update_analysis_progress(0, 0, "", "No audio files found or folder is empty")
0828	            else:
0829	                # Handle successful analysis
0830	                files_processed = result.get('files_processed', 0)
0831	                tracks_added = result.get('tracks_added', 0)
0832	                logger.info(f"Analysis complete! Processed {files_processed} files, added {tracks_added} new tracks.")
0833	                
0834	        except NameError as e:
0835	            if "name 'y' is not defined" in str(e):
0836	                logger.error(f"Error running analysis: {e}")
0837	                update_analysis_progress(0, 0, "", "Analysis error: missing audio data variable")
0838	            else:
0839	                logger.error(f"Error running analysis: {e}")
0840	                update_analysis_progress(0, 0, "", str(e))
0841	        except Exception as e:
0842	            logger.error(f"Error running analysis: {e}")
0843	            update_analysis_progress(0, 0, "", str(e))
0844	    finally:
0845	        # Update status when done
0846	        ANALYSIS_STATUS['running'] = False
0847	        ANALYSIS_STATUS['last_updated'] = datetime.now()
0848	        
0849	        # Release the lock
0850	        ANALYSIS_LOCK.release()

0852	# Add after initialize_memory_db() function
0853	def setup_playlist_tables():
0854	    """Create playlist tables if they don't exist"""
0855	    logger.info("Setting up playlist tables")
0856	    try:
0857	        with optimized_connection(DB_PATH, DB_IN_MEMORY, DB_CACHE_SIZE_MB) as conn:
0858	            cursor = conn.cursor()
0859	            
0860	            # Create playlists table with PostgreSQL syntax
0861	            cursor.execute('''
0862	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS playlists (
0863	                    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
0864	                    name TEXT NOT NULL,
0865	                    description TEXT,
0866	                    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
0867	                    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
0868	                )
0869	            ''')
0870	            
0871	            # Create playlist items table with PostgreSQL syntax
0872	            cursor.execute('''
0873	                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS playlist_items (
0874	                    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
0875	                    playlist_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
0876	                    track_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
0877	                    position INTEGER NOT NULL,
0878	                    FOREIGN KEY (playlist_id) REFERENCES playlists(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
0879	                    FOREIGN KEY (track_id) REFERENCES audio_files(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
0880	                )
0881	            ''')
0882	            
0883	            conn.commit()
0884	            
0885	            # Check if tables were created successfully
0886	            cursor.execute("SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema='public' AND (table_name='playlists' OR table_name='playlist_items')")
0887	            tables = cursor.fetchall()
0888	            
0889	            if len(tables) == 2:
0890	                logger.info("Playlist tables created successfully")
0891	                return True
0892	            else:
0893	                logger.warning(f"Not all playlist tables were created. Found: {tables}")
0894	                return False
0895	                
0896	    except Exception as e:
0897	        logger.error(f"Error setting up playlist tables: {e}")
0898	        return False

0900	@app.route('/api/playlists', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
0901	def api_playlists():
0902	    if request.method == 'GET':
0903	        try:
0904	            logger.info("API: Getting all playlists via /api/playlists (GET)")
0905	            
0906	            playlists_data = execute_query_dict(
0907	                """
0908	                SELECT p.id, p.name, p.description, p.created_at, p.updated_at
0909	                FROM playlists p
0910	                ORDER BY p.updated_at DESC
0911	                """
0912	            )
0913	            logger.debug(f"Raw playlists_data from execute_query_dict: {playlists_data}")
0914	
0915	            if not playlists_data:
0916	                logger.info("API: No playlists found in database.")
0917	                return jsonify([])
0918	
0919	            # Ensure playlists_data is a list of dicts
0920	            if isinstance(playlists_data, list) and len(playlists_data) > 0 and not isinstance(playlists_data[0], dict):
0921	                logger.warning(f"API: execute_query_dict did not return a list of dictionaries. Got: {type(playlists_data[0])}. Attempting conversion.")
0922	                try:
0923	                    # Assuming column order from the SELECT query
0924	                    column_names = ['id', 'name', 'description', 'created_at', 'updated_at']
0925	                    converted_data = []
0926	                    for row_index, row in enumerate(playlists_data):
0927	                        if len(row) == len(column_names):
0928	                            converted_data.append(dict(zip(column_names, row)))
0929	                        else:
0930	                            logger.error(f"API: Row {row_index} has mismatched column count for conversion: {row}")
0931	                            # Add a placeholder or skip
0932	                            converted_data.append({'id': None, 'name': 'Conversion Error', 'description': '', 'track_count': 0})
0933	                    playlists_data = converted_data
0934	                    logger.info("API: Successfully converted playlist data to list of dicts.")
0935	                except Exception as conversion_error:
0936	                    logger.error(f"API: Failed to convert playlist data to dicts: {conversion_error}")
0937	                    return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error: playlist data format issue'}), 500
0938	            elif not isinstance(playlists_data, list):
0939	                 logger.error(f"API: execute_query_dict did not return a list. Got: {type(playlists_data)}")
0940	                 return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error: playlist data type issue'}), 500
0941	
0942	            processed_playlists = []
0943	            for p_data in playlists_data:
0944	                if not isinstance(p_data, dict):
0945	                    logger.warning(f"API: Skipping track count for non-dict playlist item: {p_data}")
0946	                    # If p_data was from a failed conversion, it might have an ID already
0947	                    if 'id' not in p_data: p_data['id'] = None 
0948	                    if 'name' not in p_data: p_data['name'] = 'Invalid Data'
0949	                    p_data['track_count'] = 0 
0950	                    processed_playlists.append(p_data)
0951	                    continue
0952	
0953	                playlist_item_id = p_data.get('id')
0954	                if playlist_item_id is None: # Could happen if conversion failed or ID was genuinely null
0955	                    logger.warning(f"API: Playlist item missing 'id' or id is None: {p_data}")
0956	                    p_data['track_count'] = 0 # Ensure track_count exists
0957	                    # Don't skip, allow it to be returned so frontend can decide based on ID
0958	                    processed_playlists.append(p_data)
0959	                    continue
0960	                
0961	                try:
0962	                    count_query_result = execute_query(
0963	                        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM playlist_items WHERE playlist_id = %s",
0964	                        (playlist_item_id,)
0965	                    )
0966	                    if count_query_result and len(count_query_result) > 0 and len(count_query_result[0]) > 0:
0967	                        p_data['track_count'] = count_query_result[0][0]
0968	                    else:
0969	                        p_data['track_count'] = 0
0970	                except Exception as count_exc:
0971	                    logger.error(f"API: Error getting track count for playlist_id {playlist_item_id}: {count_exc}")
0972	                    p_data['track_count'] = -1 # Indicate error in count
0973	                processed_playlists.append(p_data)
0974	            
0975	            logger.info(f"API: Found and processed {len(processed_playlists)} playlists")
0976	            return jsonify(processed_playlists)
0977	            
0978	        except Exception as e:
0979	            logger.error(f"API: Error getting playlists: {e}", exc_info=True)
0980	            return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
0981	            
0982	    elif request.method == 'POST':
0983	        try:
0984	            data = request.get_json()
0985	            if not data:
0986	                return jsonify({"error": "No data provided"}), 400
0987	
0988	            playlist_name = data.get('name')
0989	            playlist_description = data.get('description', '')
0990	            tracks = data.get('tracks', [])
0991	
0992	            if not playlist_name:
0993	                return jsonify({"error": "Playlist name is required"}), 400
0994	            
0995	            # Use the optimized_connection context manager
0996	            # Assuming optimized_connection is available and configured for psycopg2
0997	            with optimized_connection() as conn: # Ensure this context manager is correctly set up for psycopg2
0998	                cursor = conn.cursor(cursor_factory=psycopg2.extras.DictCursor)
0999	                
1000	                # Create the new playlist
1001	                cursor.execute(
1002	                    "INSERT INTO playlists (name, description) VALUES (%s, %s) RETURNING id",
1003	                    (playlist_name, playlist_description)
1004	                )
1005	                playlist_id = cursor.fetchone()['id']
1006	                
1007	                # Add tracks to the playlist
1008	                for i, track_id in enumerate(tracks):
1009	                    cursor.execute(
1010	                        "INSERT INTO playlist_items (playlist_id, track_id, position) VALUES (%s, %s, %s)",
1011	                        (playlist_id, track_id, i)
1012	                    )
1013	                
1014	                conn.commit()
1015	                    
1016	                return jsonify({
1017	                    "status": "success",
1018	                    "playlist_id": playlist_id,
1019	                    "message": f"Playlist '{playlist_name}' created with {len(tracks)} tracks"
1020	                })
1021	        
1022	        except Exception as e:
1023	            logger.error(f"API: Error saving playlist: {str(e)}") # Changed app.logger to logger
1024	            return jsonify({"error": str(e)}), 500
1025	
1026	@app.route('/playlists/<int:playlist_id>', methods=['GET'])
1027	def get_playlist(playlist_id):
1028	    logger.info(f"API: Getting details for playlist_id: {playlist_id}")
1029	    try:
1030	        # Fetch playlist details (id, name, description)
1031	        playlist_query = "SELECT id, name, description, created_at, updated_at FROM playlists WHERE id = %s"
1032	        
1033	        conn = get_connection()
1034	        try:
1035	            with conn.cursor(cursor_factory=psycopg2.extras.DictCursor) as cur:
1036	                cur.execute(playlist_query, (playlist_id,))
1037	                playlist_details_row = cur.fetchone()
1038	        finally:
1039	            release_connection(conn)
1040	
1041	        if not playlist_details_row:
1042	            logger.warning(f"API: Playlist with id {playlist_id} not found.")
1043	            return jsonify({'error': 'Playlist not found'}), 404
1044	
1045	        playlist_data = dict(playlist_details_row)
1046	        logger.debug(f"API: Playlist details fetched: {playlist_data}")
1047	
1048	        # Try to fetch and process tracks for the playlist
1049	        try:
1050	            # CORRECTED QUERY: Only included columns that definitely exist according to schema
1051	            tracks_query = """
1052	                SELECT 
1053	                    t.id, t.title, t.artist, t.album, t.duration, t.file_path, t.genre,
1054	                    t.album_art_url, t.year, t.liked,
1055	                    pi.position
1056	                FROM tracks t 
1057	                JOIN playlist_items pi ON t.id = pi.track_id
1058	                WHERE pi.playlist_id = %s
1059	                ORDER BY pi.position
1060	            """
1061	            # Removed last_played which doesn't exist in the schema
1062	
1063	            logger.debug(f"API: Executing tracks query for playlist_id {playlist_id} using 'tracks' table.")
1064	            tracks_list_raw = execute_query_dict(tracks_query, (playlist_id,))
1065	            logger.debug(f"API: Raw tracks_list from execute_query_dict for playlist_id {playlist_id}: {str(tracks_list_raw)[:500]} (Type: {type(tracks_list_raw)})")
1066	
1067	            if tracks_list_raw is None:
1068	                logger.warning(f"API: No tracks found for playlist_id {playlist_id} (execute_query_dict returned None). Initializing tracks as empty list.")
1069	                playlist_data['tracks'] = []
1070	            elif isinstance(tracks_list_raw, list):
1071	                processed_tracks = []
1072	                if not tracks_list_raw: # Empty list
1073	                    logger.info(f"API: execute_query_dict returned an empty list for tracks in playlist_id {playlist_id}.")
1074	                for track_row in tracks_list_raw:
1075	                    if isinstance(track_row, dict):
1076	                        processed_tracks.append(track_row)
1077	                    elif hasattr(track_row, 'keys'): # Handle DictRow or other dict-like objects
1078	                        processed_tracks.append(dict(track_row))
1079	                    else:
1080	                        logger.warning(f"API: Track item for playlist {playlist_id} is not a dict or DictRow: {type(track_row)}. Skipping.")
1081	                playlist_data['tracks'] = processed_tracks
1082	            elif hasattr(tracks_list_raw, 'keys'): # A single dict-like item (e.g. DictRow) was returned
1083	                logger.info(f"API: tracks_list_raw for playlist {playlist_id} was a single item ({type(tracks_list_raw)}). Processing as one track.")
1084	                playlist_data['tracks'] = [dict(tracks_list_raw)] # Convert to dict and wrap in a list
1085	            else: # tracks_list_raw is some other unexpected type
1086	                logger.error(f"API: tracks_list_raw for playlist {playlist_id} is unexpected type: {type(tracks_list_raw)}. Setting tracks to empty list.")
1087	                playlist_data['tracks'] = []
1088	
1089	        except Exception as e_track_processing: 
1090	            logger.error(f"API: Error occurred while fetching or processing tracks for playlist {playlist_id}: {e_track_processing}", exc_info=True)
1091	            playlist_data['tracks'] = [] 
1092	
1093	        logger.info(f"API: Responding for playlist {playlist_id} with {len(playlist_data.get('tracks', []))} tracks.")
1094	        return jsonify(playlist_data)
1095	
1096	    except KeyError as e: 
1097	        key_error_msg = e.args[0] if e.args else "Unknown Key"
1098	        logger.error(f"API: KeyError encountered in get_playlist for {playlist_id}: '{key_error_msg}'", exc_info=True)
1099	        return jsonify({'error': f"Internal server error (KeyError: '{key_error_msg}')"}), 500
1100	    except Exception as e:
1101	        logger.error(f"API: Unexpected error in get_playlist for {playlist_id}: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
1102	        return jsonify({'error': f'Internal server error: {str(e)}'}), 500
1103	
1104	@app.route('/playlists/<int:playlist_id>', methods=['PUT'])
1105	def update_playlist(playlist_id):
1106	    try:
1107	        data = request.get_json()
1108	        
1109	        # Validate required fields
1110	        if not data:
1111	            return jsonify({'error': 'No data provided'}), 400
1112	            
1113	        name = data.get('name')
1114	        description = data.get('description')
1115	        tracks = data.get('tracks')
1116	        
1117	        # Connect to database
1118	        with optimized_connection(DB_PATH, DB_IN_MEMORY, DB_CACHE_SIZE_MB) as conn:
1119	            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
1120	            cursor = conn.cursor()
1121	            
1122	            # Check if playlist exists
1123	            cursor.execute('SELECT id FROM playlists WHERE id = ?', (playlist_id,))
1124	            if not cursor.fetchone():
1125	                return jsonify({'error': 'Playlist not found'}), 404
1126	                
1127	            # Update playlist details if provided
1128	            if name or description is not None:
1129	                update_fields = []
1130	                update_values = []
1131	                
1132	                if name:
1133	                    update_fields.append('name = ?')
1134	                    update_values.append(name)
1135	                    
1136	                if description is not None:
1137	                    update_fields.append('description = ?')
1138	                    update_values.append(description)
1139	                    
1140	                update_fields.append('updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP')
1141	                
1142	                cursor.execute(
1143	                    f'UPDATE playlists SET {", ".join(update_fields)} WHERE id = ?',
1144	                    update_values + [playlist_id]
1145	                )
1146	                
1147	            # Update tracks if provided
1148	            if tracks is not None:
1149	                # First delete existing tracks
1150	                cursor.execute('DELETE FROM playlist_items WHERE playlist_id = ?', (playlist_id,))
1151	                
1152	                # Then insert new tracks
1153	                for i, track_id in enumerate(tracks):
1154	                    cursor.execute(
1155	                        'INSERT INTO playlist_items (playlist_id, track_id, position) VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
1156	                        (playlist_id, track_id, i)
1157	                    )
1158	                    
1159	            conn.commit()
1160	            
1161	        # Mark database as modified
1162	        g.db_modified = True
1163	            
1164	        return jsonify({'message': 'Playlist updated successfully'})
1165	        
1166	    except Exception as e:
1167	        logger.error(f"Error updating playlist: {e}")
1168	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
1169

1180	                return jsonify({'error': 'Playlist not found'}), 404

1184	            conn.commit()

1187	        g.db_modified = True
1188	            
1189	        return jsonify({'message': 'Playlist deleted successfully'})

1195	@app.route('/recent')
1196	def get_recent_tracks():
1197	    """Get recently added tracks"""
1198	    try:
1199	        limit = request.args.get('limit', default=10, type=int)
1200	        
1201	        # Use PostgreSQL-compatible query
1202	        recent_tracks = execute_query_dict(
1203	            """SELECT id, file_path, title, artist, album, album_art_url, date_added, duration 
1204	               FROM tracks 
1205	               ORDER BY date_added DESC 
1206	               LIMIT %s""", 
1207	            (limit,)
1208	        )
1209	        
1210	        # Set default titles
1211	        for track in recent_tracks:
1212	            if not track['title']:
1213	                track['title'] = os.path.basename(track['file_path'])
1214	                
1215	        return jsonify(recent_tracks)
1216	    except Exception as e:
1217	        logger.error(f"Error getting recent tracks: {e}")
1218	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
1219

1236	@app.route('/stream/<int:track_id>')
1237	def stream(track_id):
1238	    try:
1239	        # Get the track information with all metadata
1240	        result = execute_query_row(
1241	            """SELECT t.file_path, t.title, t.artist, t.album, t.album_art_url, t.duration 
1242	               FROM tracks t 
1243	               WHERE t.id = %s""",
1244	            (track_id,)
1245	        )
1246	        
1247	        if not result:
1248	            return jsonify({"error": "Track not found"}), 404
1249	            
1250	        file_path = result['file_path']
1251	        
1252	        # Check if file exists
1253	        if not os.path.exists(file_path):
1254	            return jsonify({"error": "Audio file not found"}), 404
1255	            
1256	        # Add cache-control headers for better streaming performance
1257	        response = send_file(file_path, conditional=True)
1258	        response.headers['Accept-Ranges'] = 'bytes'
1259	        response.headers['Cache-Control'] = 'public, max-age=3600'  # Cache for 1 hour
1260	        
1261	        # Add metadata headers for the frontend
1262	        response.headers['X-Track-Id'] = str(track_id)
1263	        response.headers['X-Track-Title'] = result.get('title', os.path.basename(file_path))
1264	        response.headers['X-Track-Artist'] = result.get('artist', 'Unknown Artist')
1265	        response.headers['X-Track-Album'] = result.get('album', 'Unknown Album')
1266	        
1267	        # Include album art URL if available
1268	        if result.get('album_art_url'):
1269	            response.headers['X-Track-Art'] = result.get('album_art_url')
1270	            
1271	        # Include duration if available
1272	        if result.get('duration'):
1273	            response.headers['X-Track-Duration'] = str(result.get('duration'))
1274	        
1275	        return response
1276	        
1277	    except Exception as e:
1278	        logger.error(f"Error streaming track {track_id}: {e}")
1279	        return jsonify({"error": str(e)}), 500
1280

1316	@app.route('/api/library/artists')
1317	def get_artists():
1318	    """Get all artists in the library"""
1319	    try:
1320	        # Use PostgreSQL-compatible query
1321	        artists = execute_query_dict(
1322	            """SELECT DISTINCT artist, 
1323	                  COUNT(*) as track_count,
1324	                  MAX(album_art_url) as artist_image_url
1325	               FROM tracks 
1326	               WHERE artist IS NOT NULL AND artist != ''
1327	               GROUP BY artist 
1328	               ORDER BY artist"""
1329	        )
1330	        
1331	        return jsonify(artists)
1332	    except Exception as e:
1333	        logger.error(f"Error getting artists: {e}")
1334	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500

1336	@app.route('/api/library/albums')
1337	def get_albums():
1338	    """Get all albums in the library"""
1339	    try:
1340	        # Use PostgreSQL-compatible query
1341	        albums = execute_query_dict(
1342	            """SELECT DISTINCT album, artist, COUNT(*) as track_count,
1343	                   (SELECT album_art_url FROM tracks WHERE album=a.album AND artist=a.artist LIMIT 1) as album_art_url,
1344	                   (SELECT file_path FROM tracks WHERE album=a.album AND artist=a.artist LIMIT 1) as sample_track
1345	            FROM tracks a
1346	            WHERE album IS NOT NULL AND album != ''
1347	            GROUP BY album, artist
1348	            ORDER BY album"""
1349	        )
1350	        
1351	        return jsonify(albums)
1352	    except Exception as e:
1353	        logger.error(f"Error getting albums: {e}")
1354	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500

1356	@app.route('/api/library/songs')
1357	def get_songs():
1358	    """Get all songs in the library"""
1359	    try:
1360	        # Use PostgreSQL-compatible query
1361	        songs = execute_query_dict(
1362	            """SELECT id, file_path, title, artist, album, album_art_url, duration
1363	               FROM tracks
1364	               ORDER BY title
1365	               LIMIT 50"""
1366	        )
1367	        
1368	        # Set default title for songs without title
1369	        for song in songs:
1370	            if not song.get('title'):
1371	                song['title'] = os.path.basename(song.get('file_path', 'Unknown'))
1372	                
1373	        logger.info(f"Returning {len(songs)} songs for library view")
1374	        return jsonify(songs)
1375	    except Exception as e:
1376	        logger.error(f"Error getting songs: {e}")
1377	        return jsonify([])  # Return empty array instead of error object

1379	# Add this route to handle updating artist images
1380	
1381	@app.route('/api/update-artist-images', methods=['POST'])
1382	def update_artist_images():
1383	    # Initialize LastFM service
1384	    lastfm_api_key = config.get('lastfm', 'api_key', fallback='')
1385	    lastfm_api_secret = config.get('lastfm', 'api_secret', fallback='')
1386	    
1387	    if not lastfm_api_key or not lastfm_api_secret:
1388	        return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "LastFM API keys not configured"}), 400
1389	        
1390	    service_name = request.args.get('service', 'lastfm')
1391	    
1392	    if service_name == 'lastfm':
1393	        service = LastFMService(lastfm_api_key, lastfm_api_secret)
1394	    else:
1395	        return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": f"Unknown service: {service_name}"}), 400
1396	    
1397	    try:
1398	        # Connect to database
1399	        with optimized_connection(DB_PATH, DB_IN_MEMORY, DB_CACHE_SIZE_MB) as conn:
1400	            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
1401	            cursor = conn.cursor()
1402	            
1403	            # Get distinct artists without images
1404	            cursor.execute('''
1405	                SELECT DISTINCT artist
1406	                FROM audio_files
1407	                WHERE artist IS NOT NULL AND artist != '' AND artist_image_url IS NULL OR artist_image_url = ''
1408	                LIMIT 50
1409	            ''')
1410	            
1411	            artists = [row['artist'] for row in cursor.fetchall()]
1412	            
1413	            if not artists:
1414	                return jsonify({"status": "success", "message": "No artists need images"})
1415	                
1416	            # Create cache directory if it doesn't exist
1417	            artist_cache_dir = 'artist_image_cache'
1418	            if not os.path.exists(artist_cache_dir):
1419	                os.makedirs(artist_cache_dir)
1420	                
1421	            updated_count = 0
1422	            
1423	            # Get images for each artist
1424	            for artist in artists:
1425	                try:
1426	                    # Check if we already have an image for this artist (might have been added in a previous run)
1427	                    if artist_has_image(artist):
1428	                        continue
1429	                        
1430	                    # Clean artist name
1431	                    clean_artist = sanitize_artist_name(artist)
1432	                    
1433	                    # Get image from service
1434	                    image_url = service.get_artist_image_url(clean_artist, cache_dir=artist_cache_dir)
1435	                    
1436	                    if image_url:
1437	                        # Update all tracks for this artist
1438	                        cursor.execute('''
1439	                            UPDATE audio_files
1440	                            SET artist_image_url = ?
1441	                            WHERE artist = ?
1442	                        ''', (image_url, artist))
1443	                        
1444	                        updated_count += cursor.rowcount
1445	                        
1446	                except Exception as e:
1447	                    logger.error(f"Error getting image for artist '{artist}': {e}")
1448	                    continue
1449	                    
1450	            # Commit changes
1451	            conn.commit()
1452	            
1453	            # Mark database as modified
1454	            g.db_modified = True
1455	                
1456	            return jsonify({
1457	                "status": "success",
1458	                "message": f"Updated {updated_count} tracks with artist images",
1459	                "artists_processed": len(artists),
1460	                "updated_count": updated_count
1461	            })
1462	            
1463	    except Exception as e:
1464	        logger.error(f"Error updating artist images: {e}")
1465	        return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": str(e)}), 500
1466	
1467	@app.route('/api/test-lastfm/<artist_name>')
1468	def test_lastfm(artist_name):
1469	    """Test lastfm API directly"""
1470	    try:
1471	        api_key = config.get('lastfm', 'api_key', fallback=None)
1472	        api_secret = config.get('lastfm', 'api_secret', fallback=None)
1473	        
1474	        if not api_key:
1475	            return jsonify({'error': 'LastFM API key not configured'}), 400
1476	        
1477	        # Make direct API request
1478	        import requests
1479	        base_url = "http://ws.audioscrobbler.com/2.0/"
1480	        params = {
1481	            'method': 'artist.getinfo',
1482	            'artist': artist_name,
1483	            'api_key': api_key,
1484	            'format': 'json'
1485	        }
1486	        
1487	        response = requests.get(base_url, params=params, timeout=10)
1488	        data = response.json()
1489	        
1490	        # Check for errors
1491	        if 'error' in data:
1492	            return jsonify({
1493	                'success': False,
1494	                'error': data.get('message', 'Unknown LastFM error'),
1495	                'code': data.get('error')
1496	            }), 400
1497	        
1498	        # Extract image URLs
1499	        artist_data = data.get('artist', {})
1500	        images = artist_data.get('image', [])
1501	        image_urls = {img.get('size'): img.get('#text') for img in images}
1502	        
1503	        return jsonify({
1504	            'success': True,
1505	            'artist': artist_name,
1506	            'images': image_urls,
1507	            'mbid': artist_data.get('mbid'),
1508	            'url': artist_data.get('url')
1509	        })
1510	        
1511	    except Exception as e:
1512	        logger.error(f"Error testing LastFM API: {e}")
1513	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500

1516	def test_lastfm_key():
1517	    """Test if the LastFM API key is valid"""
1518	    try:
1519	        # Try both API keys
1520	        main_key = config.get('lastfm', 'api_key', fallback=None)

1522

1524	        
1525	        # Test first key
1526	        if main_key:
1527	            import requests
1528	            base_url = "http://ws.audioscrobbler.com/2.0/"
1529	            params = {
1530	                'method': 'auth.getSession',
1531	                'api_key': main_key,
1532	                'format': 'json'
1533	            }
1534	            
1535	            response = requests.get(base_url, params=params, timeout=10)
1536	            results['main_key'] = {
1537	                'key': main_key[:5] + '...',
1538	                'status_code': response.status_code,
1539	                'response': response.text[:100]
1540	            }
1541	        
1542	        # Test second key
1543	        if backup_key:
1544	            import requests
1545	            base_url = "http://ws.audioscrobbler.com/2.0/"
1546	            params = {
1547	                'method': 'auth.getSession',
1548	                'api_key': backup_key,
1549	                'format': 'json'
1550	            }
1551	            
1552	            response = requests.get(base_url, params=params, timeout=10)
1553	            results['backup_key'] = {
1554	                'key': backup_key[:5] + '...',
1555	                'status_code': response.status_code,
1556	                'response': response.text[:100]
1557	            }
1558	        
1559	        return jsonify(results)
1560	        
1561	    except Exception as e:
1562	        logger.error(f"Error testing LastFM API keys: {e}")
1563	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
