1648	def run_scheduled_full_analysis():
1649	    """Run full analysis of pending files on schedule"""
1650	    try:
1651	        logger.info("Running scheduled full analysis")
1652	        # Quick scan first to identify new files
1653	        try:
1654	            music_directory = config.get('music', 'folder_path')
1655	            recursive = config.getboolean('music', 'recursive', fallback=True)
1656	            
1657	            if music_directory:
1658	                analyzer = MusicAnalyzer()  # Don't pass DB_PATH parameter here
1659	                analyzer.scan_library(music_directory, recursive)
1660	            else:
1661	                logger.warning("No music directory configured, skipping quick scan")
1662	        except Exception as e:
1663	            logger.error(f"Error running quick scan: {e}")
1664	
1665	        # Then full analysis
1666	        try:
1667	            conn = get_connection()
1668	            try:
1669	                cursor = conn.cursor()
1670	                
1671	                # Get count of files with no analysis
1672	                cursor.execute("""
1673	                    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tracks 
1674	                    WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT track_id FROM audio_features)
1675	                """)
1676	                pending_count = cursor.fetchone()
1677	                
1678	                if (pending_count and pending_count[0] > 0):
1679	                    logger.info(f"Found {pending_count[0]} tracks needing audio analysis")
1680	                    # Start analysis in a background thread
1681	                    analyzer = MusicAnalyzer()
1682	                    analyzer.analyze_pending_files()
1683	            finally:
1684	                release_connection(conn)
1685	        except Exception as e:
1686	            logger.error(f"Error in full analysis: {e}")
1687	            
1688	    except Exception as e:
1689	        logger.error(f"Error in scheduled analysis: {e}")
1690	    
1691	    logger.info("Scheduled full analysis completed")
1692	
1693	@app.route('/station/<int:seed_track_id>')
1694	def create_station(seed_track_id):
1695	    """Create a station from a seed track"""
1696	    try:
1697	        # Get number of tracks from query string or default
1698	        num_tracks = request.args.get('num_tracks', 
1699	                                    config.get('app', 'default_playlist_size', fallback=10), 
1700	                                    type=int)
1701	        
1702	        logger.info(f"Creating station with {num_tracks} tracks")
1703	        
1704	        # Get seed track
1705	        seed_track = execute_query_dict(
1706	            "SELECT * FROM tracks WHERE id = %s",
1707	            (seed_track_id,),
1708	            fetchone=True
1709	        )
1710	        
1711	        if not seed_track:
1712	            return jsonify({'error': 'Seed track not found'}), 404
1713	        
1714	        # Get audio features for the seed track
1715	        seed_features = execute_query_dict(
1716	            """SELECT * FROM audio_features WHERE track_id = %s""",
1717	            (seed_track_id,),
1718	            fetchone=True
1719	        )
1720	        
1721	        if not seed_features:
1722	            return jsonify({'error': 'No audio features available for this seed track'}), 400
1723	        
1724	        # Find similar tracks based on audio features
1725	        similar_tracks = execute_query_dict(
1726	            """
1727	            SELECT t.*, af.*
1728	            FROM audio_features af
1729	            JOIN tracks t ON af.track_id = t.id
1730	            WHERE t.id != %s
1731	            ORDER BY 
1732	                POWER(af.energy - %s, 2) +
1733	                POWER(af.danceability - %s, 2) +
1734	                POWER(af.valence - %s, 2) +
1735	                POWER(af.acousticness - %s, 2)
1736	            LIMIT %s
1737	            """,
1738	            (
1739	                seed_track_id, 
1740	                seed_features.get('energy', 0),
1741	                seed_features.get('danceability', 0),
1742	                seed_features.get('valence', 0),
1743	                seed_features.get('acousticness', 0),
1744	                num_tracks - 1  # -1 because we add the seed track at first position
1745	            )
1746	        )
1747	        
1748	        # Add seed track to the beginning of the result set
1749	        station = [seed_track] + similar_tracks
1750	        
1751	        return jsonify(station)
1752	        
1753	    except Exception as e:
1754	        logger.error(f"Error creating station: {e}")
1755	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
1756	
1757	@app.route('/api/station/<int:seed_track_id>')
1758	def create_station_api(seed_track_id):
1759	    """Create a station from a seed track - API endpoint"""
1760	    try:
1761	        # Get number of tracks from query string or default
1762	        num_tracks = request.args.get('num_tracks', 
1763	                                     config.get('app', 'default_playlist_size', fallback=10), 
1764	                                     type=int)
1765	        
1766	        logger.info(f"Creating station API with {num_tracks} tracks from seed {seed_track_id}")
1767	        
1768	        # Get seed track
1769	        seed_track = execute_query_dict(
1770	            "SELECT * FROM tracks WHERE id = %s",
1771	            (seed_track_id,),
1772	            fetchone=True
1773	        )
1774	        
1775	        if not seed_track:
1776	            logger.error(f"Seed track not found for ID: {seed_track_id}")
1777	            return jsonify({"error": "Seed track not found"}), 404
1778	        
1779	        # Get audio features for the seed track
1780	        seed_features = execute_query_dict(
1781	            """SELECT * FROM audio_features WHERE track_id = %s""",
1782	            (seed_track_id,),
1783	            fetchone=True
1784	        )
1785	        
1786	        if not seed_features:
1787	            logger.error(f"No audio features found for seed track: {seed_track_id}")
1788	            
1789	            # Return a fallback station with random tracks if no audio features
1790	            random_tracks = execute_query_dict(
1791	                """
1792	                SELECT t.*
1793	                FROM tracks t
1794	                WHERE t.id != %s
1795	                ORDER BY RANDOM()
1796	                LIMIT %s
1797	                """,
1798	                (seed_track_id, num_tracks - 1)
1799	            )
1800	            
1801	            station = [seed_track] + random_tracks
1802	            logger.info(f"Created fallback random station with {len(station)} tracks")
1803	            return jsonify(station)
1804	        
1805	        # Find similar tracks based on audio features
1806	        similar_tracks = execute_query_dict(
1807	            """
1808	            SELECT t.*
1809	            FROM audio_features af
1810	            JOIN tracks t ON af.track_id = t.id
1811	            WHERE t.id != %s
1812	            ORDER BY 
1813	                POWER(af.energy - %s, 2) +
1814	                POWER(af.danceability - %s, 2) +
1815	                POWER(af.valence - %s, 2) +
1816	                POWER(af.acousticness - %s, 2)
1817	            LIMIT %s
1818	            """,
1819	            (
1820	                seed_track_id, 
1821	                seed_features.get('energy', 0),
1822	                seed_features.get('danceability', 0),
1823	                seed_features.get('valence', 0),
1824	                seed_features.get('acousticness', 0),
1825	                num_tracks - 1  # -1 because we add the seed track at first position
1826	            )
1827	        )
1828	        
1829	        # Add seed track to the beginning of the result set
1830	        station = [seed_track] + similar_tracks
1831	        logger.info(f"Successfully created station with {len(station)} tracks")
1832	        return jsonify(station)
1833	        
1834	    except Exception as e:
1835	        logger.error(f"Error creating station: {e}")
1836	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
1837

1876	@app.route('/api/logs/view', methods=['GET'])
1877	def view_logs():
1878	    try:
1879	        lines = request.args.get('lines', default=100, type=int)
1880	        log_dir = config.get('logging', 'log_dir', fallback='logs')
1881	        log_file = os.path.join(log_dir, 'pump.log')
1882	        
1883	        if not os.path.exists(log_file):
1884	            logger.error(f"Log file not found: {log_file}")
1885	            return jsonify({"error": "Log file not found"}), 404
1886	        
1887	        try:
1888	            with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
1889	                # Use safe approach to read logs in case file is large
1890	                all_lines = []
1891	                for line in f:
1892	                    all_lines.append(line)
1893	                    # Keep only the last 'lines' number of entries
1894	                    if len(all_lines) > lines:
1895	                        all_lines.pop(0)
1896	                log_lines = all_lines
1897	            
1898	            return jsonify({"logs": log_lines})
1899	        except UnicodeDecodeError:
1900	            # Try with different encoding if UTF-8 fails
1901	            logger.warning(f"Unicode decode error, trying with latin-1 encoding")
1902	            with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='latin-1') as f:
1903	                all_lines = []
1904	                for line in f:
1905	                    all_lines.append(line)
1906	                    if len(all_lines) > lines:
1907	                        all_lines.pop(0)
1908	                log_lines = all_lines
1909	            
1910	            return jsonify({"logs": log_lines})
1911	            
1912	    except Exception as e:
1913	        logger.error(f"Error viewing logs: {e}", exc_info=True)
1914	        return jsonify({"error": f"Failed to view logs: {str(e)}"}), 500
1915

1940	@app.route('/api/quick-scan', methods=['POST'])
1941	def quick_scan_api():
1942	    """API endpoint for quick scanning music library"""
1943	    try:
1944	        # Add more verbose debugging
1945	        logger.debug(f"Scan library request received: {request.get_data(as_text=True)}")
1946	        
1947	        # Support both JSON and form data
1948	        if request.is_json:
1949	            data = request.get_json()
1950	            logger.debug(f"JSON data received: {data}")
1951	        else:
1952	            data = request.form.to_dict()
1953	            logger.debug(f"Form data received: {data}")
1954	        
1955	        # Get folder path from POST data
1956	        folder_path = data.get('folder_path')
1957	        logger.debug(f"Extracted folder_path: {folder_path}")
1958	        
1959	        # If folder_path is not in the request, try to get it from configuration
1960	        if not folder_path:
1961	            folder_path = config.get('music', 'folder_path', fallback=None)
1962	            logger.debug(f"Using folder_path from config: {folder_path}")
1963	            
1964	            if not folder_path:
1965	                logger.warning("No music folder path specified in request or config")
1966	                return jsonify({
1967	                    'status': 'error',
1968	                    'message': 'No music folder path specified. Please configure a music folder first.'
1969	                }), 400
1970	        
1971	        # Check if folder exists
1972	        if not os.path.exists(folder_path):
1973	            return jsonify({
1974	                'status': 'error',
1975	                'message': f'Music folder not found: {folder_path}'
1976	            }), 404
1977	        
1978	        # Get recursive parameter with proper type handling
1979	        recursive_param = data.get('recursive', True)
1980	        
1981	        # Handle both string and boolean values
1982	        if isinstance(recursive_param, str):
1983	            recursive = recursive_param.lower() in ('true', '1', 't', 'y', 'yes')
1984	        else:
1985	            recursive = bool(recursive_param)
1986	            
1987	        # Don't start if already running
1988	        if QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['running']:
1989	            return jsonify({
1990	                "status": "error", 
1991	                "message": "A scan is already in progress"
1992	            }), 409
1993	            
1994	        # Start quick scan in a background thread if available
1995	        if 'run_quick_scan' in globals():
1996	            thread = threading.Thread(
1997	                target=run_quick_scan,
1998	                args=(folder_path, recursive)
1999	            )
2000	            thread.daemon = True
2001	            thread.start()
2002	            
2003	            return jsonify({
2004	                'status': 'success',
2005	                'message': "Quick scan started in background"
2006	            })
2007	        else:
2008	            # Fall back to synchronous scan if run_quick_scan is not available
2009	            analyzer = MusicAnalyzer()
2010	            result = analyzer.scan_library(folder_path, recursive)
2011	            
2012	            # Return success response
2013	            return jsonify({
2014	                'status': 'success',
2015	                'message': f"Scan complete: {result.get('files_processed', 0)} files processed",
2016	                'files_processed': result.get('files_processed', 0),
2017	                'tracks_added': result.get('tracks_added', 0)
2018	            })
2019	        
2020	    except Exception as e:
2021	        logger.error(f"Error scanning library: {e}")
2022	        return jsonify({
2023	            'status': 'error',
2024	            'message': f"Error scanning library: {str(e)}"
2025	        }), 500
2026

2031	        if analysis_progress['is_running']:
2032	            return jsonify({
2033	                'status': 'error',
2034	                'message': 'Analysis is already running'
2035	            }), 409

2108	        pending = cursor.fetchone()[0]

2191	@app.route('/api/update-metadata', methods=['POST'])
2192	def update_metadata():
2193	    """Handle metadata update request"""
2194	    try:
2195	        if not metadata_service:
2196	            logger.error("Metadata update failed: Metadata service not available")
2197	            return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Metadata service not available"}), 500
2198	            
2199	        # Extract skip_existing from form data with proper debugging
2200	        try:
2201	            skip_existing = request.form.get('skip_existing', 'false') == 'true'
2202	            logger.info(f"Received form data: {dict(request.form)}")
2203	        except Exception as form_error:
2204	            logger.error(f"Error parsing form data: {form_error}")
2205	            # Fallback to JSON if form parsing fails
2206	            data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
2207	            skip_existing = data.get('skip_existing', False)
2208	        
2209	        logger.info(f"Metadata update requested with skip_existing={skip_existing}")
2210	        
2211	        # Check if metadata update is already running
2212	        if METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS.get('running', False):
2213	            logger.info("Metadata update already in progress")
2214	            return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Metadata update already in progress"}), 409
2215	        
2216	        # Update status
2217	        METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS.update({
2218	            'running': True,
2219	            'start_time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
2220	            'total_tracks': 0,
2221	            'processed_tracks': 0,
2222	            'updated_tracks': 0,
2223	            'current_track': '',
2224	            'percent_complete': 0,
2225	            'last_updated': datetime.now().isoformat(),
2226	            'error': None,
2227	            'scan_complete': True  # Add this for UI consistency
2228	        })
2229	        
2230	        # Start metadata update in a background thread
2231	        metadata_thread = threading.Thread(target=run_metadata_update, args=(skip_existing,))
2232	        metadata_thread.daemon = True
2233	        metadata_thread.start()
2234	        
2235	        # Mark database as modified for the request context
2236	        g.db_modified = True
2237	        
2238	        logger.info("Metadata update thread started successfully")
2239	        return jsonify({"status": "started", "message": "Metadata update started"})
2240	    except Exception as e:
2241	        logger.error(f"Error starting metadata update: {e}")
2242	        # Update status with error
2243	        METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS.update({
2244	            'running': False,
2245	            'error': str(e),
2246	            'last_updated': datetime.now().isoformat()
2247	        })
2248	        return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": str(e)}), 500

2250	# Add this function to run metadata update in background
2251	def run_metadata_update(skip_existing=False):
2252	    """Run metadata update in a background thread"""
2253	    try:
2254	        if not metadata_service:
2255	            logger.error("Cannot update metadata: Metadata service not available")
2256	            return
2257	            
2258	        # Run metadata update
2259	        logger.info(f"Starting metadata update (skip_existing={skip_existing})")
2260	        result = metadata_service.update_all_metadata(status_tracker=METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS, skip_existing=skip_existing)
2261	        
2262	        # Update final status
2263	        METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS.update({
2264	            'running': False,
2265	            'percent_complete': 100,
2266	            'last_updated': datetime.now().isoformat()
2267	        })
2268	        
2269	
2270	        
2271	        logger.info(f"Metadata update completed successfully: {result.get('processed', 0)} processed, {result.get('updated', 0)} updated")
2272	        
2273	    except Exception as e:
2274	        logger.error(f"Error updating metadata: {e}")
2275	        # Update status with error
2276	        METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS.update({
2277	            'running': False,
2278	            'error': str(e),
2279	            'last_updated': datetime.now().isoformat()
2280	        })

2282	# Add this helper function to check if an artist already has an image
2283	def artist_has_image(artist_name):
2284	    """Check if artist already has an image in the database"""
2285	    if not artist_name:
2286	        return False
2287	        
2288	    try:
2289	        conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
2290	        cursor = conn.cursor()
2291	        cursor.execute(
2292	            "SELECT artist_image_url FROM audio_files WHERE artist = ? AND artist_image_url IS NOT NULL AND artist_image_url != '' LIMIT 1", 
2293	            (artist_name,)
2294	        )
2295	        result = cursor.fetchone()
2296	        conn.close()
2297	        return result is not None and result[0]
2298	    except Exception as e:
2299	        logger.error(f"Error checking artist image: {e}")
2300	        return False

2302	# Add this helper function to sanitize artist names
2303	def sanitize_artist_name(artist_name):
2304	    """Clean artist names that might contain multiple artists"""
2305	    if not artist_name:
2306	        return ""
2307	        
2308	    # Split on common separators that might indicate multiple artists
2309	    separators = ["feat.", "ft.", "featuring", "with", "vs", "x", "&"]
2310	    
2311	    for sep in separators:
2312	        if (sep in artist_name.lower()):
2313	            # Take only the main artist (before the separator)
2314	            return artist_name.split(sep, 1)[0].strip()
2315	    
2316	    # Check for patterns like "ArtistA ArtistB" (where names are concatenated)
2317	    # This is harder to detect reliably, but we can check for common cases
2318	    if len(artist_name) > 20 and not " and " in artist_name.lower() and not " & " in artist_name.lower():
2319	        # Look for potential CamelCase splitting points
2320	        camel_case_match = re.search(r'([a-z])([A-Z])', artist_name)
2321	        if camel_case_match:
2322	            split_point = camel_case_match.start() + 1
2323	            return artist_name[:split_point]
2324	    
2325	    return artist_name
2326	
2327	@app.route('/api/metadata-update/status')
2328	def metadata_update_status():
2329	    """Get the current status of a metadata update"""
2330	    try:
2331	        is_running = METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['running']
2332	        
2333	        # Calculate elapsed time if running
2334	        elapsed_seconds = 0
2335	        if is_running and METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['start_time']:
2336	            try:
2337	                # Handle both string and datetime start_time
2338	                if isinstance(METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['start_time'], str):
2339	                    start_time = datetime.fromisoformat(METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['start_time'])
2340	                else:
2341	                    start_time = METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['start_time']
2342	                    
2343	                elapsed = datetime.now() - start_time
2344	                elapsed_seconds = elapsed.total_seconds()
2345	            except Exception as e:
2346	                logger.error(f"Error calculating elapsed time: {e}")
2347	            
2348	        # Calculate estimated time remaining
2349	        remaining_seconds = 0
2350	        if is_running and METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['percent_complete'] > 0:
2351	            # Avoid division by zero
2352	            percent = max(0.1, METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['percent_complete'])
2353	            remaining_seconds = (elapsed_seconds / percent) * (100 - percent)
2354	            
2355	        return jsonify({
2356	            'running': is_running,
2357	            'total_tracks': METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['total_tracks'],
2358	            'processed_tracks': METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['processed_tracks'],
2359	            'updated_tracks': METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['updated_tracks'],
2360	            'current_track': METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['current_track'],
2361	            'percent_complete': METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['percent_complete'],
2362	            'elapsed_seconds': round(elapsed_seconds),
2363	            'remaining_seconds': round(remaining_seconds),
2364	            'error': METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['error']
2365	        })
2366	    except Exception as e:
2367	        logger.error(f"Error getting metadata update status: {e}")
2368	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
2369	
2370	@app.route('/api/analysis/status')
2371	def get_analysis_status():
2372	    """Get the current analysis status"""
2373	    try:
2374	        # Create a copy of the status to avoid race conditions
2375	        status = dict(ANALYSIS_STATUS)
2376	        
2377	        # Ensure proper percent calculation - don't show 100% unless actually complete
2378	        if status.get('running') == False and status.get('error') is not None:
2379	            # If there was an error and not running, calculate actual percentage
2380	            if status.get('total_files', 0) > 0:
2381	                status['percent_complete'] = min(99, round((status.get('files_processed', 0) / status.get('total_files', 0)) * 100, 1))
2382	            else:
2383	                status['percent_complete'] = 0
2384	        
2385	        # Convert datetime objects to ISO format strings or handle None values
2386	        if status.get('start_time') is not None:
2387	            if isinstance(status['start_time'], datetime):
2388	                status['start_time'] = status['start_time'].isoformat()
2389	        
2390	        if status.get('last_updated') is not None:
2391	            if isinstance(status['last_updated'], datetime):
2392	                status['last_updated'] = status['last_updated'].isoformat()
2393	        
2394	        return jsonify(status)
2395	    except Exception as e:
2396	        logger.error(f"Error getting analysis status: {e}")
2397	        return jsonify({
2398	            'running': False,
2399	            'error': f"Failed to get status: {str(e)}"
2400	        })

2436	@app.route('/cache/<path:filename>')
2437	def serve_cache_file(filename):
2438	    """Serve a file directly from the cache directory"""
2439	    try:
2440	        # Ensure no path traversal vulnerability
2441	        if ".." in filename:
2442	            return "Invalid path", 400
2443	            
2444	        # Full path to the cached file
2445	        cache_path = os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, filename)
2446	        
2447	        if os.path.exists(cache_path) and os.path.isfile(cache_path):
2448	            logger.debug(f"Serving cached file: {filename}")
2449	            return send_file(cache_path, mimetype='image/jpeg')
2450	        else:
2451	            logger.warning(f"Cache file not found: {filename}")
2452	            return send_file('static/images/default-album-art.png', mimetype='image/jpeg')
2453	    except Exception as e:
2454	        logger.error(f"Error serving cache file {filename}: {e}")
2455	        return send_file('static/images/default-album-art.png', mimetype='image/jpeg')

2457	@app.route('/api/library/stats')
2458	def get_library_stats():
2459	    """Get statistics about the music library"""
2460	    try:
2461	        with optimized_connection(DB_PATH, DB_IN_MEMORY, DB_CACHE_SIZE_MB) as conn:
2462	            cursor = conn.cursor()
2463	            
2464	            # Get total tracks count
2465	            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audio_files")
2466	            total_tracks = cursor.fetchone()[0]
2467	            
2468	            # Get tracks with metadata count
2469	            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audio_files WHERE metadata_source IS NOT NULL")
2470	            tracks_with_metadata = cursor.fetchone()[0]
2471	            
2472	            # Get analyzed tracks count 
2473	            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audio_files WHERE analysis_status = 'analyzed'")
2474	            analyzed_tracks = cursor.fetchone()[0]
2475	            
2476	            # Calculate DB size
2477	            db_size_bytes = os.path.getsize(DB_PATH)
2478	            db_size_mb = round(db_size_bytes / (1024 * 1024), 2)  # Convert to MB
2479	            
2480	            # Calculate cache size
2481	            cache_dir = config.get('cache', 'image_cache_dir', fallback='album_art_cache')
2482	            cache_size_bytes = 0
2483	            if os.path.exists(cache_dir):
2484	                for file in os.listdir(cache_dir):
2485	                    file_path = os.path.join(cache_dir, file)
2486	                    if os.path.isfile(file_path):
2487	                        cache_size_bytes += os.path.getsize(file_path)
2488	            cache_size_mb = round(cache_size_bytes / (1024 * 1024), 2)  # Convert to MB
2489	            
2490	            return jsonify({
2491	                'status': 'success',
2492	                'stats': {
2493	                    'total_tracks': total_tracks,
2494	                    'tracks_with_metadata': tracks_with_metadata,
2495	                    'analyzed_tracks': analyzed_tracks,
2496	                    'db_size_mb': db_size_mb,
2497	                    'cache_size_mb': cache_size_mb
2498	                }
2499	            })
2500	    except Exception as e:
2501	        logger.error(f"Error getting library stats: {e}")
2502	        return jsonify({
2503	            'status': 'error',
2504	            'message': str(e)
2505	        })

2508	def run_quick_scan(folder_path, recursive=True):
2509	    """Run quick scan in a background thread"""
2510	    try:
2511	        # Make sure analyzer exists
2512	        if not analyzer:
2513	            logger.error("Cannot run quick scan: Music analyzer not available")
2514	            return
2515	            
2516	        # Update status
2517	        QUICK_SCAN_STATUS.update({
2518	            'running': True,
2519	            'start_time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
2520	            'files_processed': 0,
2521	            'tracks_added': 0,
2522	            'total_files': 0,
2523	            'current_file': '',
2524	            'percent_complete': 0,
2525	            'last_updated': datetime.now().isoformat(),
2526	            'error': None
2527	        })
2528	        
2529	        # Run scan
2530	        logger.info(f"Starting quick scan of {folder_path} (recursive={recursive})")
2531	        result = analyzer.scan_library(folder_path, recursive=recursive)
2532	        
2533	        # Update final status
2534	        QUICK_SCAN_STATUS.update({
2535	            'running': False,
2536	            'percent_complete': 100,
2537	            'files_processed': result.get('processed', 0),
2538	            'tracks_added': result.get('added', 0),
2539	            'last_updated': datetime.now().isoformat()
2540	        })
2541	        
2542	
2543	            
2544	        logger.info(f"Quick scan completed successfully. Processed {result.get('processed', 0)} files, added {result.get('added', 0)} tracks.")
2545	        
2546	    except Exception as e:
2547	        logger.error(f"Error running quick scan: {e}")
2548	        # Update status with error
2549	        QUICK_SCAN_STATUS.update({
2550	            'running': False,
2551	            'error': str(e),
2552	            'last_updated': datetime.now().isoformat()
2553	        })
2554

2557	@app.route('/api/quick-scan/status')
2558	def quick_scan_status():
2559	    """Get the current status of a quick scan"""
2560	    try:
2561	        is_running = QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['running']
2562	        
2563	        # Calculate elapsed time if running
2564	        elapsed_seconds = 0
2565	        if is_running and QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['start_time']:
2566	            # Parse the ISO format string back to datetime
2567	            start_time = datetime.fromisoformat(QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['start_time'])
2568	            elapsed = datetime.now() - start_time
2569	            elapsed_seconds = elapsed.total_seconds()
2570	            
2571	        # Calculate estimated time remaining if possible
2572	        remaining_seconds = 0
2573	        if is_running and QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['percent_complete'] > 0:
2574	            # Avoid division by zero
2575	            percent = max(0.1, QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['percent_complete'])
2576	            remaining_seconds = (elapsed_seconds / percent) * (100 - percent)
2577	            
2578	        return jsonify({
2579	            'running': is_running,
2580	            'files_processed': QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['files_processed'],
2581	            'tracks_added': QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['tracks_added'],
2582	            'total_files': QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['total_files'],
2583	            'current_file': QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['current_file'],
2584	            'percent_complete': QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['percent_complete'],
2585	            'elapsed_seconds': round(elapsed_seconds),
2586	            'remaining_seconds': round(remaining_seconds),
2587	            'error': QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['error']
2588	        })
2589	    except Exception as e:
2590	        logger.error(f"Error getting quick scan status: {e}")
2591	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500

2593	@app.route('/api/quick-scan', methods=['POST'])
2594	def quick_scan_api():
2595	    """API endpoint for quick scanning music library"""
2596	    try:
2597	        data = request.get_json()
2598	        folder_path = data.get('folder_path', '')
2599	        recursive = data.get('recursive', True)
2600	        
2601	        if not folder_path:
2602	            return jsonify({"success": False, "error": "No folder path specified"}), 400
2603	            
2604	        # Don't start if already running
2605	        if QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['running']:
2606	            return jsonify({
2607	                "success": False, 
2608	                "error": "A scan is already in progress"
2609	            })
2610	            
2611	        # Start quick scan in a background thread
2612	        thread = threading.Thread(
2613	            target=run_quick_scan,
2614	            args=(folder_path, recursive)
2615	        )
2616	        thread.daemon = True
2617	        thread.start()
2618	        
2619	        # Mark database as modified
2620	        g.db_modified = True
2621	        
2622	        logger.info(f"Started quick scan for directory: {folder_path} (recursive={recursive})")
2623	        
2624	        return jsonify({
2625	            "success": True,
2626	            "message": "Quick scan started in background"
2627	        })
2628	        
2629	    except Exception as e:
2630	        logger.error(f"Error starting quick scan: {e}")
2631	        return jsonify({"success": False, "error": str(e)}), 500

2633	def update_scheduler():
2634	    """Update the scheduler based on current configuration"""
2635	    global SCHEDULER_TIMER, SCHEDULER_RUNNING
2636	    
2637	    # Cancel any existing timer
2638	    if SCHEDULER_TIMER:
2639	        SCHEDULER_TIMER.cancel()
2640	        SCHEDULER_TIMER = None
2641	    
2642	    # Get current settings
2643	    frequency = config.get('scheduler', 'schedule_frequency', fallback='never')
2644	    
2645	    # If schedule is disabled, just return
2646	    if frequency == 'never':
2647	        logger.info("Scheduler disabled")
2648	        return
2649	    
2650	    # Calculate interval in seconds
2651	    interval = get_interval_seconds(frequency)
2652	    
2653	    # Schedule the next run
2654	    SCHEDULER_TIMER = threading.Timer(interval, run_scheduled_tasks)
2655	    SCHEDULER_TIMER.daemon = True
2656	    SCHEDULER_TIMER.start()
2657	    
2658	    logger.info(f"Scheduler set to run every {frequency}")
2659	
2660	def get_interval_seconds(frequency):
2661	    """Convert frequency string to seconds"""
2662	    if frequency == '15min':
2663	        return 15 * 60
2664	    elif frequency == '1hour':
2665	        return 60 * 60
2666	    elif frequency == '6hours':
2667	        return 6 * 60 * 60
2668	    elif frequency == '12hours':
2669	        return 12 * 60 * 60
2670	    elif frequency == '24hours':
2671	        return 24 * 60 * 60  # Default to 24 hours
2672	
2673	def calculate_next_run_time():
2674	    """Calculate when the next scheduled run will happen"""
2675	    frequency = config.get('scheduler', 'schedule_frequency', fallback='never')
2676	    
2677	    if frequency == 'never':
2678	        return "Not scheduled"
2679	    
2680	    # Get last run time
2681	    last_run_str = config.get('scheduler', 'last_run', fallback=None)
2682	    
2683	    if not last_run_str:
2684	        # If never run, schedule from now
2685	        last_run = datetime.now()
2686	    else:
2687	        try:
2688	            last_run = datetime.fromisoformat(last_run_str)
2689	        except (ValueError, TypeError):
2690	            last_run = datetime.now()
2691	    
2692	    # Calculate next run time
2693	    interval = get_interval_seconds(frequency)
2694	    next_run = last_run + timedelta(seconds=interval)
2695	    
2696	    # Format for display
2697	    now = datetime.now()
2698	    if next_run < now:
2699	        # If we're past due, reschedule from now
2700	        next_run = now + timedelta(seconds=interval)
2701	    
2702	    # Format the time
2703	    if (next_run - now).total_seconds() < 60:
2704	        return "Less than a minute"
2705	    elif (next_run - now).total_seconds() < 3600:
2706	        minutes = int((next_run - now).total_seconds() / 60)
2707	        return f"{minutes} minute{'s' if minutes != 1 else ''}"
2708	    else:
2709	        return next_run.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
2710	
2711	def run_scheduled_tasks():
2712	    """Run the configured tasks on schedule"""
2713	    global SCHEDULER_RUNNING
2714	    
2715	    if SCHEDULER_RUNNING:
2716	        logger.warning("Scheduled tasks already running, skipping this run")
2717	        # Reschedule for next time
2718	        update_scheduler()
2719	        return
2720	    
2721	    try:
2722	        SCHEDULER_RUNNING = True
2723	        logger.info("Starting scheduled tasks")
2724	        
2725	        # Get the action to perform
2726	        action = config.get('scheduler', 'startup_action', fallback='nothing')
2727	        
2728	        # Update last run time
2729	        config.set('scheduler', 'last_run', datetime.now().isoformat())
2730	        with open(config_file, 'w') as f:
2731	            config.write(f)
2732	        
2733	        # Run the appropriate action(s)
2734	        if action == 'nothing':
2735	            logger.info("No action configured for scheduler")
2736	        elif action == 'quick_scan':
2737	            run_quick_scan_task()
2738	        elif action == 'quick_scan_metadata':
2739	            run_quick_scan_task()
2740	            run_metadata_update_task()
2741	        elif action == 'full_analysis':
2742	            run_quick_scan_task()
2743	            run_metadata_update_task()
2744	            run_full_analysis_task()
2745	        
2746	        logger.info("Scheduled tasks completed")
2747	    except Exception as e:
2748	        logger.error(f"Error running scheduled tasks: {e}")
2749	    finally:
2750	        SCHEDULER_RUNNING = False
2751	        # Reschedule for next time
2752	        update_scheduler()
2753	
2754	
2755	
2756	def run_quick_scan_task():
2757	    """Run quick scan as a scheduled task"""
2758	    logger.info("Running scheduled quick scan")
2759	    
2760	    # Get folder path from config
2761	    folder_path = config.get('music', 'folder_path', fallback='')
2762	    recursive = config.getboolean('music', 'recursive', fallback=True)
2763	    
2764	    if not folder_path:
2765	        logger.error("Music folder path not configured")
2766	        return False
2767	        
2768	    if not os.path.exists(folder_path):
2769	        logger.error(f"Music folder path does not exist: {folder_path}")
2770	        return False
2771	        
2772	    try:
2773	        result = run_quick_scan(folder_path, recursive)
2774	        logger.info("Scheduled quick scan completed")
2775	        
2776	        # Add this line to save changes if in-memory mode is active
2777	        if DB_IN_MEMORY and main_thread_conn:
2778	            throttled_save_to_disk()
2779	            
2780	        return result
2781	    except Exception as e:
2782	        logger.error(f"Error running scheduled quick scan: {e}")
2783	        return False

2786	def create_similar_playlist(seed_track_id, limit=10):
2787	    """Create a playlist of similar tracks based on audio features"""
2788	    try:
2789	        # Get seed track features
2790	        seed_features = execute_query_dict(
2791	            """
2792	            SELECT af.* 
2793	            FROM audio_features af
2794	            WHERE af.track_id = %s
2795	            """,
2796	            (seed_track_id,),
2797	            fetchone=True
2798	        )
2799	        
2800	        if not seed_features:
2801	            return []
2802	            
2803	        # Find similar tracks based on audio features
2804	        similar_tracks = execute_query_dict(
2805	            """
2806	            SELECT t.*, af.*
2807	            FROM tracks t
2808	            JOIN audio_features af ON t.id = af.track_id
2809	            WHERE t.id != %s
2810	            ORDER BY 
2811	                POWER(af.energy - %s, 2) +
2812	                POWER(af.danceability - %s, 2) +
2813	                POWER(af.valence - %s, 2) +
2814	                POWER(af.acousticness - %s, 2)
2815	            LIMIT %s
2816	            """,
2817	            (
2818	                seed_track_id, 
2819	                seed_features.get('energy', 0),
2820	                seed_features.get('danceability', 0),
2821	                seed_features.get('valence', 0),
2822	                seed_features.get('acousticness', 0),
2823	                limit
2824	            )
2825	        )
2826	        
2827	        return similar_tracks
2828	    except Exception as e:
2829	        logger.error(f"Error creating similar playlist: {e}")
2830	        return []

2894	def run_startup_actions():
2895	    """Run configured startup actions when the app starts"""
2896	    if not ensure_single_instance('startup_actions'):
2897	        logger.warning("Another startup process is running, skipping")
2898	        return
2899	        
2900	    # Prevent duplicate startup actions
2901	    if is_analysis_running() or QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['running'] or METADATA_UPDATE_STATUS['running']:
2902	        logger.warning("Background tasks already running, skipping startup actions")
2903	        return
2904	        
2905	    action = config.get('scheduler', 'startup_action', fallback='nothing')
2906	    
2907	    if (action == 'nothing'):
2908	        logger.info("No startup actions configured")
2909	        return
2910	    
2911	    logger.info(f"Running startup action: {action}")
2912	    
2913	    # Add a short delay to ensure UI has loaded before starting tasks
2914	    def run_actions():
2915	        try:
2916	            # Give UI time to initialize
2917	            time.sleep(2)
2918	            
2919	            if action == 'quick_scan':
2920	                logger.info("Starting quick scan as startup action")
2921	                run_quick_scan_task()
2922	            elif action == 'quick_scan_metadata':
2923	                logger.info("Starting quick scan and metadata update as startup action")
2924	                run_quick_scan_task()
2925	                # Only start metadata update after quick scan completes
2926	                while QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['running']:
2927	                    time.sleep(1)
2928	                run_metadata_update_task()
2929	            elif action == 'full_analysis':
2930	                logger.info("Starting full analysis workflow as startup action")
2931	                run_quick_scan_task()
2932	                # Wait for quick scan to complete
2933	                while QUICK_SCAN_STATUS['running']:
2934	                    time.sleep(1)
2935	                
2936	                # Start both metadata update and analysis concurrently
2937	                logger.info("Starting both metadata update and analysis concurrently")
2938	                metadata_thread = threading.Thread(target=run_metadata_update_task)
2939	                metadata_thread.daemon = True
2940	                metadata_thread.start()
2941	                
2942	                # Start analysis without waiting for metadata to complete
2943	                time.sleep(1)  # Small delay to let metadata initialize
2944	                run_full_analysis_task()
2945	            
2946	            logger.info("Startup actions initiated")
2947	        except Exception as e:
2948	            logger.error(f"Error running startup actions: {e}")
2949	    
2950	    thread = threading.Thread(target=run_actions)
2951	    thread.daemon = True
2952	    thread.start()
2953	
2954	# Add this API endpoint for next run time
2955	@app.route('/api/next-scheduled-run')
2956	def get_next_scheduled_run():
2957	    """Get the next scheduled run time"""
2958	    next_run = calculate_next_run_time()
2959	    return jsonify({'next_run': next_run})

2961	def setup_liked_tracks_column():
2962	    """Add a 'liked' column to the tracks table if it doesn't exist"""
2963	    try:
2964	        logger.info("Adding 'liked' column to tracks table")
2965	        # Use direct execute_write function instead of connection/DB_PATH
2966	        execute_write(
2967	            "ALTER TABLE tracks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS liked BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE"
2968	        )
2969	        return True
2970	    except Exception as e:
2971	        logger.error(f"Error setting up liked tracks column: {e}")
2972	        return False

2976	    setup_liked_tracks_column()

2978	# Add these routes for liked tracks functionality
2979	
2980	@app.route('/api/liked-tracks')
2981	def get_liked_tracks():
2982	    """Get all liked tracks"""
2983	    try:
2984	        # Use PostgreSQL-compatible query
2985	        liked_tracks = execute_query_dict(
2986	            """SELECT id, file_path, title, artist, album, album_art_url, duration, liked 
2987	               FROM tracks 
2988	               WHERE liked = TRUE 
2989	               ORDER BY title"""
2990	        )
2991	        
2992	        # Set default titles
2993	        for track in liked_tracks:
2994	            if not track['title']:
2995	                track['title'] = os.path.basename(track['file_path'])
2996	                
2997	        return jsonify(liked_tracks)
2998	    except Exception as e:

3083	def create_indexes():
3084	    """Create database indexes for better performance"""
3085	    try:
3086	        logger.info("Creating database indexes")
3087	        
3088	        # Use transaction_context from db_operations
3089	        conn = get_connection()
3090	        try:
3091	            with conn.cursor() as cursor:
3092	                # Create indexes for tracks table
3093	                cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tracks_artist ON tracks(artist)")
3094	                cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tracks_album ON tracks(album)")
3095	                cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tracks_title ON tracks(title)")
3096	                cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tracks_date_added ON tracks(date_added)")
3097	                
3098	                # First check if playlist_items table exists
3099	                cursor.execute("""
3100	                    SELECT EXISTS (
3101	                        SELECT FROM information_schema.tables 
3102	                        WHERE table_name = 'playlist_items'
3103	                    )
3104	                """)
3105	                table_exists = cursor.fetchone()[0]
3106	                
3107	                # Only create indexes if the table exists
3108	                if table_exists:
3109	                    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_playlist_items_playlist_id ON playlist_items(playlist_id)")
3110	                    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_playlist_items_track_id ON playlist_items(track_id)")
3111	                    
3112	            conn.commit()
3113	            logger.info("Database indexes created successfully")
3114	        except Exception as e:
3115	            conn.rollback()
3116	            raise e
3117	        finally:
3118	            release_connection(conn)
3119	        return True
3120	    except Exception as e:
3121	        logger.error(f"Error creating database indexes: {e}")
3122	        return False
3123

3125	# Add this near line 270-300 where you initialize other components
3126	create_indexes()

3170	def print_database_schema():
3171	    """
3172	    Print the database schema to the logs at startup
3173	    This helps with troubleshooting column name and table structure issues
3174	    """
3175	    logger.info("Printing database schema for troubleshooting")
3176	    
3177	    try:
3178	        conn = get_connection()
3179	        try:
3180	            with conn.cursor() as cursor:
3181	                # Get list of tables
3182	                cursor.execute("""
3183	                    SELECT table_name
3184	                    FROM information_schema.tables
3185	                    WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_type='BASE TABLE'
3186	                    ORDER BY table_name
3187	                """)
3188	                
3189	                tables = [row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()]
3190	                logger.info(f"Database tables: {tables}")
3191	                
3192	                # For each table, get column information
3193	                for table in tables:
3194	                    cursor.execute(f"""
3195	                        SELECT column_name, data_type, is_nullable
3196	                        FROM information_schema.columns
3197	                        WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_name='{table}'
3198	                        ORDER BY ordinal_position
3199	                    """)
3200	                    
3201	                    columns = cursor.fetchall()
3202	                    logger.info(f"Table '{table}' structure:")
3203	                    for col in columns:
3204	                        col_name, col_type, nullable = col
3205	                        logger.info(f"  - {col_name} ({col_type}, {'NULL' if nullable == 'YES' else 'NOT NULL'})")
3206	        finally:
3207	            release_connection(conn)
3208	            
3209	        return True
3210	    except Exception as e:
3211	        logger.error(f"Error printing database schema: {e}")
3212	        return False
3213	
3214	# Call the function early in the initialization to help with troubleshooting
3215	print_database_schema()

3217	@app.route('/albumart/<path:image_url>')
3218	def album_art_proxy(image_url):
3219	    """
3220	    Proxy for album art URLs. This handles album art URLs that are external links
3221	    by either redirecting to them or downloading and serving them.
3222	    """
3223	    try:
3224	        logger.debug(f"Album art request for: {image_url}")
3225	        
3226	        # Check if URL is properly formatted
3227	        if not image_url.startswith('http'):
3228	            # If it doesn't start with http, it might be URL encoded
3229	            try:
3230	                from urllib.parse import unquote
3231	                decoded_url = unquote(image_url)
3232	                if decoded_url.startswith('http'):
3233	                    image_url = decoded_url
3234	            except Exception as e:
3235	                logger.error(f"Error decoding URL: {e}")
3236	                
3237	        # For LastFM and other trusted sources, just redirect
3238	        if 'lastfm' in image_url or 'spotify' in image_url:
3239	            logger.debug(f"Redirecting to external album art: {image_url}")
3240	            return redirect(image_url)
3241	        
3242	        # For other sources, we might want to download and cache
3243	        # But for now, just redirect to everything
3244	        return redirect(image_url)
3245	        
3246	    except Exception as e:
3247	        logger.error(f"Error handling album art proxy request: {e}")
3248	        # Return default album art
3249	        return send_file('static/images/default-album-art.png', mimetype='image/jpeg')

3251	@app.route('/api/db-diagnostic')
3252	def db_diagnostic():
3253	    """Return database diagnostics for PostgreSQL"""
3254	    try:
3255	        conn = get_connection()
3256	        cursor = conn.cursor()
3257	        
3258	        # Get PostgreSQL version
3259	        cursor.execute("SELECT version();")
3260	        version = cursor.fetchone()[0]
3261	        
3262	        # Get database size
3263	        cursor.execute("SELECT pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size(current_database())) as size")
3264	        size_info = cursor.fetchone()
3265	        
3266	        # Get table counts 
3267	        tables_query = """
3268	            SELECT table_name, 
3269	                   (SELECT count(*) FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name=t.table_name) as column_count
3270	            FROM information_schema.tables t
3271	            WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_type='BASE TABLE'
3272	            ORDER BY table_name;
3273	        """
3274	        cursor.execute(tables_query)
3275	        table_info = cursor.fetchall()
3276	        
3277	        # Build table structure
3278	        tables = []
3279	        counts = {}
3280	        
3281	        for table in table_info:
3282	            table_name = table[0]
3283	            column_count = table[1]
3284	            
3285	            # Count rows in this table
3286	            cursor.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table_name}")
3287	            row_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]
3288	            
3289	            tables.append({
3290	                'name': table_name,
3291	                'columns': column_count,
3292	                'rows': row_count
3293	            })
3294	            
3295	            counts[table_name] = row_count
3296	        
3297	        release_connection(conn)
3298	        
3299	        return jsonify({
3300	            'postgres_version': version,
3301	            'database': {
3302	                'name': conn.info.dbname,
3303	                'size_pretty': size_info[0] if size_info else '0 KB'
3304	            },
3305	            'tables': tables,
3306	            'counts': counts,
3307	            'environment': {
3308	                'python_version': sys.version,
3309	                'working_directory': os.getcwd()
3310	            }
3311	        })
3312	    except Exception as e:
3313	        logger.error(f"Error in db diagnostic: {e}")
3314	        return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500

3361	@app.route('/api/discover')
3362	def discover_tracks():
3363	    """Return a selection of tracks for the Discover view"""
3364	    try:
3365	        logger.info("API: Discover tracks requested")
3366	        
3367	        # Initialize an empty list for each category
3368	        dance_tracks = []
3369	        recent_tracks = []
3370	        random_tracks = []
3371	        
3372	        # Try to get some tracks with high danceability if audio features exist
3373	        try:
3374	            dance_tracks = execute_query_dict(
3375	                """
3376	                SELECT t.* FROM tracks t
3377	                JOIN audio_features af ON t.id = af.track_id
3378	                WHERE af.danceability > 0.7
3379	                ORDER BY RANDOM()
3380	                LIMIT 5
3381	                """
3382	            ) or []
3383	            logger.debug(f"Retrieved {len(dance_tracks)} dance tracks")
3384	        except Exception as dance_error:
3385	            logger.warning(f"Failed to get dance tracks: {dance_error}")
3386	            # Continue execution even if this query fails
3387	        
3388	        # Get some recently added tracks
3389	        try:
3390	            recent_tracks = execute_query_dict(
3391	                """
3392	                SELECT * FROM tracks
3393	                ORDER BY date_added DESC
3394	                LIMIT 10
3395	                """
3396	            ) or []
3397	            logger.debug(f"Retrieved {len(recent_tracks)} recent tracks")
3398	        except Exception as recent_error:
3399	            logger.warning(f"Failed to get recent tracks: {recent_error}")
3400	        
3401	        # Get a few random tracks for variety
3402	        try:
3403	            random_tracks = execute_query_dict(
3404	                """
3405	                SELECT * FROM tracks
3406	                ORDER BY RANDOM()
3407	                LIMIT 15
3408	                """
3409	            ) or []
3410	            logger.debug(f"Retrieved {len(random_tracks)} random tracks")
3411	        except Exception as random_error:
3412	            logger.warning(f"Failed to get random tracks: {random_error}")
3413	        
3414	        # Combine all tracks and remove duplicates by ID
3415	        all_tracks = {}
3416	        for track in dance_tracks + recent_tracks + random_tracks:
3417	            if track and 'id' in track and track['id'] not in all_tracks:
3418	                # Ensure all tracks have the expected fields
3419	                processed_track = {
3420	                    'id': track.get('id'),
3421	                    'title': track.get('title', 'Unknown Title'),
3422	                    'artist': track.get('artist', 'Unknown Artist'),
3423	                    'album': track.get('album', 'Unknown Album'),
3424	                    'album_art_url': track.get('album_art_url'),
3425	                    'file_path': track.get('file_path', ''),
3426	                    'duration': track.get('duration', 0),
3427	                    'liked': bool(track.get('liked', False))
3428	                }
3429	                all_tracks[track['id']] = processed_track
3430	        
3431	        discover_tracks = list(all_tracks.values())
3432	        
3433	        logger.info(f"API: Returning {len(discover_tracks)} tracks for discover view")
3434	        return jsonify(discover_tracks)
3435	    except Exception as e:
3436	        logger.error(f"API: Error in discover tracks: {e}", exc_info=True)
3437	        return jsonify([])  # Return empty array instead of error for better UI handling

3465	# Updated run_server function that initializes scheduler and runs startup actions
3466	def run_server():
3467	    """Run the Flask server"""
3468	    logger.info(f"Starting server on {HOST}:{PORT} (debug={DEBUG})")
3469	    
3470	    # Initialize the scheduler
3471	    update_scheduler()
3472	    
3473	    # Run startup actions
3474	    run_startup_actions()
3475	    
3476	    try:
3477	        # Use Werkzeug's run_simple for better error handling
3478	        run_simple(hostname=HOST, port=PORT, application=app, use_reloader=DEBUG, use_debugger=DEBUG)
3479	    except Exception as e:
3480	        logger.error(f"Error running server: {e}")
3481	
3482	
3483	if __name__ == '__main__':
3484	    run_server()

0001	// PUMP Music Player - Main Application JavaScript
0002	
0003	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0004	    console.log('PUMP Music Player application initialized');
0005	
0006	    // Create and initialize the main player manager
0007	    window.playerManager = new PlayerManager();
0008	
0009	    // Initialize page-specific functionality based on the current page
0010	    initializeCurrentPage();
0011	});
0012	
0013	/**
0014	 * Player Manager - Handles all audio playback functionality
0015	 */
0016	class PlayerManager {
0017	    constructor() {
0018	        this.audioPlayer = document.getElementById('audio-player');
0019	        this.nowPlayingBar = document.getElementById('now-playing-bar');
0020	        this.nowPlayingArt = document.getElementById('now-playing-art');
0021	        this.nowPlayingTitle = document.getElementById('now-playing-title');
0022	        this.nowPlayingArtist = document.getElementById('now-playing-artist');
0023	        this.playPauseButton = document.getElementById('play-pause');
0024	        this.progressFill = document.getElementById('progress-fill');
0025	        this.currentTimeDisplay = document.getElementById('current-time');
0026	        this.totalTimeDisplay = document.getElementById('total-time');
0027	        this.prevButton = document.getElementById('prev-track');
0028	        this.nextButton = document.getElementById('next-track');
0029	        
0030	        // Queue management
0031	        this.queue = [];
0032	        this.history = [];
0033	        this.currentTrackIndex = -1;
0034	        this.currentTrackId = null;
0035	        this.isPlaying = false;
0036	
0037	        // Initialize
0038	        this.initialize();
0039	    }
0040	
0041	    initialize() {
0042	        console.log('Initializing player manager');
0043	        
0044	        // Set up event listeners
0045	        if (this.audioPlayer) {
0046	            this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('ended', () => this.onTrackEnded());
0047	            this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('timeupdate', () => this.updateProgress());
0048	            this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('play', () => this.onPlay());
0049	            this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('pause', () => this.onPause());
0050	            this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('error', (e) => this.onError(e));
0051	            
0052	            // Set initial volume
0053	            this.audioPlayer.volume = 0.7;
0054	        } else {
0055	            console.error('Audio player element not found');
0056	        }
0057	        
0058	        // Set up control button listeners
0059	        if (this.playPauseButton) {
0060	            this.playPauseButton.addEventListener('click', () => this.togglePlayPause());
0061	        }
0062	        
0063	        if (this.prevButton) {
0064	            this.prevButton.addEventListener('click', () => this.playPrevious());
0065	        }
0066	        
0067	        if (this.nextButton) {
0068	            this.nextButton.addEventListener('click', () => this.playNext());
0069	        }
0070	        
0071	        // Try to load most recent track if available
0072	        this.loadRecentTrack();
0073	    }
0074	    
0075	    /**
0076	     * Play a specific track by file path
0077	     * @param {string} filePath - Path to the audio file
0078	     * @param {boolean} resetQueue - Whether to reset the queue or add to it
0079	     */
0080	    playTrack(filePath, resetQueue = true) {
0081	        console.log(`Playing track: ${filePath}`);
0082	        
0083	        if (!filePath) {
0084	            console.error('No file path provided');
0085	            return;
0086	        }
0087	        
0088	        // If this is a new session, reset the queue
0089	        if (resetQueue || this.queue.length === 0) {
0090	            this.queue = [filePath];
0091	            this.currentTrackIndex = 0;
0092	        } else {
0093	            // Add to queue and play immediately
0094	            this.queue.push(filePath);
0095	            this.currentTrackIndex = this.queue.length - 1;
0096	        }
0097	        
0098	        // Get track metadata
0099	        fetch(`/api/tracks/info?path=${encodeURIComponent(filePath)}`)
0100	            .then(response => response.json())
0101	            .then(data => {
0102	                if (data.error) {
0103	                    console.error('Error fetching track info:', data.error);
0104	                    return;
0105	                }
0106	                
0107	                // Update player UI
0108	                this.updateNowPlayingDisplay(data);
0109	                
0110	                // Save track ID for later
0111	                this.currentTrackId = data.id || null;
0112	                
0113	                // Add track to recent history if it has an ID
0114	                if (data.id) {
0115	                    this.addToHistory(data.id);
0116	                }
0117	                
0118	                // Play the audio
0119	                this.audioPlayer.src = `/api/stream?path=${encodeURIComponent(filePath)}`;
0120	                this.audioPlayer.play()
0121	                    .catch(error => console.error('Error playing audio:', error));
0122	                
0123	                // Show now playing bar
0124	                if (this.nowPlayingBar) {
0125	                    this.nowPlayingBar.classList.add('active');
0126	                    this.nowPlayingBar.classList.remove('empty');
0127	                }
0128	                
0129	                // Update play button state
0130	                if (this.playPauseButton) {
0131	                    this.playPauseButton.textContent = '❚❚';
0132	                }
0133	                
0134	                // Update isPlaying state
0135	                this.isPlaying = true;
0136	            })
0137	            .catch(error => {
0138	                console.error('Error:', error);
0139	                
0140	                // Fallback to play without metadata
0141	                this.audioPlayer.src = `/api/stream?path=${encodeURIComponent(filePath)}`;
0142	                this.audioPlayer.play()
0143	                    .catch(err => console.error('Error playing audio:', err));
0144	            });
0145	    }
0146	    
0147	    /**
0148	     * Play a specific track by ID
0149	     * @param {number} trackId - ID of the track to play
0150	     */
0151	    playTrackById(trackId) {
0152	        console.log(`Global playTrack called with ID: ${trackId}`);
0153	
0154	        // Update the stream URL to use the correct endpoint
0155	        const streamUrl = `/stream/${trackId}`;
0156	        console.log(`Using stream URL: ${streamUrl}`);
0157	
0158	        // Set the audio source and play
0159	        this.audioPlayer.src = streamUrl;
0160	        this.audioPlayer.play().catch(error => {
0161	            console.error('Error playing track:', error);
0162	        });
0163	
0164	        // Check like status using the correct endpoint
0165	        this.checkTrackLikeStatus(trackId);
0166	    }
0167	    
0168	    /**
0169	     * Toggle between play and pause
0170	     */
0171	    togglePlayPause() {
0172	        if (!this.audioPlayer) return;
0173	        
0174	        if (this.audioPlayer.paused) {
0175	            if (this.audioPlayer.src) {
0176	                this.audioPlayer.play();
0177	            } else if (this.queue.length > 0 && this.currentTrackIndex >= 0) {
0178	                this.playTrack(this.queue[this.currentTrackIndex], false);
0179	            } else {
0180	                this.loadRecentTrack();
0181	            }
0182	        } else {
0183	            this.audioPlayer.pause();
0184	        }
0185	    }
0186	    
0187	    /**
0188	     * Play the next track in the queue
0189	     */
0190	    playNext() {
0191	        if (this.queue.length === 0) {
0192	            console.log('Queue is empty');
0193	            return;
0194	        }
0195	        
0196	        if (this.currentTrackIndex < this.queue.length - 1) {
0197	            this.currentTrackIndex++;
0198	            this.playTrack(this.queue[this.currentTrackIndex], false);
0199	        } else {
0200	            console.log('Reached end of queue');
0201	            // Optional: loop back to beginning
0202	            // this.currentTrackIndex = 0;
0203	            // this.playTrack(this.queue[this.currentTrackIndex], false);
0204	        }
0205	    }
0206	    
0207	    /**
0208	     * Play the previous track in the queue
0209	     */
0210	    playPrevious() {
0211	        if (this.queue.length === 0) {
0212	            console.log('Queue is empty');
0213	            return;
0214	        }
0215	        
0216	        // If we're more than 3 seconds into the track, restart it instead of going back
0217	        if (this.audioPlayer.currentTime > 3) {
0218	            this.audioPlayer.currentTime = 0;
0219	            return;
0220	        }
0221	        
0222	        if (this.currentTrackIndex > 0) {
0223	            this.currentTrackIndex--;
0224	            this.playTrack(this.queue[this.currentTrackIndex], false);
0225	        } else {
0226	            console.log('Already at beginning of queue');
0227	            // Optional: loop to end
0228	            // this.currentTrackIndex = this.queue.length - 1;
0229	            // this.playTrack(this.queue[this.currentTrackIndex], false);
0230	            
0231	            // Or just restart current track
0232	            this.audioPlayer.currentTime = 0;
0233	        }
0234	    }
0235	    
0236	    /**
0237	     * Update the progress bar and time displays
0238	     */
0239	    updateProgress() {
0240	        if (!this.audioPlayer || !this.progressFill || !this.currentTimeDisplay || !this.totalTimeDisplay) return;
0241	        
0242	        const currentTime = this.audioPlayer.currentTime;
0243	        const duration = this.audioPlayer.duration;
0244	        
0245	        if (duration > 0) {
0246	            // Update progress bar
0247	            const progressPercent = (currentTime / duration) * 100;
0248	            this.progressFill.style.width = `${progressPercent}%`;
0249	            
0250	            // Update time displays
0251	            this.currentTimeDisplay.textContent = this.formatTime(currentTime);
0252	            this.totalTimeDisplay.textContent = this.formatTime(duration);
0253	        }
0254	    }
0255	    
0256	    /**
0257	     * Format seconds into MM:SS display
0258	     * @param {number} time - Time in seconds
0259	     * @returns {string} Formatted time
0260	     */
0261	    formatTime(time) {
0262	        if (isNaN(time)) return '0:00';
0263	        
0264	        const minutes = Math.floor(time / 60);
0265	        const seconds = Math.floor(time % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
0266	        return `${minutes}:${seconds}`;
0267	    }
0268	    
0269	    /**
0270	     * Update now playing display with track metadata
0271	     * @param {object} trackData - Track metadata
0272	     */
0273	    updateNowPlayingDisplay(trackData) {
0274	        if (!this.nowPlayingTitle || !this.nowPlayingArtist || !this.nowPlayingArt) return;
0275	        
0276	        // Update track info
0277	        this.nowPlayingTitle.textContent = trackData.title || 'Unknown Track';
0278	        this.nowPlayingArtist.textContent = trackData.artist || 'Unknown Artist';
0279	        
0280	        // Update album art if available
0281	        if (trackData.album_art_url) {
0282	            this.nowPlayingArt.src = trackData.album_art_url;
0283	        } else {
0284	            this.nowPlayingArt.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png';
0285	        }
0286	    }
0287	    
0288	    /**
0289	     * Handle track ended event
0290	     */
0291	    onTrackEnded() {
0292	        this.playNext();
0293	    }
0294	    
0295	    /**
0296	     * Handle audio play event
0297	     */
0298	    onPlay() {
0299	        if (this.playPauseButton) {
0300	            this.playPauseButton.textContent = '❚❚';
0301	        }
0302	        this.isPlaying = true;
0303	    }
0304	    
0305	    /**
0306	     * Handle audio pause event
0307	     */
0308	    onPause() {
0309	        if (this.playPauseButton) {
0310	            this.playPauseButton.textContent = '▶';
0311	        }
0312	        this.isPlaying = false;
0313	    }
0314	    
0315	    /**
0316	     * Handle audio error event
0317	     */
0318	    onError(error) {
0319	        console.error('Audio player error:', error);
0320	        if (this.nowPlayingTitle) {
0321	            this.nowPlayingTitle.textContent = 'Error playing track';
0322	        }
0323	        if (this.nowPlayingArtist) {
0324	            this.nowPlayingArtist.textContent = 'Please try another track';
0325	        }
0326	    }
0327	    
0328	    /**
0329	     * Add a track to play history
0330	     * @param {number|string} trackId - Track ID
0331	     */
0332	    addToHistory(trackId) {
0333	        // Don't add if it's already the most recent in history
0334	        if (this.history.length > 0 && this.history[this.history.length - 1] === trackId) {
0335	            return;
0336	        }
0337	        
0338	        this.history.push(trackId);
0339	        
0340	        // Keep history at a reasonable size
0341	        if (this.history.length > 100) {
0342	            this.history.shift();
0343	        }
0344	        
0345	        // Save to localStorage
0346	        localStorage.setItem('recentTrackId', trackId);
0347	    }
0348	    
0349	    /**
0350	     * Load the most recently played track
0351	     */
0352	    loadRecentTrack() {
0353	        const recentId = localStorage.getItem('recentTrackId');
0354	        if (recentId) {
0355	            fetch(`/api/tracks/${recentId}`)
0356	                .then(response => response.json())
0357	                .then(data => {
0358	                    if (data && data.file_path) {
0359	                        console.log('Loading recent track:', data.title);
0360	                        
0361	                        // Add to queue without playing
0362	                        this.queue = [data.file_path];
0363	                        this.currentTrackIndex = 0;
0364	                        
0365	                        // Update now playing display
0366	                        this.updateNowPlayingDisplay(data);
0367	                        
0368	                        // Show now playing bar but don't play automatically
0369	                        if (this.nowPlayingBar) {
0370	                            this.nowPlayingBar.classList.add('active');
0371	                            this.nowPlayingBar.classList.remove('empty');
0372	                        }
0373	                        
0374	                        // Save current track ID
0375	                        this.currentTrackId = data.id;
0376	                    }
0377	                })
0378	                .catch(error => {
0379	                    console.error('Error loading recent track:', error);
0380	                });
0381	        }
0382	    }
0383	    
0384	    /**
0385	     * Add tracks to the queue
0386	     * @param {Array} tracks - Array of track objects with file_path property
0387	     * @param {boolean} playNow - Whether to play the first track immediately
0388	     */
0389	    addToQueue(tracks, playNow = false) {
0390	        if (!tracks || tracks.length === 0) return;
0391	        
0392	        // Get just the file paths
0393	        const paths = tracks.map(t => t.file_path);
0394	        
0395	        // Add to queue
0396	        this.queue = [...this.queue, ...paths];
0397	        
0398	        if (playNow) {
0399	            this.currentTrackIndex = this.queue.length - paths.length;
0400	            this.playTrack(this.queue[this.currentTrackIndex], false);
0401	        }
0402	    }
0403	    
0404	    /**
0405	     * Replace the current queue with new tracks
0406	     * @param {Array} tracks - Array of track objects with file_path property
0407	     * @param {boolean} playNow - Whether to play the first track immediately
0408	     */
0409	    replaceQueue(tracks, playNow = true) {
0410	        if (!tracks || tracks.length === 0) return;
0411	        
0412	        // Get just the file paths
0413	        const paths = tracks.map(t => t.file_path);
0414	        
0415	        // Replace queue
0416	        this.queue = [...paths];
0417	        
0418	        if (playNow) {
0419	            this.currentTrackIndex = 0;
0420	            this.playTrack(this.queue[this.currentTrackIndex], false);
0421	        } else {
0422	            this.currentTrackIndex = 0;
0423	        }
0424	    }
0425	    
0426	    /**
0427	     * Clear the queue
0428	     */
0429	    clearQueue() {
0430	        this.queue = [];
0431	        this.currentTrackIndex = -1;
0432	    }
0433	    
0434	    /**
0435	     * Get the current queue
0436	     * @returns {Array} Array of track file paths
0437	     */
0438	    getQueue() {
0439	        return this.queue;
0440	    }
0441	    
0442	    /**
0443	     * Get current state of the player
0444	     * @returns {Object} Player state
0445	     */
0446	    getState() {
0447	        return {
0448	            isPlaying: this.isPlaying,
0449	            currentTrackId: this.currentTrackId,
0450	            currentTrackIndex: this.currentTrackIndex,
0451	            queueLength: this.queue.length,
0452	            currentTime: this.audioPlayer ? this.audioPlayer.currentTime : 0,
0453	            duration: this.audioPlayer ? this.audioPlayer.duration : 0
0454	        };
0455	    }
0456	}
