0477	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0478	  // Cache DOM elements
0479	  const searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input');
0480	  const searchButton = document.getElementById('search-button');
0481	  const exploreLink = document.getElementById('explore-link');
0482	  const searchResultsList = document.getElementById('search-results-list');
0483	  const exploreList = document.getElementById('explore-list');
0484	  const playlistTracks = document.getElementById('playlist-tracks');
0485	  const numTracksSelect = document.getElementById('num-tracks');
0486	  const regenerateButton = document.getElementById('regenerate-playlist');
0487	  const playlistTitle = document.getElementById('playlist-title');
0488	  
0489	  // Sections
0490	  const welcomeSection = document.getElementById('welcome-section');
0491	  const searchResultsSection = document.getElementById('search-results');
0492	  const exploreSection = document.getElementById('explore-section');
0493	  const playlistSection = document.getElementById('playlist-section');
0494	  
0495	  // Track player elements
0496	  const trackName = document.querySelector('.track-name');
0497	  const trackArtist = document.querySelector('.track-artist');
0498	  
0499	  // Current state
0500	  let currentSeedTrackId = null;
0501	  let currentPlaylistTracks = [];
0502	  
0503	  // Search functionality
0504	  searchButton.addEventListener('click', performSearch);
0505	  searchInput.addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
0506	    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
0507	      performSearch();
0508	    }
0509	  });
0510	  
0511	  function performSearch() {
0512	    const query = searchInput.value.trim();
0513	    if (query) {
0514	      fetch(`/search?query=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`)
0515	        .then(response => response.json())
0516	        .then(tracks => {
0517	          displaySearchResults(tracks);
0518	        })
0519	        .catch(error => console.error('Error searching tracks:', error));
0520	    }
0521	  }
0522	  
0523	  function displaySearchResults(tracks) {
0524	    searchResultsList.innerHTML = '';
0525	    
0526	    if (tracks.length === 0) {
0527	      searchResultsList.innerHTML = '<p>No tracks found matching your search.</p>';
0528	    } else {
0529	      tracks.forEach(track => {
0530	        const trackCard = createTrackCard(track);
0531	        searchResultsList.appendChild(trackCard);
0532	      });
0533	    }
0534	    
0535	    // Show search results section, hide others
0536	    showSection(searchResultsSection);
0537	  }
0538	  
0539	  // Explore functionality
0540	  exploreLink.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
0541	    e.preventDefault();
0542	    fetchExplore();
0543	  });
0544	  
0545	  function fetchExplore() {
0546	    fetch('/explore')
0547	      .then(response => response.json())
0548	      .then(tracks => {
0549	        displayExplore(tracks);
0550	      })
0551	      .catch(error => console.error('Error getting explore tracks:', error));
0552	  }
0553	  
0554	  function displayExplore(tracks) {
0555	    exploreList.innerHTML = '';
0556	    
0557	    if (tracks.length === 0) {
0558	      exploreList.innerHTML = '<p>No tracks available for exploration. Try adding some music first!</p>';
0559	    } else {
0560	      tracks.forEach(track => {
0561	        const trackCard = createTrackCard(track);
0562	        exploreList.appendChild(trackCard);
0563	      });
0564	    }
0565	    
0566	    // Show explore section, hide others
0567	    showSection(exploreSection);
0568	  }
0569	  
0570	  // Generate playlist functionality
0571	  function generatePlaylist(seedTrackId, numTracks) {
0572	    currentSeedTrackId = seedTrackId;
0573	    
0574	    fetch(`/playlist?seed_track_id=${seedTrackId}&num_tracks=${numTracks}`)
0575	      .then(response => response.json())
0576	      .then(playlist => {
0577	        displayPlaylist(playlist);
0578	      })
0579	      .catch(error => console.error('Error generating playlist:', error));
0580	  }
0581	  
0582	  function displayPlaylist(playlist) {
0583	    currentPlaylistTracks = playlist;
0584	    playlistTracks.innerHTML = '';
0585	    
0586	    if (playlist.length === 0 || playlist.error) {
0587	      playlistTracks.innerHTML = '<p>Error generating playlist. Please try another track.</p>';
0588	    } else {
0589	      // Update playlist title with seed track
0590	      const seedTrack = playlist[0];
0591	      playlistTitle.textContent = `Playlist based on "${seedTrack.title || 'Unknown'}"`;
0592	      
0593	      // Display each track
0594	      playlist.forEach((track, index) => {
0595	        const trackItem = document.createElement('div');
0596	        trackItem.className = 'track-card';
0597	        trackItem.innerHTML = `
0598	          <div class="album-art">
0599	            <i class="fas fa-music"></i>
0600	            <div class="play-overlay">
0601	              <i class="fas fa-play"></i>
0602	            </div>
0603	          </div>
0604	          <div class="track-title">${index + 1}. ${track.title || 'Unknown'}</div>
0605	          <div class="track-artist">${track.artist || 'Unknown Artist'}</div>
0606	          <div class="track-album">${track.album || 'Unknown Album'}</div>
0607	        `;
0608	        
0609	        // When clicking a track in the playlist
0610	        trackItem.addEventListener('click', function() {
0611	          // In a real app, this would play the track
0612	          trackName.textContent = track.title || 'Unknown';
0613	          trackArtist.textContent = track.artist || 'Unknown Artist';
0614	        });
0615	        
0616	        playlistTracks.appendChild(trackItem);
0617	      });
0618	    }
0619	    
0620	    // Show playlist section, hide others
0621	    showSection(playlistSection);
0622	  }
0623	  
0624	  // Regenerate the playlist with potentially different number of tracks
0625	  regenerateButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
0626	    if (currentSeedTrackId) {
0627	      const numTracks = parseInt(numTracksSelect.value);
0628	      generatePlaylist(currentSeedTrackId, numTracks);
0629	    }
0630	  });
0631	  
0632	  // Helper function to create a track card
0633	  function createTrackCard(track) {
0634	    const trackCard = document.createElement('div');
0635	    trackCard.className = 'track-card';
0636	    trackCard.innerHTML = `
0637	      <div class="album-art">
0638	        <i class="fas fa-music"></i>
0639	        <div class="play-overlay">
0640	          <i class="fas fa-play"></i>
0641	        </div>
0642	      </div>
0643	      <div class="track-title">${track.title || 'Unknown'}</div>
0644	      <div class="track-artist">${track.artist || 'Unknown Artist'}</div>
0645	      <div class="track-album">${track.album || 'Unknown Album'}</div>
0646	    `;
0647	    
0648	    // When clicking a track
0649	    trackCard.addEventListener('click', function() {
0650	      // Generate playlist with this track as seed
0651	      const numTracks = parseInt(numTracksSelect.value);
0652	      generatePlaylist(track.id, numTracks);
0653	      
0654	      // Update player bar info
0655	      trackName.textContent = track.title || 'Unknown';
0656	      trackArtist.textContent = track.artist || 'Unknown Artist';
0657	    });
0658	    
0659	    return trackCard;
0660	  }
0661	  
0662	  // Helper function to show a section and hide others
0663	  function showSection(sectionToShow) {
0664	    // Hide all sections
0665	    welcomeSection.style.display = 'none';
0666	    searchResultsSection.style.display = 'none';
0667	    exploreSection.style.display = 'none';
0668	    playlistSection.style.display = 'none';
0669	    
0670	    // Show the requested section
0671	    sectionToShow.style.display = 'block';
0672	  }
0673	  
0674	  // Load explore tracks on initial load
0675	  fetchExplore();
0676	});

--- File: static/js/app.js ---
0001	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0002	    // Library link
0003	    const libraryLink = document.getElementById('library-link');
0004	    if (libraryLink) {
0005	        libraryLink.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
0006	            e.preventDefault();
0007	            window.location.href = '/library';
0008	        });
0009	    }
0010	
0011	    // Global status checking
0012	    window.checkGlobalAnalysisStatus = function() {
0013	        const statusIndicator = document.getElementById('global-analysis-status');
0014	        
0015	        if (!statusIndicator) return null;
0016	        
0017	        let lastMetadataStatus = null;
0018	        let lastAnalysisStatus = null;
0019	        let lastQuickScanStatus = null;
0020	        let pollInterval = 3000; // Start with 3 seconds between polls
0021	        let consecutiveErrors = 0;
0022	        let timer = null;
0023	        let isNavigating = false;
0024	        
0025	        // Function to pause polling during navigation
0026	        window.pauseStatusPolling = function() {
0027	            isNavigating = true;
0028	            console.log('Status polling paused for navigation');
0029	            if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
0030	        };
0031	        
0032	        // Function to resume polling after navigation
0033	        window.resumeStatusPolling = function() {
0034	            isNavigating = false;
0035	            console.log('Status polling resumed');
0036	            checkStatus();
0037	        };
0038	        
0039	        function checkStatus() {
0040	            // Skip checking if we're in the middle of navigation
0041	            if (isNavigating) {
0042	                scheduleNextPoll();
0043	                return;
0044	            }
0045	            
0046	            // Use Promise.all to make parallel requests for better performance
0047	            Promise.all([
0048	                fetch('/api/analysis/status').then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({})),
0049	                fetch('/api/metadata-update/status').then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({})),
0050	                fetch('/api/quick-scan/status').then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({}))
0051	            ])
0052	            .then(([analysisData, metadataData, quickScanData]) => {
0053	                // Reset error counter on success
0054	                consecutiveErrors = 0;
0055	                
0056	                // Update DOM only if status has changed to reduce repaints
0057	                const analysisChanged = JSON.stringify(analysisData) !== JSON.stringify(lastAnalysisStatus);
0058	                const metadataChanged = JSON.stringify(metadataData) !== JSON.stringify(lastMetadataStatus);
0059	                const quickScanChanged = JSON.stringify(quickScanData) !== JSON.stringify(lastQuickScanStatus);
0060	                
0061	                if (!analysisChanged && !metadataChanged && !quickScanChanged) {
0062	                    // No changes, skip DOM update but continue polling
0063	                    scheduleNextPoll();
0064	                    return;
0065	                }
0066	                
0067	                // Save the current status for future comparison
0068	                lastAnalysisStatus = analysisData;
0069	                lastMetadataStatus = metadataData;
0070	                lastQuickScanStatus = quickScanData;
0071	                
0072	                // Only update the UI if something is running or being processed
0073	                if ((analysisData.running || metadataData.running || quickScanData.running || 
0074	                    (analysisData.files_processed > 0 && analysisData.files_processed < analysisData.total_files)) && !isNavigating) {
0075	                    statusIndicator.classList.add('active');
0076	                    
0077	                    // Simplified DOM update - determine the most important status to show
0078	                    let statusHTML = '';
0079	                    if (analysisData.running || (analysisData.files_processed > 0 && analysisData.files_processed < analysisData.total_files)) {
0080	                        // Calculate percent complete if needed
0081	                        let percentComplete = analysisData.percent_complete;
0082	                        if (!percentComplete && analysisData.total_files > 0) {
0083	                            percentComplete = Math.round((analysisData.files_processed / analysisData.total_files) * 100);
0084	                        }
0085	                        
0086	                        statusHTML = 
0087	                            '<div class="status-icon pulse"></div>' +
0088	                            '<div class="status-text">Analysis running (' + 
0089	                            percentComplete + '%) - ' + analysisData.files_processed + '/' + analysisData.total_files + ' files</div>';
0090	                    } else if (quickScanData.running) {
0091	                        statusHTML = 
0092	                            '<div class="status-icon pulse"></div>' +
0093	                            '<div class="status-text">Quick scanning files (' + 
0094	                            quickScanData.percent_complete + '%)</div>';
0095	                    } else if (metadataData.running) {
0096	                        statusHTML = 
0097	                            '<div class="status-icon pulse"></div>' +
0098	                            '<div class="status-text">Updating metadata (' + 
0099	                            metadataData.percent_complete + '%)</div>';
0100	                    }
0101	                    
0102	                    if (statusHTML) {
0103	                        statusIndicator.innerHTML = statusHTML;
0104	                    } else {
0105	                        statusIndicator.classList.remove('active');
0106	                    }
0107	                } else {
0108	                    statusIndicator.classList.remove('active');
0109	                    statusIndicator.innerHTML = '';
0110	                }
0111	                
0112	                // Schedule next poll with adaptive interval
0113	                if (analysisData.running || metadataData.running || quickScanData.running) {
0114	                    // Poll more frequently if tasks are running
0115	                    pollInterval = 3000;
0116	                } else {
0117	                    // Poll less frequently when idle
0118	                    pollInterval = 8000;
0119	                }
0120	                
0121	                scheduleNextPoll();
0122	            })
0123	            .catch(error => {
0124	                console.error('Error checking global status:', error);
0125	                consecutiveErrors++;
0126	                
0127	                // Increase poll interval on errors to reduce server load
0128	                if (consecutiveErrors > 3) {
0129	                    pollInterval = Math.min(pollInterval * 1.5, 10000); // Max 10 seconds
0130	                }
0131	                
0132	                // Even on error, continue checking
0133	                scheduleNextPoll();
0134	            });
0135	        }
0136	        
0137	        function scheduleNextPoll() {
0138	            // Clear existing timer to prevent overlap
0139	            if (pollTimer) clearTimeout(pollTimer);
0140	            
0141	            // Schedule next check
0142	            pollTimer = setTimeout(checkAllStatuses, pollInterval);
0143	        }
0144	        
0145	        // Debounce function to avoid excessive event handling
0146	        function debounce(func, wait) {
0147	            let timeout;
0148	            return function() {
0149	                const context = this, args = arguments;
0150	                clearTimeout(timeout);
0151	                timeout = setTimeout(() => func.apply(context, args), wait);
0152	            };
0153	        }
0154	        
0155	        // Return the control functions
0156	        return {
0157	            stop: function() {
0158	                if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
0159	            },
0160	            pause: window.pauseStatusPolling,
0161	            resume: window.resumeStatusPolling
0162	        };
0163	    };
0164	
0165	    // Remove all other global status checking functions and intervals
0166	    if (window.metadataStatusChecker) {
0167	        clearInterval(window.metadataStatusChecker);
0168	        window.metadataStatusChecker = null;
0169	    }
0170	
0171	    // Create a single centralized status checker
0172	    window.statusChecker = window.checkGlobalAnalysisStatus();
0173	});

0123	    function filterContent(query) {
0124	        query = query.toLowerCase();
0125	        
0126	        if (query === '') {
0127	            filteredData = currentData;
0128	        } else {
0129	            if (currentTab === 'artists') {
0130	                filteredData = currentData.filter(artist => 
0131	                    artist.artist.toLowerCase().includes(query)
0132	                );
0133	            } else if (currentTab === 'albums') {
0134	                filteredData = currentData.filter(album => 
0135	                    album.album.toLowerCase().includes(query) || 
0136	                    album.artist.toLowerCase().includes(query)
0137	                );
0138	            } else if (currentTab === 'songs') {
0139	                filteredData = currentData.filter(song => 
0140	                    (song.title && song.title.toLowerCase().includes(query)) || 
0141	                    (song.artist && song.artist.toLowerCase().includes(query)) || 
0142	                    (song.album && song.album.toLowerCase().includes(query))
0143	                );
0144	            }
0145	        }
0146	        
0147	        renderContent();
0148	    }
0149

0160	        } else if (currentTab === 'songs') {

0322	    // Helper function to escape HTML to prevent XSS
0323	    function escapeHtml(str) {
0324	        if (!str) return '';
0325	        return str
0326	            .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
0327	            .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
0328	            .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
0329	            .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
0330	            .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
0331	    }

0001	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0002	    console.log('Initializing logs page functionality');
0003	    
0004	    // Get DOM elements
0005	    const logContent = document.getElementById('log-content');
0006	    const logLines = document.getElementById('log-lines');
0007	    const refreshBtn = document.getElementById('refresh-logs');
0008	    const downloadBtn = document.getElementById('download-logs');
0009	    
0010	    // Check if we're on the logs page
0011	    if (!logContent || !logLines || !refreshBtn) {
0012	        console.log('Not on logs page or missing required elements');
0013	        return;
0014	    }
0015	    
0016	    console.log('Found log page elements, setting up functionality');
0017	    
0018	    // Initialize by loading logs
0019	    loadLogs();
0020	    
0021	    // Set up event listeners
0022	    refreshBtn.addEventListener('click', loadLogs);
0023	    logLines.addEventListener('change', loadLogs);
0024	    
0025	    if (downloadBtn) {
0026	        downloadBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
0027	            window.location.href = '/api/logs/download';
0028	        });
0029	    }
0030	    
0031	    function loadLogs() {
0032	        const lines = logLines.value;
0033	        logContent.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading logs...</div>';
0034	        
0035	        console.log('Loading logs with', lines, 'lines');
0036	        
0037	        fetch(`/api/logs/view?lines=${lines}`)
0038	            .then(response => {
0039	                if (!response.ok) {
0040	                    throw new Error(`HTTP error: ${response.status}`);
0041	                }
0042	                return response.json();
0043	            })
0044	            .then(data => {
0045	                if (data.error) {
0046	                    logContent.innerHTML = `<div class="error">${data.error}</div>`;
0047	                    return;
0048	                }
0049	                
0050	                if (!data.logs || data.logs.length === 0) {
0051	                    logContent.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No logs found</div>';
0052	                    return;
0053	                }
0054	                
0055	                logContent.innerHTML = '';
0056	                data.logs.forEach(line => {
0057	                    const logLine = document.createElement('div');
0058	                    logLine.className = 'log-line';
0059	                    logLine.textContent = line;
0060	                    
0061	                    // Add color based on log level
0062	                    if (line.includes(' DEBUG ')) logLine.classList.add('log-debug');
0063	                    if (line.includes(' INFO ')) logLine.classList.add('log-info');
0064	                    if (line.includes(' WARNING ')) logLine.classList.add('log-warning');
0065	                    if (line.includes(' ERROR ')) logLine.classList.add('log-error');
0066	                    if (line.includes(' CRITICAL ')) logLine.classList.add('log-critical');
0067	                    
0068	                    logContent.appendChild(logLine);
0069	                });
0070	                
0071	                // Scroll to bottom
0072	                logContent.scrollTop = logContent.scrollHeight;
0073	            })
0074	            .catch(error => {
0075	                console.error('Error loading logs:', error);
0076	                logContent.innerHTML = `<div class="error">Error loading logs: ${error.message}</div>`;
0077	            });
0078	    }
0079	    
0080	    // Flag that we've initialized
0081	    window.logsInitialized = true;
0082	});

0001	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0002	    console.log('Navigation.js loaded with enhanced debugging');
0003	    
0004	    // Track audio player state
0005	    const audioPlayer = document.getElementById('audio-player');
0006	    
0007	    // Define a global current track ID if it doesn't exist
0008	    window.currentTrackId = window.currentTrackId || null;
0009	    
0010	    // Create a simple system for preserving audio state during navigation
0011	    window.audioState = {
0012	        save: function() {
0013	            if (!audioPlayer) return null;
0014	            
0015	            // Get the Now Playing bar element
0016	            const nowPlayingBar = document.getElementById('now-playing-bar');
0017	            
0018	            return {
0019	                src: audioPlayer.src,
0020	                currentTime: audioPlayer.currentTime,
0021	                isPlaying: !audioPlayer.paused,
0022	                volume: audioPlayer.volume,
0023	                trackId: window.currentTrackId,
0024	                nowPlayingActive: nowPlayingBar ? nowPlayingBar.classList.contains('active') : false,
0025	                nowPlayingEmpty: nowPlayingBar ? nowPlayingBar.classList.contains('empty') : true,
0026	                trackTitle: document.getElementById('now-playing-title')?.textContent,
0027	                trackArtist: document.getElementById('now-playing-artist')?.textContent,
0028	                trackArt: document.getElementById('now-playing-art')?.src
0029	            };
0030	        },
0031	        restore: function(state) {
0032	            if (!audioPlayer || !state) return;
0033	            
0034	            // Get the Now Playing bar element
0035	            const nowPlayingBar = document.getElementById('now-playing-bar');
0036	            const nowPlayingTitle = document.getElementById('now-playing-title');
0037	            const nowPlayingArtist = document.getElementById('now-playing-artist');
0038	            const nowPlayingArt = document.getElementById('now-playing-art');
0039	            
0040	            // Preserve current playback state
0041	            const wasPlaying = !audioPlayer.paused;
0042	            const currentSrc = audioPlayer.src;
0043	            const currentTime = audioPlayer.currentTime;
0044	            
0045	            // Restore Now Playing bar state ONLY IF classes need to change
0046	            if (nowPlayingBar) {
0047	                if (state.nowPlayingActive && !nowPlayingBar.classList.contains('active')) {
0048	                    nowPlayingBar.classList.add('active');
0049	                } else if (!state.nowPlayingActive && nowPlayingBar.classList.contains('active')) {
0050	                    nowPlayingBar.classList.remove('active');
0051	                }
0052	                
0053	                if (state.nowPlayingEmpty && !nowPlayingBar.classList.contains('empty')) {
0054	                    nowPlayingBar.classList.add('empty');
0055	                } else if (!state.nowPlayingEmpty && nowPlayingBar.classList.contains('empty')) {
0056	                    nowPlayingBar.classList.remove('empty');
0057	                }
0058	            }
0059	            
0060	            // Only update display info if it's different
0061	            if (nowPlayingTitle && state.trackTitle && nowPlayingTitle.textContent !== state.trackTitle) {
0062	                nowPlayingTitle.textContent = state.trackTitle;
0063	            }
0064	            
0065	            if (nowPlayingArtist && state.trackArtist && nowPlayingArtist.textContent !== state.trackArtist) {
0066	                nowPlayingArtist.textContent = state.trackArtist;
0067	            }
0068	            
0069	            if (nowPlayingArt && state.trackArt && nowPlayingArt.src !== state.trackArt) {
0070	                nowPlayingArt.src = state.trackArt;
0071	            }
0072	            
0073	            // Only restore audio if we have a source and it's different from current
0074	            if (state.src && state.src !== '' && state.src !== currentSrc) {
0075	                console.log('Restoring audio source and state');
0076	                audioPlayer.src = state.src;
0077	                audioPlayer.currentTime = state.currentTime;
0078	                audioPlayer.volume = state.volume;
0079	                
0080	                if (state.isPlaying) {
0081	                    audioPlayer.play().catch(err => console.log('Auto-play prevented:', err));
0082	                }
0083	            } else if (state.src && state.src !== '' && state.src === currentSrc) {
0084	                // If same source but time position changed significantly
0085	                if (Math.abs(state.currentTime - currentTime) > 1) {
0086	                    audioPlayer.currentTime = state.currentTime;
0087	                }
0088	                
0089	                // Preserve playing state
0090	                if (state.isPlaying && !wasPlaying) {
0091	                    audioPlayer.play().catch(err => console.log('Auto-play prevented:', err));
0092	                } else if (!state.isPlaying && wasPlaying) {
0093	                    audioPlayer.pause();
0094	                }
0095	            }
0096	            
0097	            // Restore track ID
0098	            if (state.trackId) {
0099	                window.currentTrackId = state.trackId;
0100	            }
0101	        }
0102	    };

0118	    // Define the navigation function with improved content replacement and audio state preservation
0119	    function navigateTo(url) {
0120	        console.log('Navigating to:', url);
0121	        
0122	        // Save audio state before navigation
0123	        const savedAudioState = window.audioState.save();
0124	        
0125	        // Show loading indicator
0126	        const mainContent = document.querySelector('.main-content');
0127	        if (mainContent) {
0128	            mainContent.classList.add('loading-content');
0129	        }
0130	        
0131	        // Fetch the new page content
0132	        fetch(url)
0133	            .then(response => response.text())
0134	            .then(html => {
0135	                console.log('Received content from server for:', url);
0136	                
0137	                // Parse the HTML response
0138	                const parser = new DOMParser();
0139	                const doc = parser.parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
0140	                
0141	                // Extract only the main content from the fetched page
0142	                const newContent = doc.querySelector('.main-content');
0143	                
0144	                if (newContent && mainContent) {
0145	                    // Update page title
0146	                    document.title = doc.title;
0147	                    
0148	                    // KEY FIX: Only replace the children of main-content instead of using innerHTML
0149	                    // This preserves event handlers and prevents reflow of the whole document
0150	                    const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
0151	                    
0152	                    // Clone all child nodes from the new content
0153	                    Array.from(newContent.children).forEach(node => {
0154	                        fragment.appendChild(node.cloneNode(true));
0155	                    });
0156	                    
0157	                    // Clear existing content carefully
0158	                    while (mainContent.firstChild) {
0159	                        mainContent.removeChild(mainContent.firstChild);
0160	                    }
0161	                    
0162	                    // Append new content
0163	                    mainContent.appendChild(fragment);
0164	                    mainContent.classList.remove('loading-content');
0165	                    
0166	                    // Update URL in the browser history
0167	                    window.history.pushState({ url: url }, doc.title, url);
0168	                    
0169	                    // Restore audio state AFTER content replacement
0170	                    window.audioState.restore(savedAudioState);
0171	                    
0172	                    // Update active navigation links
0173	                    updateActiveNavigation(url);
0174	                    
0175	                    // Initialize any scripts needed for the new content
0176	                    initializePageScripts(url);
0177	                    
0178	                    // Reinitialize player controls after navigation
0179	                    reinitializePlayerControls();
0180	                    
0181	                    console.log('Content update complete for:', url);
0182	                } else {
0183	                    console.error('Could not extract main content from response for:', url);
0184	                    console.error('newContent exists:', !!newContent);
0185	                    console.error('mainContent exists:', !!mainContent);
0186	                    
0187	                    // Fallback to traditional navigation if content extraction fails
0188	                    window.location.href = url;
0189	                }
0190	            })
0191	            .catch(error => {
0192	                console.error('Error during AJAX navigation:', error);
0193	                // Fallback to traditional navigation on error
0194	                window.location.href = url;
0195	            });
0196	    }

0415	    function loadScript(src) {
0416	        if (document.querySelector(`script[src="${src}"]`)) {
0417	            console.log(`Script already loaded: ${src}`);
0418	            return;
0419	        }
0420	        
0421	        console.log(`Dynamically loading script: ${src}`);
0422	        const script = document.createElement('script');
0423	        script.src = src;
0424	        script.onload = function() {
0425	            console.log(`Script loaded: ${src}`);
0426	            
0427	            // Call the initialization function after loading the script
0428	            if (src.includes('library.js') && window.initLibraryPage) {
0429	                window.initLibraryPage();
0430	            } else if (src.includes('settings.js') && window.initSettingsPage) {
0431	                window.initSettingsPage();
0432	            } else if (src.includes('player.js') && window.initPlayerPage) {
0433	                window.initPlayerPage();
0434	            }
0435	        };
0436	        document.head.appendChild(script);
0437	    }

0474	        function loadLogs() {
0475	            const lines = logLines.value;
0476	            logContent.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading logs...</div>';
0477	            
0478	            console.log('Loading logs with', lines, 'lines');
0479	            fetch(`/api/logs/view?lines=${lines}`)
0480	                .then(response => response.json())
0481	                .then(data => {
0482	                    if (data.error) {
0483	                        logContent.innerHTML = `<div class="error">${data.error}</div>`;
0484	                        return;
0485	                    }
0486	                    
0487	                    if (!data.logs || data.logs.length === 0) {
0488	                        logContent.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No logs found</div>';
0489	                        return;
0490	                    }
0491	                    
0492	                    logContent.innerHTML = '';
0493	                    data.logs.forEach(line => {
0494	                        const logLine = document.createElement('div');
0495	                        logLine.className = 'log-line';
0496	                        logLine.textContent = line;
0497	                        
0498	                        // Add color based on log level
0499	                        if (line.includes(' DEBUG ')) logLine.classList.add('log-debug');
0500	                        if (line.includes(' INFO ')) logLine.classList.add('log-info');
0501	                        if (line.includes(' WARNING ')) logLine.classList.add('log-warning');
0502	                        if (line.includes(' ERROR ')) logLine.classList.add('log-error');
0503	                        if (line.includes(' CRITICAL ')) logLine.classList.add('log-critical');
0504	                        
0505	                        logContent.appendChild(logLine);
0506	                    });
0507	                    
0508	                    // Scroll to bottom
0509	                    logContent.scrollTop = logContent.scrollHeight;
0510	                })
0511	                .catch(error => {
0512	                    logContent.innerHTML = `<div class="error">Error loading logs: ${error}</div>`;
0513	                });
0514	        }
0515	    }

0555	            } else {

0557	                audioPlayer.pause();
0558	                newButton.textContent = '▶'; // Play symbol
0559	                newButton.title = 'Play';
0560	            }
0561	        });
0562	        
0563	        // Update the button to match current state
0564	        if (audioPlayer && !audioPlayer.paused) {
0565	            newButton.textContent = '⏸';
0566	            newButton.title = 'Pause';
0567	        } else {
0568	            newButton.textContent = '▶';
0569	            newButton.title = 'Play';
0570	        }

0573	    }

0008	function initializePlayerControls() {
0009	    // Get DOM elements
0010	    const audioPlayer = document.getElementById('audio-player');
0011	    const playPauseBtn = document.getElementById('play-pause');
0012	    const prevBtn = document.getElementById('prev-track');

0029	    if (window.PUMPPlayer) {

0031	            console.log('PlayerManager is ready, initializing controls');
0032	            setupControls(playerManager);
0033	            setupPlayButtons();
0034	        });
0035	    } else {
0036	        console.error('PUMPPlayer not found. Using fallback initialization.');
0037	        // Fallback to direct check with retry
0038	        checkForPlayerManager();

0040	    
0041	    // Fallback mechanism if PUMPPlayer is not available
0042	    function checkForPlayerManager(retry = 0) {
0043	        if (window.playerManager) {
0044	            console.log('PlayerManager found, initializing controls');
0045	            setupControls(window.playerManager);
0046	            setupPlayButtons();
0047	            return;
0048	        }
0049	        
0050	        if (retry < 5) {
0051	            console.log(`Waiting for PlayerManager (attempt ${retry + 1}/5)...`);
0052	            setTimeout(() => checkForPlayerManager(retry + 1), 300);
0053	        } else {
0054	            console.error('PlayerManager not available after maximum retries. Audio controls may not work properly.');
0055	            // Still set up the controls with limited functionality
0056	            setupControls(null);
0057	            setupPlayButtons();
0058	        }
0059	    }
0060	    
0061	    function setupControls(playerManager) {
0062	        // Progress bar click handling
0063	        if (progressBar) {
0064	            progressBar.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
0065	                if (!audioPlayer || !audioPlayer.duration) return;
0066	                
0067	                const rect = this.getBoundingClientRect();
0068	                const pos = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;
0069	                audioPlayer.currentTime = pos * audioPlayer.duration;
0070	            });
0071	            
0072	            // Make progress bar draggable
0073	            let isDragging = false;
0074

0076	                isDragging = true;
0077	                progressBar.classList.add('dragging');
0078	            });
0079	            
0080	            document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
0081	                if (!isDragging || !audioPlayer || !audioPlayer.duration) return;
0082	                
0083	                const rect = progressBar.getBoundingClientRect();
0084	                let pos = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;
0085	                pos = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, pos)); // Clamp between 0 and 1
0086	                
0087	                // Update UI while dragging
0088	                progressFill.style.width = `${pos * 100}%`;
0089	                
0090	                // Calculate and display time
0091	                const time = pos * audioPlayer.duration;
0092	                currentTimeDisplay.textContent = formatTime(time);
0093	            });
0094	            
0095	            document.addEventListener('mouseup', function(e) {
0096	                if (!isDragging) return;
0097	                
0098	                const rect = progressBar.getBoundingClientRect();
0099	                let pos = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;
0100	                pos = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, pos)); // Clamp between 0 and 1
0101	                
0102	                // Set the time when finished dragging
0103	                if (audioPlayer && audioPlayer.duration) {
0104	                    audioPlayer.currentTime = pos * audioPlayer.duration;
0105	                }
0106	                
0107	                isDragging = false;
0108	                progressBar.classList.remove('dragging');
0109	            });
0110	        }
0111	        
0112	        // Volume control
0113	        if (volumeSlider) {
0114	            volumeSlider.addEventListener('input', function() {
0115	                if (!audioPlayer) return;
0116	                
0117	                const volume = parseFloat(this.value);
0118	                audioPlayer.volume = volume;
0119	                
0120	                // Save volume preference
0121	                localStorage.setItem('volume', volume);
0122	                
0123	                // Update mute button icon
0124	                updateMuteButtonIcon(volume);
0125	            });
0126	            
0127	            // Load saved volume
0128	            const savedVolume = localStorage.getItem('volume');
0129	            if (savedVolume !== null) {
0130	                const volume = parseFloat(savedVolume);
0131	                volumeSlider.value = volume;
0132	                if (audioPlayer) audioPlayer.volume = volume;
0133	                updateMuteButtonIcon(volume);
0134	            }
0135	        }
0136	        
0137	        // Mute button
0138	        if (muteButton) {
0139	            muteButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
0140	                if (!audioPlayer) return;
0141	                
0142	                if (audioPlayer.volume > 0) {
0143	                    // Store current volume and mute
0144	                    localStorage.setItem('volumeBeforeMute', audioPlayer.volume);
0145	                    audioPlayer.volume = 0;
0146	                    volumeSlider.value = 0;
0147	                    updateMuteButtonIcon(0);
0148	                } else {
0149	                    // Restore previous volume
0150	                    const previousVolume = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('volumeBeforeMute') || 0.7);
0151	                    audioPlayer.volume = previousVolume;
0152	                    volumeSlider.value = previousVolume;
0153	                    updateMuteButtonIcon(previousVolume);
0154	                }
0155	            });
0156	        }
0157	        
0158	        // Handle like button
0159	        if (likeButton && playerManager) {
0160	            likeButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
0161	                // Get the currently playing track ID
0162	                const trackId = playerManager.currentTrackId;
0163	                if (!trackId) {
0164	                    console.log('No track currently playing');
0165	                    return;
0166	                }
0167	                
0168	                const isCurrentlyLiked = likeButton.classList.contains('liked');
0169	                const action = isCurrentlyLiked ? 'unlike' : 'like';
0170	                
0171	                fetch(`/api/tracks/${trackId}/${action}`, {
0172	                    method: 'POST'
0173	                })
0174	                .then(response => response.json())
0175	                .then(data => {
0176	                    if (data.status === 'success') {
0177	                        // Toggle liked state visually
0178	                        likeButton.classList.toggle('liked');
0179	                        likeButton.textContent = isCurrentlyLiked ? 'â™¡' : 'â™¥';
0180	                        
0181	                        // Show feedback
0182	                        const message = isCurrentlyLiked ? 'Removed from liked tracks' : 'Added to liked tracks';
0183	                        showToast(message);
0184	                    } else {
0185	                        console.error('Error toggling like:', data.error);
0186	                    }
0187	                })
0188	                .catch(error => {
0189	                    console.error('Error toggling like:', error);
0190	                });
0191	            });
0192	        }
0193	    }

0201	        } else if (volume < 0.5) {

0280	                            clearInterval(checkInterval);
0281	                        }

0283

0308	// Listen for dynamic UI changes (e.g., AJAX loads) to set up new play buttons
0309	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0310	    // Set up a MutationObserver to watch for new play buttons added to the DOM
0311	    const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
0312	        mutations.forEach(function(mutation) {
0313	            if (mutation.addedNodes && mutation.addedNodes.length > 0) {
0314	                // Check if any of the added nodes contain play buttons
0315	                mutation.addedNodes.forEach(function(node) {
0316	                    if (node.nodeType === 1 && node.classList && node.classList.contains('play-track')) {
0317	                        // Found a new play button
0318	                        setupPlayButton(node);
0319	                    } else if (node.nodeType === 1 && node.querySelectorAll) {
0320	                        // Check for play buttons inside this node
0321	                        const buttons = node.querySelectorAll('.play-track');
0322	                        if (buttons.length > 0) {
0323	                            console.log('Found new play buttons after DOM mutation:', buttons.length);
0324	                            buttons.forEach(setupPlayButton);
0325	                        }
0326	                    }
0327	                });
0328	            }
0329	        });
0330	    });
0331	    
0332	    // Start observing the document body for changes
0333	    observer.observe(document.body, { 
0334	        childList: true, 
0335	        subtree: true 
0336	    });
0337	    
0338	    function setupPlayButton(button) {
0339	        button.addEventListener('click', function() {
0340	            const trackId = this.dataset.id;
0341	            console.log('Dynamically added play button clicked for track ID:', trackId);
0342	            
0343	            if (trackId && typeof window.playTrack === 'function') {
0344	                window.playTrack(trackId);
0345	            } else {
0346	                console.error('Cannot play track: playTrack function not available or no track ID');
0347	            }
0348	        });
0349	    }

0001	/**
0002	 * PlayerManager - Handles audio playback, track history, and queue management
0003	 * Replaces the previous SQLite memory database approach with a client-side system
0004	 */
0005	class PlayerManager {
0006	    constructor() {
0007	        console.log('Initializing PlayerManager');
0008	        this.audioPlayer = document.getElementById('audio-player');
0009	        this.nowPlayingBar = document.getElementById('now-playing-bar');
0010	        this.playPauseBtn = document.getElementById('play-pause');
0011	        this.prevTrackBtn = document.getElementById('prev-track');
0012	        this.nextTrackBtn = document.getElementById('next-track');
0013	        this.progressFill = document.getElementById('progress-fill');
0014	        this.currentTimeDisplay = document.getElementById('current-time');
0015	        this.totalTimeDisplay = document.getElementById('total-time');
0016	        this.nowPlayingTitle = document.getElementById('now-playing-title');
0017	        this.nowPlayingArtist = document.getElementById('now-playing-artist');
0018	        this.nowPlayingArt = document.getElementById('now-playing-art');
0019	        this.likeButton = document.getElementById('like-track');
0020	        
0021	        // Track management
0022	        this.queue = [];          // Upcoming tracks
0023	        this.history = [];        // Previously played tracks
0024	        this.currentTrack = null; // Currently playing track
0025	        this.currentTrackId = null; // ID of current track
0026	        
0027	        // Initialize event listeners
0028	        this.initializeEventListeners();
0029	        
0030	        // Load recent track from local storage
0031	        this.loadRecentTrack();
0032	    }
0033	    
0034	    initializeEventListeners() {
0035	        if (!this.audioPlayer) {
0036	            console.error('Audio player element not found!');
0037	            return;
0038	        }
0039	        
0040	        // Audio player events
0041	        this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('timeupdate', () => this.updateProgressBar());
0042	        this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('ended', () => this.playNextTrack());
0043	        this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('play', () => this.updatePlayPauseButton(true));
0044	        this.audioPlayer.addEventListener('pause', () => this.updatePlayPauseButton(false));
0045	        
0046	        // Control button events
0047	        if (this.playPauseBtn) {
0048	            this.playPauseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => this.togglePlayPause());
0049	        }
0050	        
0051	        if (this.nextTrackBtn) {
0052	            this.nextTrackBtn.addEventListener('click', () => this.playNextTrack());
0053	        }
0054	        
0055	        if (this.prevTrackBtn) {
0056	            this.prevTrackBtn.addEventListener('click', () => this.playPreviousTrack());
0057	        }
0058	    }
0059	    
0060	    // Play a specific track by ID
0061	    playTrack(trackId) {
0062	        console.log(`Playing track: ${trackId}`);
0063	        
0064	        // Clear any existing audio source first to prevent AbortError
0065	        if (this.audioPlayer) {
0066	            this.audioPlayer.pause();
0067	            this.audioPlayer.removeAttribute('src');
0068	            this.audioPlayer.load();
0069	        }
0070	        
0071	        // Fetch track details and URL from the API
0072	        fetch(`/api/tracks/${trackId}`)
0073	            .then(response => {
0074	                if (!response.ok) {
0075	                    throw new Error(`Failed to fetch track: ${response.status}`);
0076	                }
0077	                return response.json();
0078	            })
0079	            .then(data => {
0080	                if (data.error) {
0081	                    console.error('Error fetching track:', data.error);
0082	                    return;
0083	                }
0084	                
0085	                // If we're playing a different track, add the current one to history
0086	                if (this.currentTrackId && this.currentTrackId !== trackId) {
0087	                    this.history.push(this.currentTrack);
0088	                    // Keep history at a reasonable size
0089	                    if (this.history.length > 50) {
0090	                        this.history.shift();
0091	                    }
0092	                }
0093	                
0094	                // Update current track
0095	                this.currentTrack = data;
0096	                this.currentTrackId = trackId;
0097	                
0098	                // FIXED: Use the correct streaming endpoint format based on API
0099	                // Try the ID-based endpoint first, and if file_path exists, use that as fallback
0100	                let streamUrl;
0101	                if (data.file_path) {
0102	                    // Use file path-based streaming if available
0103	                    streamUrl = `/api/stream?path=${encodeURIComponent(data.file_path)}`;
0104	                } else {
0105	                    // Otherwise use ID-based streaming
0106	                    streamUrl = `/stream/${trackId}`;
0107	                }
0108	                
0109	                console.log(`Using stream URL: ${streamUrl}`);
0110	                
0111	                // Set audio source and play
0112	                this.audioPlayer.src = streamUrl;
0113	                
0114	                // Play after a small delay to prevent AbortError
0115	                setTimeout(() => {
0116	                    this.audioPlayer.play().catch(err => {
0117	                        console.error('Error playing track:', err);
0118	                    });
0119	                }, 50);
0120	                
0121	                // Update the UI
0122	                this.updateNowPlayingUI(data);
0123	                
0124	                // Save to local storage
0125	                localStorage.setItem('lastTrackId', trackId);
0126	                
0127	                // Check if track is liked (with error handling)
0128	                this.checkTrackLikeStatus(trackId);
0129	            })
0130	            .catch(error => {
0131	                console.error('Error playing track:', error);
0132	                this.showToast('Error playing track: ' + error.message);
0133	            });
0134	    }
0135	    
0136	    // Update the progress bar based on current playback time
0137	    updateProgressBar() {
0138	        if (!this.audioPlayer || !this.progressFill) return;
0139	        
0140	        if (this.audioPlayer.duration) {
0141	            const percent = (this.audioPlayer.currentTime / this.audioPlayer.duration) * 100;
0142	            this.progressFill.style.width = `${percent}%`;
0143	            
0144	            // Update time displays
0145	            if (this.currentTimeDisplay) {
0146	                this.currentTimeDisplay.textContent = this.formatTime(this.audioPlayer.currentTime);
0147	            }
0148	            
0149	            if (this.totalTimeDisplay) {
0150	                this.totalTimeDisplay.textContent = this.formatTime(this.audioPlayer.duration);
0151	            }
0152	        }
0153	    }
0154	    
0155	    // Format time in seconds to MM:SS format
0156	    formatTime(time) {
0157	        if (isNaN(time)) return '0:00';
0158	        
0159	        const minutes = Math.floor(time / 60);
0160	        const seconds = Math.floor(time % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
0161	        return `${minutes}:${seconds}`;
0162	    }

0164	    // Toggle play/pause
0165	    togglePlayPause() {

0167	        
0168	        if (this.audioPlayer.paused) {
0169	            if (this.audioPlayer.src) {
0170	                this.audioPlayer.play().catch(err => {
0171	                    console.error('Error playing audio:', err);
0172	                });
0173	            } else if (this.currentTrackId) {
0174	                // If we have a track ID but no source, reload it
0175	                this.playTrack(this.currentTrackId);
0176	            }
0177	        } else {
0178	            this.audioPlayer.pause();
0179	        }
0180	    }

0193	            this.playPauseBtn.title = 'Play';
0194	            if (!this.currentTrack) {

0196	            }

0222	    // Play the next track in the queue
0223	    playNextTrack() {
0224	        if (this.queue.length > 0) {
0225	            // Get next track from queue
0226	            const nextTrack = this.queue.shift();
0227	            this.playTrack(nextTrack.id);
0228	        } else {
0229	            // No more tracks in queue
0230	            console.log('End of queue reached');
0231	        }
0232	    }

0234	    // Play the previous track from history
0235	    playPreviousTrack() {
0236	        if (this.history.length > 0) {
0237	            // Get the most recent track from history
0238	            const prevTrack = this.history.pop();
0239	            
0240	            // Add current track to the front of the queue
0241	            if (this.currentTrack) {
0242	                this.queue.unshift(this.currentTrack);
0243	            }
0244	            
0245	            // Play the previous track
0246	            this.playTrack(prevTrack.id);
0247	        } else {
0248	            // No history, restart current track
0249	            if (this.audioPlayer) {
0250	                this.audioPlayer.currentTime = 0;
0251	            }
0252	        }
0253	    }

0255	    // Add a track to the queue

0260	        if (!this.currentTrack || this.audioPlayer.paused) {
0261	            this.playNextTrack();
0262	        }
0263	        
0264	        // Show notification
0265	        this.showToast(`Added "${track.title}" to queue`);
0266	    }
0267	    
0268	    // Add multiple tracks to the queue

0270	        if (!tracks || tracks.length === 0) return;
0271

0273	        
0274	        // If nothing is playing, start playing the first track in the queue
0275	        if (!this.currentTrack || this.audioPlayer.paused) {
0276	            this.playNextTrack();
0277	        }
0278	        
0279	        this.showToast(`Added ${tracks.length} tracks to queue`);
0280	    }

0325	        
0326	        fetch(`/api/tracks/${trackId}/liked`)

0331	                    if (response.status === 404) {

0333	                        return null;

0339	            .then(data => {

0345	                    this.likeButton.textContent = '♥';

0384	    manager: null,

0386	    // Function to initialize the PlayerManager
0387	    initialize: function() {

0389	        
0390	        if (this.manager) {
0391	            console.log('PlayerManager already initialized');

0394	        
0395	        try {
0396	            // Create PlayerManager instance
0397	            this.manager = new PlayerManager();
0398	            window.playerManager = this.manager; // Also set global reference
0399	            
0400	            // Create global playTrack function that all components can use
0401	            window.playTrack = function(trackId) {
0402	                console.log('Global playTrack called with ID:', trackId);
0403	                if (window.playerManager) {
0404	                    window.playerManager.playTrack(trackId);
0405	                } else {
0406	                    console.error('PlayerManager not available for playTrack function');
0407	                    // Store the track ID for later playback
0408	                    window.pendingPlayTrackId = trackId;
0409	                }
0410	            };
0411	            
0412	            // Set ready state and process queued callbacks
0413	            this.isReady = true;
0414	            this.processPendingCallbacks();
0415	            
0416	            // Handle any pending track playback
0417	            if (window.pendingPlayTrackId) {
0418	                console.log('Playing pending track:', window.pendingPlayTrackId);
0419	                window.playTrack(window.pendingPlayTrackId);
0420	                window.pendingPlayTrackId = null;
0421	            }
0422	            
0423	            console.log('PlayerManager successfully initialized');
0424	            return this.manager;
0425	        } catch (error) {
0426	            console.error('Error initializing PlayerManager:', error);
0427	            this.isReady = false;
0428	            return null;
0429	        }
0430	    },
0431	    
0432	    // Function to add callback to be executed when PlayerManager is ready
0433	    ready: function(callback) {
0434	        if (this.isReady && this.manager) {
0435	            // If already ready, execute immediately
0436	            callback(this.manager);
0437	        } else {
0438	            // Otherwise queue for execution
0439	            this.pendingCallbacks.push(callback);
0440	        }
0441	    },
0442	    
0443	    // Process all pending callbacks
0444	    processPendingCallbacks: function() {
0445	        console.log(`Processing ${this.pendingCallbacks.length} pending callbacks`);
0446	        
0447	        while (this.pendingCallbacks.length > 0) {
0448	            const callback = this.pendingCallbacks.shift();
0449	            try {
0450	                callback(this.manager);
0451	            } catch (error) {
0452	                console.error('Error executing callback:', error);
0453	            }
0454	        }
0455	    }
0456	};
0457	
0458	// Initialize the player manager when the DOM is fully loaded
0459	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0460	    console.log('DOM loaded - initializing PlayerManager');
0461	    window.PUMPPlayer.initialize();
0462	    
0463	    // Dispatch a custom event that other scripts can listen for
0464	    document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('playerManagerReady'));
0465	    
0466	    // Also execute any remaining inline scripts (this is a safety measure)
0467	    setTimeout(function() {
0468	        if (typeof window.onPlayerManagerReady === 'function') {
0469	            window.onPlayerManagerReady(window.playerManager);
0470	        }
0471	    }, 100);
0472	});

0053	    console.log(`Global loadPlaylist called for playlist ${playlistId}`);
0054	    
0055	    // Add safeguard against undefined playlist IDs
0056	    if (playlistId === undefined || playlistId === 'undefined') {
0057	        console.error('Cannot load playlist: Invalid playlist ID');
0058	        const analyzeStatus = document.getElementById('analyze-status');
0059	        if (analyzeStatus) {
0060	            analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '<div class="status-error">Error: Invalid playlist ID</div>';
0061	        }
0062	        return;
0063	    }
0064	    
0065	    // Show loading state
0066	    const analyzeStatus = document.getElementById('analyze-status');
0067	    if (analyzeStatus) {
0068	        analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '<div class="status-progress">Loading playlist...</div>';
0069	    }
0070	    
0071	    // Make the playlist container visible
0072	    const playlistContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-container');
0073	    if (playlistContainer) {
0074	        playlistContainer.style.display = 'block';
0075	    }
0076	    
0077	    // Updated URL from /playlists/${playlistId} to match backend route
0078	    fetch(`/playlists/${playlistId}`)
0079	        .then(response => response.json())
0080	        .then(data => {
0081	            console.log('Loaded playlist:', data);
0082	            if (data.error) {
0083	                if (analyzeStatus) {
0084	                    analyzeStatus.innerHTML = `<div class="status-error">Error: ${data.error}</div>`;
0085	                }
0086	                return;
0087	            }
0088	            
0089	            // Store current playlist in the global scope
0090	            window.currentPlaylist = data.tracks;
0091	            
0092	            // Display the playlist
0093	            if (typeof displayPlaylist === 'function') {
0094	                displayPlaylist(data.tracks);
0095	            } else if (typeof window.displayPlaylist === 'function') {
0096	                window.displayPlaylist(data.tracks);
0097	            } else {
0098	                console.error('displayPlaylist function not available');
0099	            }
0100	            
0101	            // Update header with playlist name
0102	            const playlistHeader = document.querySelector('.playlist-header h2');
0103	            if (playlistHeader) {
0104	                playlistHeader.textContent = `Playlist: ${data.name}`;
0105	            }
0106	            
0107	            // Enable save button
0108	            const savePlaylistBtn = document.getElementById('save-playlist-btn');
0109	            if (savePlaylistBtn) {
0110	                savePlaylistBtn.disabled = false;
0111	            }
0112	            
0113	            // Clear status after a delay
0114	            if (analyzeStatus) {
0115	                setTimeout(() => {
0116	                    analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '';
0117	                }, 2000);
0118	            }
0119	        })
0120	        .catch(error => {
0121	            console.error('Error loading playlist:', error);
0122	            if (analyzeStatus) {
0123	                analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '<div class="status-error">Failed to load playlist</div>';
0124	            }
0125	        });
0126	};

0372	    const stationSize = window.stationSize || 20;

0374	    fetch(`/api/station/${trackId}?num_tracks=${stationSize}`)
0375	        .then(response => {

0387	                return {

0389	                    file_path: trackArray[1],
0390	                    title: trackArray[2] || 'Unknown Title',
0391	                    artist: trackArray[3] || 'Unknown Artist',
0392	                    album: trackArray[4] || 'Unknown Album',
0393	                    genre: trackArray[5],
0394	                    duration: trackArray[7],
0395	                    album_art_url: trackArray[11]
0396	                };
0397	            });
0398

0401	            
0402	            // Display the tracks in the playlist

0534	// Add this to optimize album art loading
0535	function optimizeImageLoading(imgElement, src) {
0536	    // Create a low-priority image loader
0537	    const loader = new Image();
0538	    
0539	    // Set up load handler before setting src
0540	    loader.onload = function() {
0541	        // Only update the main image when loading completes
0542	        if (imgElement) {
0543	            imgElement.src = src;
0544	        }
0545	    };
0546	    
0547	    loader.onerror = function() {
0548	        // Fallback to default on error
0549	        if (imgElement) {
0550	            imgElement.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png';
0551	        }
0552	    };
0553	    
0554	    // Low priority loading
0555	    if ('fetchPriority' in loader) {
0556	        loader.fetchPriority = 'low';
0557	    }
0558	    
0559	    // Start loading
0560	    loader.src = src;
0561	}
0562	
0563	// Update the displayTracksInGrid function to use lazy loading and optimized image loading
0564	function displayTracksInGrid(tracks, container) {
0565	    if (!container) return;
0566	    
0567	    container.innerHTML = '';
0568	    
0569	    // Create track grid
0570	    const trackGrid = document.createElement('div');
0571	    trackGrid.className = 'track-grid';
0572	    
0573	    // Only render visible tracks initially (virtual rendering)
0574	    const initialVisibleCount = Math.min(20, tracks.length);
0575	    
0576	    // Add initial visible tracks to grid
0577	    for (let i = 0; i < initialVisibleCount; i++) {
0578	        const trackCard = createOptimizedTrackCard(tracks[i]);
0579	        trackGrid.appendChild(trackCard);
0580	    }
0581	    
0582	    // Add grid to container
0583	    container.appendChild(trackGrid);
0584	    
0585	    // Setup intersection observer for infinite scrolling if there are more tracks
0586	    if (tracks.length > initialVisibleCount) {
0587	        setupInfiniteScroll(trackGrid, tracks, initialVisibleCount);
0588	    }
0589	    
0590	    // Add Play All button at the top
0591	    const actionButton = document.createElement('div');
0592	    actionButton.className = 'liked-actions-container';
0593	    actionButton.innerHTML = `
0594	        <button class="primary-button" id="play-all-liked-btn">Play All Tracks</button>
0595	    `;
0596	    container.insertBefore(actionButton, trackGrid);
0597	    
0598	    // Add event listener for Play All button
0599	    document.getElementById('play-all-liked-btn')?.addEventListener('click', function() {
0600	        if (window.currentPlaylist && window.currentPlaylist.length > 0) {
0601	            if (typeof window.playEntirePlaylist === 'function') {
0602	                window.playEntirePlaylist(window.currentPlaylist);
0603	            } else {
0604	                // Fallback to playing the first track
0605	                if (typeof window.playTrack === 'function' && window.currentPlaylist[0]) {
0606	                    window.playTrack(window.currentPlaylist[0].id);
0607	                }
0608	            }
0609	        }
0610	    });
0611	}
0612	
0613	// Optimized track card creation
0614	function createOptimizedTrackCard(track) {
0615	    const trackCard = document.createElement('div');
0616	    trackCard.className = 'track-card';
0617	    
0618	    // Create album art container with image placeholder
0619	    const albumArt = document.createElement('div');
0620	    albumArt.className = 'album-art';
0621	    
0622	    // Create image with lazy loading
0623	    const img = document.createElement('img');
0624	    img.alt = 'Album Art';
0625	    img.loading = "lazy"; // Use browser's lazy loading
0626	    img.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png'; // Start with default
0627	    
0628	    if (track.album_art_url) {
0629	        // Process image URL but delay actual loading
0630	        let imgSrc = track.album_art_url;
0631	        
0632	        if (imgSrc.includes('album_art_cache/') || imgSrc.includes('album_art_cache\\')) {
0633	            const parts = imgSrc.split(/[\/\\]/);
0634	            const filename = parts[parts.length - 1];
0635	            imgSrc = `/cache/${filename}`;
0636	        } else if (imgSrc.startsWith('http')) {
0637	            imgSrc = `/albumart/${encodeURIComponent(imgSrc)}`;
0638	        }
0639	        
0640	        // Set the data-src instead of src for delayed loading
0641	        img.setAttribute('data-src', imgSrc);
0642	        
0643	        // Use intersection observer to load when visible
0644	        if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
0645	            const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
0646	                entries.forEach(entry => {
0647	                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
0648	                        const image = entry.target;
0649	                        image.src = image.dataset.src;
0650	                        observer.unobserve(image);
0651	                    }
0652	                });
0653	            });
0654	            observer.observe(img);
0655	        } else {
0656	            // Fallback for browsers without IntersectionObserver
0657	            setTimeout(() => { img.src = imgSrc; }, 100);
0658	        }
0659	    }
0660	    
0661	    // Create play overlay with hover effect
0662	    const playOverlay = document.createElement('div');
0663	    playOverlay.className = 'play-overlay';
0664	    playOverlay.innerHTML = '<i class="play-icon">▶</i>';
0665	    
0666	    // Add elements to container
0667	    albumArt.appendChild(img);
0668	    albumArt.appendChild(playOverlay);
0669	    trackCard.appendChild(albumArt);
0670	    
0671	    // Add track info
0672	
0673	    return trackCard;
0674	}
0675	
0676	// Setup infinite scrolling
0677	function setupInfiniteScroll(container, allTracks, startIndex) {
0678	    // Create a sentinel element to observe
0679	    const sentinel = document.createElement('div');
0680	    sentinel.className = 'scroll-sentinel';
0681	    sentinel.style.height = '10px';
0682	    sentinel.style.width = '100%';
0683	    container.appendChild(sentinel);
0684	    
0685	    // Function to load more items
0686	    let isLoading = false;
0687	    let currentIndex = startIndex;
0688	    
0689	    function loadMoreItems() {
0690	        if (isLoading || currentIndex >= allTracks.length) return;
0691	        
0692	        isLoading = true;
0693	        const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
0694	        
0695	        // Load next batch
0696	        const batchSize = 10;
0697	        const endIndex = Math.min(currentIndex + batchSize, allTracks.length);
0698	        
0699	        for (let i = currentIndex; i < endIndex; i++) {
0700	            const trackCard = createOptimizedTrackCard(allTracks[i]);
0701	            fragment.appendChild(trackCard);
0702	        }
0703	        
0704	        // Insert before sentinel
0705	        container.insertBefore(fragment, sentinel);
0706	        currentIndex = endIndex;
0707	        isLoading = false;
0708	    }
0709	    
0710	    // Create intersection observer
0711	    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
0712	        if (entries[0].isIntersecting) {
0713	            loadMoreItems();
0714	        }
0715	    });
0716	    
0717	    // Start observing
0718	    observer.observe(sentinel);
0719	}

0959	            }

0963	                .then(response => {

0965	                    return response.json();
0966	                })

0969	                    if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
0970	                        // Limit to 6 items only

0972	                    } else if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length === 0) {
0973	                        searchResults.innerHTML = '<p>No tracks found. Try adding some music!</p>';
0974	                    } else if (data.error) {
0975	                        searchResults.innerHTML = `<p>Error: ${data.error}</p>`;
0976	                    }
0977	                })
0978	                .catch(error => {
0979	                    console.error('Error loading explore:', error);
0980	                    if (searchResults) {

0982	                    }
0983	                });

0986	        function loadRecent() {
0987	            console.log('loadRecent called');
0988	            if (resultsContainer) {
0989	                const heading = resultsContainer.querySelector('h2');
0990	                if (heading) heading.textContent = 'Recently Added';
0991	            }

0994	                searchResults.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading recent tracks...</div>';
0995	            }
0996	            
0997	            fetch('/recent')
0998	                .then(response => {
0999	                    console.log('Recent response received:', response);
1000	                    return response.json();
1001	                })
1002	                .then(data => {
1003	                    console.log('Recent data:', data);
1004	                    if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
1005	                        // Process the tracks - convert arrays to objects if needed
1006	                        let processedTracks;
1007	                        
1008	                        // Check if we have an array of arrays (numbered indices)
1009	                        if (Array.isArray(data[0])) {
1010	                            console.log('Converting array format to object format for recent tracks');
1011	                            processedTracks = data.map(trackArray => {
1012	                                return {
1013	                                    id: trackArray[0],
1014	                                    file_path: trackArray[1],
1015	                                    title: trackArray[2] || 'Unknown Title',
1016	                                    artist: trackArray[3] || 'Unknown Artist',
1017	                                    album: trackArray[4] || 'Unknown Album',
1018	                                    album_art_url: trackArray[5],
1019	                                    duration: trackArray[6]
1020	                                };
1021	                            });
1022	                        } else {
1023	                            // Already in object format
1024	                            processedTracks = data;
1025	                        }
1026	                        
1027	                        // Limit to 12 items for better performance
1028	                        const displayData = processedTracks.slice(0, 12);
1029	                        
1030	                        // Save in current playlist for potential saving
1031	                        window.currentPlaylist = displayData;
1032	                        
1033	                        // Display the tracks
1034	                        displaySearchResults(displayData);
1035	                    } else if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length === 0) {
1036	                        searchResults.innerHTML = '<p>No recent tracks found. Try adding some music!</p>';
1037	                    } else if (data.error) {
1038	                        searchResults.innerHTML = `<p>Error: ${data.error}</p>`;
1039	                    }
1040	                })
1041	                .catch(error => {
1042	                    console.error('Error loading recent tracks:', error);
1043	                    if (searchResults) {
1044	                        searchResults.innerHTML = '<p>Failed to load recent tracks. See console for details.</p>';
1045	                    }
1046	                });
1047	        }

1053	            }

1057	                .then(data => {
1058	                    console.log('Search results:', data);
1059	                    if (Array.isArray(data)) {
1060	                        displaySearchResults(data);
1061	                    } else if (data.error) {
1062	                        searchResults.innerHTML = `<p>Error: ${data.error}</p>`;
1063	                    }
1064	                })
1065	                .catch(error => {
1066	                    console.error('Error searching:', error);
1067	                    if (searchResults) {
1068	                        searchResults.innerHTML = '<p>Search failed. See console for details.</p>';
1069	                    }
1070	                });
1071	        }

1119	        // Update the createStation function to work from any view, including the Liked view
1120	    
1121	        function createStation(trackId) {
1122	            if (!trackId) return;
1123	            
1124	            console.log('Creating station for track ID:', trackId);
1125	            
1126	            // Show loading state in the playlist area
1127	            const playlist = document.getElementById('playlist');
1128	            const playlistContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-container');
1129	            
1130	            // Make sure playlist container is visible
1131	            if (playlistContainer) {
1132	                playlistContainer.style.display = 'block';
1133	            }
1134	            
1135	            // Show loading state
1136	            if (playlist) {
1137	                playlist.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Creating playlist based on this track...</div>';
1138	            }
1139	            
1140	            // Reset any status messages
1141	            const analyzeStatus = document.getElementById('analyze-status');
1142	            if (analyzeStatus) {
1143	                analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '<div class="status-progress">Creating station...</div>';
1144	            }
1145	            
1146	            // Fetch the station tracks
1147	            fetch(`/station/${trackId}`)
1148	                .then(response => response.json())
1149	                .then(data => {
1150	                    if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
1151	                        // Store tracks in currentPlaylist
1152	                        currentPlaylist = data;
1153	                        
1154	                        // Display track list
1155	                        displayPlaylist(data);
1156	                        
1157	                        // Set seed track information for header
1158	                        const seedTrack = data[0];
1159	                        const playlistHeader = document.querySelector('.playlist-header h2');
1160	                        
1161	                        if (playlistHeader) {
1162	                            playlistHeader.textContent = `Station: ${seedTrack.artist || 'Unknown'} - ${seedTrack.title || 'Unknown'} (${data.length} tracks)`;
1163	                        }
1164	                        
1165	                        // Enable save button
1166	                        const savePlaylistBtn = document.getElementById('save-playlist-btn');
1167	                        if (savePlaylistBtn) {
1168	                            savePlaylistBtn.disabled = false;
1169	                        }
1170	                        
1171	                        // Clear status
1172	                        if (analyzeStatus) {
1173	                            analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '';
1174	                        }
1175	                    } else if (data.error) {
1176	                        if (analyzeStatus) {
1177	                            analyzeStatus.innerHTML = `<div class="status-error">Error: ${data.error}</div>`;
1178	                        }
1179	                        if (playlist) {
1180	                            playlist.innerHTML = `<div class="error">Error creating station: ${data.error}</div>`;
1181	                        }
1182	                    } else {
1183	                        if (analyzeStatus) {
1184	                            analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '<div class="status-error">Invalid response from server</div>';
1185	                        }
1186	                        if (playlist) {
1187	                            playlist.innerHTML = '<div class="error">Failed to create station</div>';
1188	                        }
1189	                    }
1190	                })
1191	                .catch(error => {
1192	                    console.error('Error creating station:', error);
1193	                    if (analyzeStatus) {
1194	                        analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '<div class="status-error">Failed to create playlist</div>';
1195	                    }
1196	                    if (playlist) {
1197	                        playlist.innerHTML = `<div class="error">Error: ${error}</div>`;
1198	                    }
1199	                });
1200	        }
