1203	        function displayPlaylist(tracks) {
1204	            console.log('Displaying playlist:', tracks);
1205	            const playlist = document.getElementById('playlist');
1206	            
1207	            if (!playlist) {
1208	                console.error('Playlist container not found');
1209	                return;
1210	            }
1211	            
1212	            playlist.innerHTML = '';
1213	            
1214	            if (!Array.isArray(tracks) || tracks.length === 0) {
1215	                playlist.innerHTML = '<p>No tracks in playlist.</p>';
1216	                return;
1217	            }
1218	            
1219	            // Add Play All button at the top
1220	            const playAllContainer = document.createElement('div');
1221	            playAllContainer.className = 'playlist-controls';
1222	            playAllContainer.innerHTML = `
1223	                <button id="play-all-button" class="primary-button">
1224	                    ▶️ Play All (${tracks.length} tracks)
1225	                </button>
1226	            `;
1227	            playlist.appendChild(playAllContainer);
1228	            
1229	            // Add event listener to Play All button
1230	            const playAllButton = document.getElementById('play-all-button');
1231	            if (playAllButton) {
1232	                playAllButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
1233	                    playEntirePlaylist(tracks);
1234	                });
1235	            }
1236	            
1237	            const tracksContainer = document.createElement('div');
1238	            tracksContainer.className = 'track-grid';
1239	            
1240	            tracks.forEach((track, index) => {
1241	                const trackCard = document.createElement('div');
1242	                trackCard.className = 'track-card';
1243	                
1244	                const artDiv = document.createElement('div');
1245	                artDiv.className = 'track-art';
1246	                
1247	                // Add album art if available
1248	                if (track.album_art_url) {
1249	                    const img = document.createElement('img');
1250	                    const imgUrl = track.album_art_url.startsWith('http') ? 
1251	                        `/albumart/${encodeURIComponent(track.album_art_url)}` : track.album_art_url;
1252	                    
1253	                    img.src = imgUrl;
1254	                    img.alt = track.album || track.title;
1255	                    img.onerror = function() {
1256	                        this.style.display = 'none';
1257	                        artDiv.classList.add('default-art');
1258	                        artDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="art-placeholder">${(track.artist || 'Unknown').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>`;
1259	                    };
1260	                    artDiv.appendChild(img);
1261	                } else {
1262	                    // No album art URL, use placeholder
1263	                    artDiv.classList.add('default-art');
1264	                    artDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="art-placeholder">${(track.artist || 'Unknown').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>`;
1265	                }
1266	                
1267	                const infoDiv = document.createElement('div');
1268	                infoDiv.className = 'track-info';
1269	                infoDiv.innerHTML = `
1270	                    <div class="track-title">${track.title || 'Unknown'}</div>
1271	                    <div class="track-artist">${track.artist || 'Unknown'}</div>
1272	                    <div class="track-album">${track.album || 'Unknown'}</div>
1273	                `;
1274	                
1275	                const actionsDiv = document.createElement('div');
1276	                actionsDiv.className = 'track-actions';
1277	                actionsDiv.innerHTML = `
1278	                    <div class="track-number">${index + 1}</div>
1279	                    <button class="play-track" data-id="${track.id}">Play</button>
1280	                `;
1281	                
1282	                trackCard.appendChild(artDiv);
1283	                trackCard.appendChild(infoDiv);
1284	                trackCard.appendChild(actionsDiv);
1285	                
1286	                tracksContainer.appendChild(trackCard);
1287	            });
1288	            
1289	            playlist.appendChild(tracksContainer);
1290	        }

1301	        // Add these supporting functions
1302	        function loadPlaylist(playlistId) {
1303	            console.log(`Loading playlist ${playlistId}`);
1304	            if (analyzeStatus) {
1305	                showAnalyzeStatus('Loading playlist...', 'progress');
1306	            }
1307	            
1308	            // Updated URL from /playlists/${playlistId} to match backend route
1309	            fetch(`/playlists/${playlistId}`)
1310	                .then(response => response.json())
1311	                .then(data => {
1312	                    console.log('Loaded playlist:', data);
1313	                    if (data.error) {
1314	                        showAnalyzeStatus(`Error: ${data.error}`, 'error');
1315	                        return;
1316	                    }
1317	                    
1318	                    // Store current playlist
1319	                    currentPlaylist = data.tracks;
1320	                    
1321	                    // Display the playlist
1322	                    displayPlaylist(data.tracks);
1323	                    
1324	                    // Update header with playlist name
1325	                    const playlistHeader = document.querySelector('.playlist-container h2');
1326	                    if (playlistHeader) {
1327	                        playlistHeader.textContent = `Playlist: ${data.name}`;
1328	                    }
1329	                    
1330	                    // Enable save button
1331	                    const savePlaylistBtn = document.getElementById('save-playlist-btn');
1332	                    if (savePlaylistBtn) {
1333	                        savePlaylistBtn.disabled = false;
1334	                    }
1335	                    
1336	                    // Clear status
1337	                    if (analyzeStatus) {
1338	                        setTimeout(() => {
1339	                            analyzeStatus.innerHTML = '';
1340	                        }, 2000);
1341	                    }
1342	                })
1343	                .catch(error => {
1344	                    console.error('Error loading playlist:', error);
1345	                    showAnalyzeStatus('Failed to load playlist', 'error');
1346	                });
1347	        }

1353	            console.log(`Deleting playlist ${playlistId}`);
1354	            
1355	            // Updated URL from /playlists/${playlistId} to match backend route
1356	            fetch(`/playlists/${playlistId}`, {
1357	                method: 'DELETE'
1358	            })
1359	            .then(response => response.json())
1360	            .then(data => {

1362	                    alert(`Error: ${data.error}`);
1363	                } else {
1364	                    // Refresh playlist list
1365	                    loadPlaylists();
1366	                }
1367	            })
1368	            .catch(error => {
1369	                console.error('Error deleting playlist:', error);
1370	                alert('Failed to delete playlist');
1371	            });
1372	        }

1378	        // Define savePlaylist inside DOMContentLoaded
1379	        function savePlaylist() {
1380	            const playlistName = document.getElementById('playlist-name');
1381	            const playlistDescription = document.getElementById('playlist-description');
1382	            const savePlaylistModal = document.getElementById('save-playlist-modal');
1383	            
1384	            if (!playlistName) {
1385	                console.error('Playlist name element not found');
1386	                return;
1387	            }
1388	            
1389	            // Check if we're on the liked tracks page
1390	            const isLikedPage = window.location.href.includes('view=liked');
1391	            
1392	            // If currentPlaylist is empty but we're on liked page, try to reload liked tracks
1393	            if ((!window.currentPlaylist || window.currentPlaylist.length === 0) && isLikedPage) {
1394	                console.log('Reloading liked tracks for playlist save');
1395	                
1396	                fetch('/api/liked-tracks')
1397	                    .then(response => response.json())
1398	                    .then(data => {
1399	                        if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
1400	                            window.currentPlaylist = data;
1401	                            // Try saving again
1402	                            savePlaylist();
1403	                        } else {
1404	                            alert('No liked tracks found to save as playlist');
1405	                        }
1406	                    })
1407	                    .catch(error => {
1408	                        console.error('Error fetching liked tracks:', error);
1409	                        alert('Error fetching liked tracks');
1410	                    });
1411	                return;
1412	            }
1413	            
1414	            if (!window.currentPlaylist || window.currentPlaylist.length === 0) {
1415	                alert('Cannot save an empty playlist');
1416	                return;
1417	            }
1418	            
1419	            const name = playlistName.value.trim();
1420	            const description = playlistDescription ? playlistDescription.value.trim() : '';
1421	            
1422	            if (!name) {
1423	                alert('Please enter a playlist name');
1424	                return;
1425	            }
1426	            
1427	            // Get track IDs
1428	            const trackIds = window.currentPlaylist.map(track => track.id);
1429	            
1430	            // Save to server
1431	            console.log('Saving playlist:', { name, description, tracks: trackIds.length });
1432	            
1433	            // Updated URL from /api/playlists to the correct endpoint /playlists
1434	            fetch('/playlists', {
1435	                method: 'POST',
1436	                headers: {
1437	                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
1438	                },
1439	                body: JSON.stringify({
1440	                    name: name,
1441	                    description: description,
1442	                    tracks: trackIds
1443	                })
1444	            })
1445	            .then(response => response.json())
1446	            .then(data => {
1447	                if (data.error) {
1448	                    alert(`Error: ${data.error}`);
1449	                } else {
1450	                    // Close modal
1451	                    if (savePlaylistModal) {
1452	                        savePlaylistModal.style.display = 'none';
1453	                    }
1454	                    
1455	                    // Reset form
1456	                    if (playlistName) playlistName.value = '';
1457	                    if (playlistDescription) playlistDescription.value = '';
1458	                    
1459	                    // Show success notification
1460	                    alert(`Playlist "${name}" saved successfully`);
1461	                    
1462	                    // Refresh playlist list in sidebar
1463	                    if (typeof window.loadSidebarPlaylists === 'function') {
1464	                        window.loadSidebarPlaylists();
1465	                    }
1466	                }
1467	            })
1468	            .catch(error => {
1469	                console.error('Error saving playlist:', error);
1470	                alert('Failed to save playlist');
1471	            });
1472	        }
1473	        
1474	        // Expose the inner savePlaylist function to the window for any direct HTML onclick references
1475	        window.savePlaylist = savePlaylist;
1476

1527	    
1528	        // Debounce function to prevent excessive UI updates
1529	        function debounce(func, wait) {
1530	            let timeout;
1531	            return function(...args) {
1532	                const context = this;
1533	                clearTimeout(timeout);
1534	                timeout = setTimeout(() => func.apply(context, args), wait);
1535	            };
1536	        }
1537	    
1538	        // Use requestAnimationFrame for smoother progress bar updates
1539	        function updateSmoothProgress() {
1540	            if (!audioPlayer || !progressFill || !currentTimeDisplay) return;
1541	            
1542	            const currentTime = audioPlayer.currentTime;
1543	            const duration = audioPlayer.duration || 0;
1544	            
1545	            // Update progress bar
1546	            if (duration > 0) {
1547	                const percent = (currentTime / duration) * 100;
1548	                progressFill.style.width = `${percent}%`;
1549	            }
1550	            
1551	            // Only update time display every 500ms to reduce DOM updates
1552	            if (!updateSmoothProgress.lastUpdate || Date.now() - updateSmoothProgress.lastUpdate > 500) {
1553	                currentTimeDisplay.textContent = formatTime(currentTime);
1554	                updateSmoothProgress.lastUpdate = Date.now();
1555	            }
1556	            
1557	            // Schedule next update
1558	            if (!audioPlayer.paused) {
1559	                requestAnimationFrame(updateSmoothProgress);
1560	            }
1561	        }
1562	    
1563	        // Replace the existing updateProgress function
1564	        function updateProgress() {
1565	            requestAnimationFrame(updateSmoothProgress);
1566	        }
1567	    });

1570	    function displayPlaylistTracks(tracks) {

1572	        if (!resultsContainer) return;

1575	        resultsContainer.innerHTML = '';
1576

1578	            resultsContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="no-results">No tracks found in playlist</div>';
1579	            return;
1580	        }

1583	        const trackGrid = document.createElement('div');

1590	        });

1593	        resultsContainer.appendChild(trackGrid);

1596	    // Replace the createTrackCard function with this complete version
1597	    function createTrackCard(track) {
1598	        const trackCard = document.createElement('div');
1599	        trackCard.className = 'track-card';
1600	        
1601	        // Create album art container with image
1602	        const albumArt = document.createElement('div');
1603	        albumArt.className = 'album-art';
1604	        
1605	        const img = document.createElement('img');
1606	        if (track.album_art_url) {
1607	            let imgSrc = track.album_art_url;
1608	            
1609	            // If it starts with album_art_cache, convert to web-accessible URL
1610	            if (imgSrc.includes('album_art_cache/') || imgSrc.includes('album_art_cache\\')) {
1611	                // Extract the filename only
1612	                const parts = imgSrc.split(/[\/\\]/);
1613	                const filename = parts[parts.length - 1];
1614	                imgSrc = `/cache/${filename}`;
1615	            }
1616	            // If it's an external URL, route through proxy
1617	            else if (imgSrc.startsWith('http')) {
1618	                imgSrc = `/albumart/${encodeURIComponent(imgSrc)}`;
1619	            }
1620	            
1621	            img.src = imgSrc;
1622	        } else {
1623	            img.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png';
1624	        }
1625	        img.alt = 'Album Art';
1626	        img.onerror = function() {
1627	            this.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png';
1628	        };
1629	        
1630	        const playOverlay = document.createElement('div');
1631	        playOverlay.className = 'play-overlay';
1632	        playOverlay.innerHTML = '<i class="play-icon">▶</i>';
1633	        
1634	        albumArt.appendChild(img);
1635	        albumArt.appendChild(playOverlay);
1636	        trackCard.appendChild(albumArt);
1637	        
1638	        // Create track info container
1639	        const trackInfo = document.createElement('div');
1640	        trackInfo.className = 'track-info';
1641	        
1642	        const trackTitle = document.createElement('div');
1643	        trackTitle.className = 'track-title';
1644	        trackTitle.textContent = track.title || 'Unknown Title';
1645	        
1646	        const trackArtist = document.createElement('div');
1647	        trackArtist.className = 'track-artist';
1648	        trackArtist.textContent = track.artist || 'Unknown Artist';
1649	        
1650	        trackInfo.appendChild(trackTitle);
1651	        trackInfo.appendChild(trackArtist);
1652	        
1653	        // Create actions container with all buttons
1654	        const trackActions = document.createElement('div');
1655	        trackActions.className = 'track-actions';
1656	        
1657	        // Play button
1658	        const playButton = document.createElement('button');
1659	        playButton.className = 'play-track';
1660	        playButton.textContent = 'Play';
1661	        playButton.dataset.id = track.id;
1662	        playButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
1663	            e.stopPropagation();
1664	            if (typeof window.playTrack === 'function') {
1665	                window.playTrack(track.id);
1666	            } else if (typeof playTrack === 'function') {
1667	                playTrack(track);
1668	            }
1669	        });
1670	        
1671	        // Station button
1672	        const stationButton = document.createElement('button');
1673	        stationButton.className = 'create-station';
1674	        stationButton.textContent = 'Station';
1675	        stationButton.dataset.id = track.id;
1676	        stationButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
1677	            e.stopPropagation();
1678	            // Always show the playlist container when creating a station
1679	            const playlistContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-container');
1680	            if (playlistContainer) {
1681	                playlistContainer.style.display = 'block';
1682	            }
1683	            // Use the global createStation function
1684	            createStation(track.id);
1685	        });
1686	        
1687	        // Like button
1688	        const likeButton = document.createElement('button');
1689	        likeButton.className = track.liked ? 'track-like-button liked' : 'track-like-button';
1690	        likeButton.innerHTML = track.liked ? '♥' : '♡';
1691	        likeButton.title = track.liked ? 'Unlike' : 'Like';
1692	        likeButton.dataset.id = track.id;
1693	        likeButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
1694	            e.stopPropagation();
1695	            toggleLikeStatus(track.id);
1696	        });
1697	        
1698	        // Add all buttons to actions container
1699	        trackActions.appendChild(playButton);
1700	        trackActions.appendChild(stationButton);
1701	        trackActions.appendChild(likeButton);
1702	        
1703	        // Add actions to track info
1704	        trackInfo.appendChild(trackActions);
1705	        trackCard.appendChild(trackInfo);
1706	        
1707	        // Make the whole card clickable to play
1708	        trackCard.addEventListener('click', function() {
1709	            if (typeof window.playTrack === 'function') {
1710	                window.playTrack(track.id);
1711	            } else if (typeof playTrack === 'function') {
1712	                playTrack(track);
1713	            }
1714	        });
1715	        
1716	        return trackCard;
1717	    }

1719	    // Add this helper function
1720	    function toggleLikeStatus(trackId) {
1721	        if (!trackId) return;
1722	    
1723	        console.log('Toggling like status for track:', trackId);
1724	        
1725	        // Send request to toggle like status without content type header
1726	        fetch(`/api/tracks/${trackId}/like`, {
1727	            method: 'POST'
1728	        })
1729	        .then(response => response.json())
1730	        .then(data => {
1731	            console.log('Like status toggled:', data);
1732	            
1733	            // Update the UI
1734	            const likeButtons = document.querySelectorAll(`.track-like-button[data-id="${trackId}"]`);
1735	            likeButtons.forEach(button => {
1736	                if (data.liked) {
1737	                    button.classList.add('liked');
1738	                    button.innerHTML = '♥';
1739	                    button.title = 'Unlike';
1740	                } else {
1741	                    button.classList.remove('liked');
1742	                    button.innerHTML = '♡';
1743	                    button.title = 'Like';
1744	                }
1745	            });
1746	        })
1747	        .catch(error => {
1748	            console.error('Error toggling like status:', error);
1749	        });
1750	    }

1752	    // Update the image handling logic
1753	    function displayTrack(track) {
1754	        if (!track) return;
1755	        
1756	        const trackElement = document.createElement('div');
1757	        trackElement.className = 'track-item';
1758	        
1759	        // Create track art container
1760	        const artContainer = document.createElement('div');
1761	        artContainer.className = 'track-art';
1762	        
1763	        // Create img element
1764	        const img = document.createElement('img');
1765	        img.alt = track.title || 'Track';
1766	        
1767	        if (track.album_art_url) {
1768	            let imgSrc = track.album_art_url;
1769	            
1770	            // If it starts with album_art_cache, convert to web-accessible URL
1771	            if (imgSrc.includes('album_art_cache/') || imgSrc.includes('album_art_cache\\')) {
1772	                // Extract the filename only
1773	                const parts = imgSrc.split(/[\/\\]/);
1774	                const filename = parts[parts.length - 1];
1775	                imgSrc = `/cache/${filename}`;
1776	            }
1777	            // If it's an external URL, route through proxy
1778	            else if (imgSrc.startsWith('http')) {
1779	                imgSrc = `/albumart/${encodeURIComponent(imgSrc)}`;
1780	            }
1781	            
1782	            img.src = imgSrc;
1783	        } else {
1784	            // Use default image if no album art available
1785	            img.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png';
1786	        }
1787	        
1788	        // Handle image loading errors
1789	        img.onerror = function() {
1790	            this.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png';
1791	        };
1792	        
1793	        artContainer.appendChild(img);
1794	        trackElement.appendChild(artContainer);
1795	        
1796	        // Create track info container
1797	        const infoContainer = document.createElement('div');
1798	        infoContainer.className = 'track-info';
1799	        
1800	        // Add track title
1801	        const titleElement = document.createElement('div');
1802	        titleElement.className = 'track-title';
1803	        titleElement.textContent = track.title || 'Unknown Title';
1804	        
1805	        // Add track artist
1806	        const artistElement = document.createElement('div');
1807	        artistElement.className = 'track-artist';
1808	        artistElement.textContent = track.artist || 'Unknown Artist';
1809	        
1810	        // Add track album
1811	        const albumElement = document.createElement('div');
1812	        albumElement.className = 'track-album';
1813	        albumElement.textContent = track.album || 'Unknown Album';
1814	        
1815	        // Add to info container
1816	        infoContainer.appendChild(titleElement);
1817	        infoContainer.appendChild(artistElement);
1818	        infoContainer.appendChild(albumElement);
1819	        
1820	        // Create actions container
1821	        const actionsContainer = document.createElement('div');
1822	        actionsContainer.className = 'track-actions';
1823	        
1824	        // Add play button
1825	        const playButton = document.createElement('button');
1826	        playButton.className = 'play-button';
1827	        playButton.innerHTML = '▶';
1828	        playButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
1829	            e.stopPropagation();
1830	            playTrack(track);
1831	        });
1832	        
1833	        // Add like button
1834	        const likeButton = document.createElement('button');
1835	        likeButton.className = track.liked ? 'track-like-button liked' : 'track-like-button';
1836	        likeButton.innerHTML = track.liked ? '♥' : '♡';
1837	        likeButton.title = track.liked ? 'Unlike' : 'Like';
1838	        likeButton.setAttribute('data-id', track.id);
1839	        likeButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
1840	            e.stopPropagation();
1841	            toggleLikeStatus(track.id);
1842	        });
1843	        
1844	        actionsContainer.appendChild(playButton);
1845	        actionsContainer.appendChild(likeButton);
1846	        
1847	        // Add to track element
1848	        trackElement.appendChild(infoContainer);
1849	        trackElement.appendChild(actionsContainer);
1850	        
1851	        // Add click event to play track when clicking on the track element
1852	        trackElement.addEventListener('click', function() {
1853	            playTrack(track);
1854	        });
1855	        
1856	        return trackElement;
1857	    }
1858	    
1859	    // Update the loadLiked function to preserve station functionality
1860	    
1861	    function loadLiked() {
1862	        console.log('loadLiked called');
1863	        
1864	        // Update heading
1865	        const resultsHeading = document.getElementById('results-heading');
1866	        if (resultsHeading) {
1867	            resultsHeading.textContent = 'Liked Tracks';
1868	        }
1869	        
1870	        const searchResults = document.getElementById('search-results');
1871	        // Show loading indicator
1872	        if (searchResults) {
1873	            searchResults.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading liked tracks...</div>';
1874	        }
1875	        
1876	        // Hide the playlist container initially, but keep it in the DOM
1877	        const playlistContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-container');
1878	        if (playlistContainer) {
1879	            playlistContainer.style.display = 'none';
1880	        }
1881	        
1882	        // Fetch liked tracks
1883	        fetch('/api/liked-tracks')
1884	            .then(response => response.json())
1885	            .then(data => {
1886	                console.log('Liked tracks data:', data);
1887	                
1888	                // Handle empty results
1889	                if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length === 0) {
1890	                    if (searchResults) {
1891	                        searchResults.innerHTML = `
1892	                            <div class="empty-state">
1893	                                <h3>No liked tracks found</h3>
1894	                                <p>Like some tracks to see them appear here.</p>
1895	                            </div>
1896	                        `;
1897	                    }
1898	                    return;
1899	                }
1900	                
1901	                // Ensure all tracks have the liked property set to true
1902	                const likedTracks = data.map(track => ({
1903	                    ...track,
1904	                    liked: true
1905	                }));
1906	                
1907	                // Set currentPlaylist so Save Playlist works
1908	                window.currentPlaylist = likedTracks;
1909	                
1910	                // Enable save playlist button
1911	                const savePlaylistBtn = document.getElementById('save-playlist-btn');
1912	                if (savePlaylistBtn) {
1913	                    savePlaylistBtn.disabled = false;
1914	                }
1915	                
1916	                // Display the tracks in a grid format
1917	                displayTracksInGrid(likedTracks, searchResults);
1918	            })
1919	            .catch(error => {
1920	                console.error('Error loading liked tracks:', error);
1921	                if (searchResults) {
1922	                    searchResults.innerHTML = `<div class="error">Error loading liked tracks: ${error}</div>`;
1923	                }
1924	            });
1925	    }
1926	    
1927	    // Helper function to display tracks in a grid
1928	    function displayTracksInGrid(tracks, container) {
1929	        if (!container) return;
1930	        
1931	        container.innerHTML = '';
1932	        
1933	        // Create track grid
1934	        const trackGrid = document.createElement('div');
1935	        trackGrid.className = 'track-grid';
1936	        
1937	        // Add tracks to grid
1938	        tracks.forEach(track => {
1939	            const trackCard = createTrackCard(track);
1940	            trackGrid.appendChild(trackCard);
1941	        });
1942	        
1943	        // Add grid to container
1944	        container.appendChild(trackGrid);
1945	        
1946	        // Add a "Play All Liked" button instead of Save as Playlist
1947	        const actionButton = document.createElement('div');
1948	        actionButton.className = 'liked-actions-container';
1949	        actionButton.innerHTML = `
1950	            <button class="primary-button" id="play-all-liked-btn">Play All Liked Tracks</button>
1951	        `;
1952	        container.insertBefore(actionButton, trackGrid);
1953	        
1954	        // Add event listener to the play all button
1955	        document.getElementById('play-all-liked-btn').addEventListener('click', function() {
1956	            if (window.currentPlaylist && window.currentPlaylist.length > 0) {
1957	                // Use the existing playEntirePlaylist function if available
1958	                if (typeof window.playEntirePlaylist === 'function') {
1959	                    window.playEntirePlaylist(window.currentPlaylist);
1960	                } else {
1961	                    // Fallback - play the first track
1962	                    if (typeof window.playTrack === 'function' && window.currentPlaylist[0]) {
1963	                        window.playTrack(window.currentPlaylist[0].id);
1964	                    }
1965	                }
1966	            }
1967	        });
1968	    }
1969	};

1976	window.loadExplore = function() {
1977	    console.log('Global loadExplore called');
1978	    
1979	    // Update heading
1980	    const resultsHeading = document.getElementById('results-heading');
1981	    if (resultsHeading) {
1982	        resultsHeading.textContent = 'Discover';
1983	    }
1984	    
1985	    const searchResults = document.getElementById('search-results');
1986	    if (!searchResults) {
1987	        console.error('search-results element not found');
1988	        return;
1989	    }
1990	    
1991	    // Show loading indicator
1992	    searchResults.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading tracks...</div>';
1993	    
1994	    // Hide the playlist container without recreating it
1995	    const playlistContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-container');
1996	    if (playlistContainer) {
1997	        playlistContainer.style.display = 'none';
1998	    }
1999	    
2000	    // Fetch discover data from the API endpoint instead of /explore
2001	    console.log('Fetching from /api/discover endpoint');
2002	    fetch('/api/discover')
2003	        .then(response => {
2004	            console.log('Discover response received:', response);
2005	            if (!response.ok) {
2006	                throw new Error(`HTTP error ${response.status}`);
2007	            }
2008	            return response.json();
2009	        })
2010	        .then(data => {
2011	            console.log('Discover data received:', data);
2012	            if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
2013	                // Limit to 12 items for better performance
2014	                const displayData = data.slice(0, 12);
2015	                window.currentPlaylist = displayData;
2016	                displaySearchResults(displayData);
2017	            } else if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length === 0) {
2018	                searchResults.innerHTML = '<p>No tracks found. Try adding some music!</p>';
2019	            } else if (data.error) {
2020	                searchResults.innerHTML = `<p>Error: ${data.error}</p>`;
2021	            }
2022	        })
2023	        .catch(error => {
2024	            console.error('Error loading discover tracks:', error);
2025	            if (searchResults) {
2026	                searchResults.innerHTML = '<p>Failed to load tracks. See console for details.</p>';
2027	            }
2028	        });
2029	};

2031	window.loadRecent = function() {
2032	    console.log('Global loadRecent called');
2033	    
2034	    // Update heading
2035	    const resultsHeading = document.getElementById('results-heading');
2036	    if (resultsHeading) {
2037	        resultsHeading.textContent = 'Recently Added';
2038	    }
2039	    
2040	    const searchResults = document.getElementById('search-results');
2041	    if (!searchResults) {
2042	        console.error('search-results element not found');
2043	        return;
2044	    }
2045	    
2046	    // Show loading indicator
2047	    searchResults.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading recent tracks...</div>';
2048	    
2049	    // Hide the playlist container without recreating it
2050	    const playlistContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-container');
2051	    if (playlistContainer) {
2052	        playlistContainer.style.display = 'none';
2053	    }
2054	    
2055	    // Fetch recent tracks
2056	    fetch('/recent')
2057	        .then(response => response.json())
2058	        .then(data => {
2059	            console.log('Recent data received:', data);
2060	            if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
2061	                // Process the tracks - convert arrays to objects if needed
2062	                let processedTracks;
2063	                
2064	                // Check if we have an array of arrays (numbered indices)
2065	                if (Array.isArray(data[0])) {
2066	                    console.log('Converting array format to object format for recent tracks');
2067	                    processedTracks = data.map(trackArray => {
2068	                        return {
2069	                            id: trackArray[0],
2070	                            file_path: trackArray[1],
2071	                            title: trackArray[2] || 'Unknown Title',
2072	                            artist: trackArray[3] || 'Unknown Artist',
2073	                            album: trackArray[4] || 'Unknown Album',
2074	                            album_art_url: trackArray[5],
2075	                            duration: trackArray[6]
2076	                        };
2077	                    });
2078	                } else {
2079	                    // Already in object format
2080	                    processedTracks = data;
2081	                }
2082	                
2083	                // Limit to 12 items for better performance
2084	                const displayData = processedTracks.slice(0, 12);
2085	                
2086	                // Save in current playlist for potential saving
2087	                window.currentPlaylist = displayData;
2088	                
2089	                // Display the tracks
2090	                displaySearchResults(displayData);
2091	            } else if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length === 0) {
2092	                searchResults.innerHTML = '<p>No recent tracks found. Try adding some music!</p>';
2093	            } else if (data.error) {
2094	                searchResults.innerHTML = `<p>Error: ${data.error}</p>`;
2095	            }
2096	        })
2097	        .catch(error => {
2098	            console.error('Error loading recent tracks:', error);
2099	            if (searchResults) {
2100	                searchResults.innerHTML = '<p>Failed to load recent tracks. See console for details.</p>';
2101	            }
2102	        });
2103	};

2190	// Make displaySearchResults globally accessible for all views
2191	function displaySearchResults(tracks) {
2192	    console.log('Displaying tracks:', tracks);
2193	    const searchResults = document.getElementById('search-results');
2194	    
2195	    if (!searchResults) {
2196	        console.error('search-results element not found');
2197	        return;
2198	    }
2199	    
2200	    searchResults.innerHTML = '';
2201	    
2202	    if (!Array.isArray(tracks) || tracks.length === 0) {
2203	        searchResults.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>No tracks found</h3><p>Try another search or add more music to your library.</p></div>';
2204	        return;
2205	    }
2206	    
2207	    const resultsContainer = document.createElement('div');
2208	    resultsContainer.className = 'track-grid';
2209	    
2210	    tracks.forEach(track => {
2211	        const trackCard = createTrackCard(track);
2212	        resultsContainer.appendChild(trackCard);
2213	    });
2214	    
2215	    searchResults.appendChild(resultsContainer);
2216	}

2218	// Create track card function
2219	function createTrackCard(track) {
2220	    const trackCard = document.createElement('div');
2221	    trackCard.className = 'track-card';
2222	    
2223	    // Create album art container with image
2224	    const albumArt = document.createElement('div');
2225	    albumArt.className = 'album-art';
2226	    
2227	    const img = document.createElement('img');
2228	    if (track.album_art_url) {
2229	        img.src = track.album_art_url;
2230	    } else {
2231	        img.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png';
2232	    }
2233	    img.alt = 'Album Art';
2234	    img.onerror = function() {
2235	        this.src = '/static/images/default-album-art.png';
2236	    };
2237	    
2238	    const playOverlay = document.createElement('div');
2239	    playOverlay.className = 'play-overlay';
2240	    playOverlay.innerHTML = '<i class="play-icon">▶</i>';
2241	    
2242	    albumArt.appendChild(img);
2243	    albumArt.appendChild(playOverlay);
2244	    trackCard.appendChild(albumArt);
2245	    
2246	    // Create track info container
2247	    const trackInfo = document.createElement('div');
2248	    trackInfo.className = 'track-info';
2249	    
2250	    const trackTitle = document.createElement('div');
2251	    trackTitle.className = 'track-title';
2252	    trackTitle.textContent = track.title || 'Unknown Title';
2253	    
2254	    const trackArtist = document.createElement('div');
2255	    trackArtist.className = 'track-artist';
2256	    trackArtist.textContent = track.artist || 'Unknown Artist';
2257	    
2258	    trackInfo.appendChild(trackTitle);
2259	    trackInfo.appendChild(trackArtist);
2260	    trackCard.appendChild(trackInfo);
2261	    
2262	    // Create actions container with all buttons
2263	    const trackActions = document.createElement('div');
2264	    trackActions.className = 'track-actions';
2265	    
2266	    // Play button
2267	    const playButton = document.createElement('button');
2268	    playButton.className = 'play-track';
2269	    playButton.textContent = 'Play';
2270	    playButton.dataset.id = track.id;
2271	    playButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
2272	        e.stopPropagation();
2273	        window.playTrack(track.id);
2274	    });
2275	    
2276	    // Station button
2277	    const stationButton = document.createElement('button');
2278	    stationButton.className = 'create-station';
2279	    stationButton.textContent = 'Station';
2280	    stationButton.dataset.id = track.id;
2281	    stationButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
2282	        e.stopPropagation();
2283	        createStation(track.id);
2284	    });
2285	    
2286	    // Like button
2287	    const likeButton = document.createElement('button');
2288	    likeButton.className = track.liked ? 'track-like-button liked' : 'track-like-button';
2289	    likeButton.innerHTML = track.liked ? '♥' : '♡';
2290	    likeButton.title = track.liked ? 'Unlike' : 'Like';
2291	    likeButton.dataset.id = track.id;
2292	    likeButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
2293	        e.stopPropagation();
2294	        toggleLikeStatus(track.id);
2295	    });
2296	    
2297	    // Add all buttons to actions container
2298	    trackActions.appendChild(playButton);
2299	    trackActions.appendChild(stationButton);
2300	    trackActions.appendChild(likeButton);
2301	    
2302	    trackCard.appendChild(trackActions);
2303	    
2304	    // Add click event to play track when clicking card
2305	    trackCard.addEventListener('click', function() {
2306	        window.playTrack(track.id);
2307	    });
2308	    
2309	    return trackCard;
2310	}

2448	    if (resultsHeading) {
2449	        resultsHeading.textContent = 'Liked Tracks';
2450	    }

2453	    if (!searchResults) {

2456	    }

2459	    searchResults.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading liked tracks...</div>';
2460

2462	    const playlistContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-container');
2463	    if (playlistContainer) {
2464	        playlistContainer.style.display = 'none';
2465	    }
2466	    
2467	    // Fetch liked tracks
2468	    fetch('/api/liked-tracks')
2469	        .then(response => response.json())
2470	        .then(data => {
2471	            console.log('Liked tracks data received:', data);
2472

2474	            if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length === 0) {
2475	                if (searchResults) {
2476	                    searchResults.innerHTML = `
2477	                        <div class="empty-state">
2478	                            <h3>No liked tracks found</h3>
2479	                            <p>Like some tracks to see them appear here.</p>
2480	                        </div>
2481	                    `;
2482	                }
2483	                return;
2484	            }
2485	            
2486	            // Process the tracks - convert arrays to objects if needed
2487	            let processedTracks;
2488	            
2489	            // Check if we have an array of arrays (numbered indices)
2490	            if (Array.isArray(data[0])) {
2491	                console.log('Converting array format to object format');
2492	                processedTracks = data.map(trackArray => {
2493	                    return {
2494	                        id: trackArray[0],
2495	                        file_path: trackArray[1],
2496	                        title: trackArray[2] || 'Unknown Title',
2497	                        artist: trackArray[3] || 'Unknown Artist',
2498	                        album: trackArray[4] || 'Unknown Album',
2499	                        album_art_url: trackArray[5],
2500	                        duration: trackArray[6],
2501	                        liked: true
2502	                    };
2503	                });
2504	            } else {
2505	                // Already in object format, just ensure liked property is true
2506	                processedTracks = data.map(track => ({
2507	                    ...track,
2508	                    liked: true
2509	                }));
2510	            }
2511	            
2512	            // Save current playlist for save function
2513	            window.currentPlaylist = processedTracks;
2514	            
2515	            // Display the tracks
2516	            displaySearchResults(processedTracks);
2517	        })
2518	        .catch(error => {
2519	            console.error('Error loading liked tracks:', error);
2520	            if (searchResults) {
2521	                searchResults.innerHTML = `<div class="error">Error loading liked tracks: ${error}</div>`;
2522	            }
2523	        });
2524	};

2559	// Add create station function
2560	function createStation(trackId) {
2561	    if (!trackId) return;
2562	    
2563	    console.log('Creating station for track ID:', trackId);
2564	    
2565	    // Show the playlist container if it exists
2566	    const playlistContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-container');
2567	    if (playlistContainer) {
2568	        playlistContainer.style.display = 'block';
2569	    }
2570	    
2571	    // Show loading state in the playlist
2572	    const playlist = document.getElementById('playlist');
2573	    if (playlist) {
2574	        playlist.innerHTML = '<div class="loading-large">Generating station...</div>';
2575	    }
2576	    
2577	    // Get configured station size from settings if available
2578	    const stationSize = window.stationSize || 20;    
2579	    // Fetch station tracks from our API endpoint - UPDATED TO USE CORRECT ENDPOINT
2580	    fetch(`/api/station/${trackId}?num_tracks=${stationSize}`)
2581	        .then(response => {
2582	            if (!response.ok) {
2583	                throw new Error(`Station request failed: ${response.status}`);
2584	            }
2585	            return response.json();
2586	        })
2587	        .then(data => {
2588	            console.log('Station created with tracks:', data);
2589	            
2590	            // Process the tracks - convert arrays to objects
2591	            const processedTracks = data.map(trackArray => {
2592	                // Map array indices to properties based on database schema
2593	                return {
2594	                    id: trackArray[0],
2595	                    file_path: trackArray[1],
2596	                    title: trackArray[2] || 'Unknown Title',
2597	                    artist: trackArray[3] || 'Unknown Artist',
2598	                    album: trackArray[4] || 'Unknown Album',
2599	                    genre: trackArray[5],
2600	                    duration: trackArray[7],
2601	                    album_art_url: trackArray[11]
2602	                };
2603	            });
2604	            
2605	            // Save current playlist
2606	            window.currentPlaylist = processedTracks;
2607	            
2608	            // Display the tracks in the playlist
2609	            displayPlaylist(processedTracks);
2610	            
2611	            // Auto-play the first track if desired
2612	            if (processedTracks.length > 0 && window.autoPlayStation) {
2613	                playTrackFromPlaylist(0);
2614	            }
2615	        })
2616	        .catch(error => {
2617	            console.error('Error creating station:', error);
2618	            if (playlist) {
2619	                playlist.innerHTML = `<div class="error">Error creating station: ${error.message}</div>`;
2620	            }
2621	        });
2622	}

2700	// Add this function to handle saving playlists - this replaces the existing version if present
2701	function saveCurrentPlaylist() {
2702	    console.log('saveCurrentPlaylist called');
2703	    
2704	    // Check if we have a playlist to save
2705	    if (!window.currentPlaylist || window.currentPlaylist.length === 0) {
2706	        alert('No playlist to save');
2707	        return;
2708	    }
2709	    
2710	    // Show the save playlist modal
2711	    const savePlaylistModal = document.getElementById('save-playlist-modal');
2712	    if (savePlaylistModal) {
2713	        savePlaylistModal.style.display = 'block';
2714	        
2715	        // Set up the form submission
2716	        const savePlaylistForm = document.getElementById('save-playlist-form');
2717	        
2718	        // Remove any previous event listeners
2719	        const newForm = savePlaylistForm.cloneNode(true);
2720	        savePlaylistForm.parentNode.replaceChild(newForm, savePlaylistForm);
2721	        
2722	        // Get fresh references to the NEW form elements after replacement
2723	        const playlistNameInput = document.getElementById('playlist-name');
2724	        const playlistDescriptionInput = document.getElementById('playlist-description');
2725	        
2726	        // Add new event listener
2727	        newForm.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
2728	            e.preventDefault();
2729	            
2730	            const name = playlistNameInput.value.trim();
2731	            const description = playlistDescriptionInput.value.trim();
2732	            
2733	            console.log('Saving playlist with name:', name);
2734	            
2735	            if (!name) {
2736	                alert('Please enter a playlist name');
2737	                return;
2738	            }
2739	            
2740	            // Get track IDs from the current playlist
2741	            const trackIds = window.currentPlaylist.map(track => track.id);
2742	            
2743	            // Send request to save playlist
2744	            console.log('Sending save request with tracks:', trackIds.length);
2745	            fetch('/api/playlists', {
2746	                method: 'POST',
2747	                headers: {
2748	                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
2749	                },
2750	                body: JSON.stringify({
2751	                    name: name,
2752	                    description: description,
2753	                    tracks: trackIds
2754	                })
2755	            })
2756	            .then(response => response.json())
2757	            .then(data => {
2758	                console.log('Playlist saved response:', data);
2759	                
2760	                // Show success message
2761	                alert(`Playlist "${name}" saved successfully!`);
2762	                
2763	                // Hide the modal
2764	                savePlaylistModal.style.display = 'none';
2765	                
2766	                // Reset form
2767	                playlistNameInput.value = '';
2768	                if (playlistDescriptionInput) {
2769	                    playlistDescriptionInput.value = '';
2770	                }
2771	                
2772	                // Refresh the playlists in sidebar
2773	                if (typeof window.loadSidebarPlaylists === 'function') {
2774	                    window.loadSidebarPlaylists();
2775	                }
2776	            })
2777	            .catch(error => {
2778	                console.error('Error saving playlist:', error);
2779	                alert(`Error saving playlist: ${error.message}`);
2780	            });
2781	        });
2782	    } else {
2783	        console.error('Save playlist modal not found');
2784	        alert('Save playlist feature is not available');
2785	    }
2786	}
2787	
2788	// Make the function globally available
2789	window.saveCurrentPlaylist = saveCurrentPlaylist;

0001	/**
0002	 * Save Button Fix - Immediate fix for the Save Playlist button's disabled state
0003	 * This script removes the disabled attribute and ensures event listeners are properly attached
0004	 */
0005	
0006	(function() {
0007	    console.log('Save Button Fix: Script loaded');
0008	    
0009	    // Function to fix the save button
0010	    function fixSaveButton() {
0011	        console.log('Save Button Fix: Attempting to fix the button');
0012	        
0013	        // Get the save playlist button
0014	        const savePlaylistBtn = document.getElementById('save-playlist-btn');
0015	        
0016	        if (!savePlaylistBtn) {
0017	            console.error('Save Button Fix: Button not found!');
0018	            return false;
0019	        }
0020	        
0021	        console.log('Save Button Fix: Button found, current disabled state:', savePlaylistBtn.disabled);
0022	        
0023	        // Remove the disabled attribute
0024	        savePlaylistBtn.removeAttribute('disabled');
0025	        
0026	        // Check if event listeners are attached by setting a flag
0027	        if (!savePlaylistBtn._hasFixedClickHandler) {
0028	            console.log('Save Button Fix: Adding click event listener');
0029	            
0030	            // Add a direct click handler
0031	            savePlaylistBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
0032	                console.log('Save Button Fix: Button clicked');
0033	                
0034	                // If there's an existing handler, try to call it
0035	                if (typeof window.saveCurrentPlaylist === 'function') {
0036	                    console.log('Save Button Fix: Calling saveCurrentPlaylist function');
0037	                    window.saveCurrentPlaylist();
0038	                } else {
0039	                    console.log('Save Button Fix: No saveCurrentPlaylist function found, showing modal directly');
0040	                    // Fallback to showing the modal directly
0041	                    const modal = document.getElementById('save-playlist-modal');
0042	                    if (modal) {
0043	                        modal.style.display = 'block';
0044	                    } else {
0045	                        console.error('Save Button Fix: Modal not found');
0046	                        alert('Save playlist modal not found');
0047	                    }
0048	                }
0049	            });
0050	            
0051	            // Mark that we've added our handler
0052	            savePlaylistBtn._hasFixedClickHandler = true;
0053	        }
0054	        
0055	        // Ensure the button is visible and styled properly
0056	        savePlaylistBtn.style.opacity = '1';
0057	        savePlaylistBtn.style.cursor = 'pointer';
0058	        
0059	        console.log('Save Button Fix: Button fixed, new disabled state:', savePlaylistBtn.disabled);
0060	        
0061	        return true;
0062	    }
0063	    
0064	    // Try to fix as soon as DOM is available
0065	    function init() {
0066	        console.log('Save Button Fix: Initializing');
0067	        
0068	        if (!fixSaveButton()) {
0069	            // If failed, retry after a delay (button might be loaded dynamically)
0070	            console.log('Save Button Fix: Retrying in 500ms');
0071	            setTimeout(fixSaveButton, 500);
0072	            
0073	            // And one more time after a longer delay if needed
0074	            setTimeout(fixSaveButton, 1500);
0075	        }
0076	    }
0077	    
0078	    // Initialize immediately if DOM is already loaded
0079	    if (document.readyState === 'complete' || document.readyState === 'interactive') {
0080	        init();
0081	    } else {
0082	        // Otherwise wait for DOM to be ready
0083	        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
0084	    }
0085	    
0086	    // Also try when window is fully loaded
0087	    window.addEventListener('load', function() {
0088	        console.log('Save Button Fix: Window load event fired');
0089	        fixSaveButton();
0090	    });
0091	})();

0001	// Debug Script for save-playlist button
0002	console.log('Debug script loaded for save-playlist button');
0003	
0004	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0005	  console.log('DOM loaded, looking for save-playlist button');
0006	  const savePlaylistBtn = document.getElementById('save-playlist-btn');
0007	  
0008	  if (savePlaylistBtn) {
0009	    console.log('Found Save Playlist button, attaching event listener');
0010	    
0011	    // Force remove disabled attribute
0012	    savePlaylistBtn.removeAttribute('disabled');
0013	    
0014	    // Add direct click event listener
0015	    savePlaylistBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
0016	      e.preventDefault();
0017	      console.log('Save playlist button clicked');
0018	      
0019	      // Get playlist name from input or modal
0020	      let playlistName = '';
0021	      const playlistNameInput = document.getElementById('playlist-name');
0022	      
0023	      if (playlistNameInput && playlistNameInput.value.trim()) {
0024	        playlistName = playlistNameInput.value.trim();
0025	      } else {
0026	        playlistName = prompt('Enter a name for this playlist:');
0027	        if (!playlistName) return; // User cancelled
0028	      }
0029	      
0030	      console.log('Attempting to save playlist:', playlistName);
0031	      
0032	      // Get tracks from currentPlaylist
0033	      let tracks = [];
0034	      
0035	      // Try different sources for the tracks
0036	      if (window.currentPlaylist && window.currentPlaylist.length) {
0037	        console.log('Using window.currentPlaylist');
0038	        tracks = window.currentPlaylist.map(track => track.id || track);
0039	      } else if (window.playerManager) {
0040	        console.log('Using playerManager.getQueue()');
0041	        const queue = window.playerManager.getQueue();
0042	        if (queue && queue.length) {
0043	          tracks = queue.map(track => track.id || track);
0044	        }
0045	      }
0046	      
0047	      console.log('Tracks to save:', tracks);
0048	      
0049	      if (tracks.length === 0) {
0050	        console.error('No tracks to save');
0051	        alert('There are no tracks to save');
0052	        return;
0053	      }
0054	      
0055	      // Send save playlist request to server - USING CORRECT ENDPOINT
0056	      fetch('/api/playlists', {
0057	        method: 'POST',
0058	        headers: {
0059	          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
0060	        },
0061	        body: JSON.stringify({
0062	          name: playlistName,
0063	          description: '',
0064	          tracks: tracks
0065	        })
0066	      })
0067	      .then(response => {
0068	        if (!response.ok) {
0069	          throw new Error(`Server returned ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`);
0070	        }
0071	        return response.json();
0072	      })
0073	      .then(data => {
0074	        console.log('Save playlist response:', data);
0075	        if (data.success || data.id) {
0076	          alert('Playlist saved successfully!');
0077	          // Optionally close modal if it exists
0078	          const modal = document.getElementById('save-playlist-modal');
0079	          if (modal && typeof modal.close === 'function') {
0080	            modal.close();
0081	          } else if (modal && modal.style) {
0082	            modal.style.display = 'none';
0083	          }
0084	          
0085	          // Reload playlists in sidebar
0086	          if (typeof window.loadSidebarPlaylists === 'function') {
0087	            window.loadSidebarPlaylists();
0088	          } else if (typeof window.loadPlaylists === 'function') {
0089	            window.loadPlaylists();
0090	          }
0091	        } else {
0092	          alert('Error saving playlist: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown error'));
0093	        }
0094	      })
0095	      .catch(error => {
0096	        console.error('Error saving playlist:', error);
0097	        alert('Error saving playlist: ' + error.message);
0098	      });
0099	    });
0100	    
0101	    console.log('Save playlist button initialized');
0102	  } else {
0103	    console.warn('Save Playlist button not found in DOM');
0104	  }
0105	});

0049	            });

0095	    // Function to close the save playlist modal
0096	    function closeSavePlaylistModal() {
0097	        const savePlaylistModal = document.getElementById('save-playlist-modal');
0098	        const playlistNameInput = document.getElementById('playlist-name');
0099	        const playlistDescriptionInput = document.getElementById('playlist-description');
0100	        
0101	        if (savePlaylistModal) {
0102	            savePlaylistModal.style.display = 'none';
0103	            
0104	            // Clear form fields
0105	            if (playlistNameInput) playlistNameInput.value = '';
0106	            if (playlistDescriptionInput) playlistDescriptionInput.value = '';
0107	        }
0108	    }
0109	    
0110	    // Function to handle form submission
0111	    function handleSavePlaylistSubmit() {
0112	        console.log('Save Playlist Fix: Handling form submission');
0113	        
0114	        const playlistNameInput = document.getElementById('playlist-name');
0115	        const playlistDescriptionInput = document.getElementById('playlist-description');
0116	        
0117	        if (!playlistNameInput) {
0118	            console.error('Save Playlist Fix: Playlist name input not found');
0119	            return;
0120	        }
0121	        
0122	        // Check if the currentPlaylist global variable exists and has tracks
0123	        if (!window.currentPlaylist || window.currentPlaylist.length === 0) {
0124	            console.error('Save Playlist Fix: No playlist to save');
0125	            showToast('No playlist to save', 'error');
0126	            return;
0127	        }
0128	        
0129	        const name = playlistNameInput.value.trim();
0130	        const description = playlistDescriptionInput ? playlistDescriptionInput.value.trim() : '';
0131	        
0132	        if (!name) {
0133	            showToast('Please enter a playlist name', 'error');
0134	            playlistNameInput.focus();
0135	            return;
0136	        }
0137	        
0138	        // Get track IDs
0139	        const tracks = window.currentPlaylist.map(track => track.id);
0140	        
0141	        // Prepare the data
0142	        const playlistData = {
0143	            name: name,
0144	            description: description,
0145	            tracks: tracks
0146	        };
0147	        
0148	        console.log('Saving playlist with data:', playlistData);
0149	        
0150	        // Save playlist with proper error handling
0151	        fetch('/api/playlists', { // Corrected endpoint
0152	            method: 'POST',
0153	            headers: {
0154	                'Content-Type': 'application/json'
0155	            },
0156	            body: JSON.stringify(playlistData)
0157	        })
0158	        .then(response => {
0159	            if (!response.ok) {
0160	                return response.json().then(data => {
0161	                    throw new Error(`Server returned ${response.status}: ${data.error || 'Unknown error'}`);
0162	                });
0163	            }
0164	            return response.json();
0165	        })
0166	        .then(data => {
0167	            console.log('Save Playlist Fix: Playlist saved successfully:', data);
0168	            
0169	            // Show success message
0170	            showToast('Playlist saved successfully!');
0171	            
0172	            // Close the modal
0173	            closeSavePlaylistModal();
0174	            
0175	            // Reload playlists in sidebar
0176	            if (typeof window.loadSidebarPlaylists === 'function') {
0177	                window.loadSidebarPlaylists();
0178	            }
0179	        })
0180	        .catch(error => {
0181	            console.error('Save Playlist Fix: Error saving playlist:', error);
0182	            
0183	            // Show error message
0184	            showToast(`Error saving playlist: ${error.message}`, 'error');
0185	        });
0186	    }
0187	    
0188	    // Function to show toast notifications
0189	    function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
0190	        // Check if toast container exists, create if not
0191	        let toastContainer = document.querySelector('.toast-container');
0192	        if (!toastContainer) {
0193	            toastContainer = document.createElement('div');
0194	            toastContainer.className = 'toast-container';
0195	            document.body.appendChild(toastContainer);
0196	        }
0197	        
0198	        // Create toast
0199	        const toast = document.createElement('div');
0200	        toast.className = `toast-notification ${type}`;
0201	        toast.textContent = message;
0202	        toastContainer.appendChild(toast);
0203	        
0204	        // Remove after 3 seconds
0205	        setTimeout(() => {
0206	            toast.classList.add('fade-out');
0207	            setTimeout(() => {
0208	                toast.remove();
0209	            }, 500);
0210	        }, 3000);
0211	    }
0212	    
0213	    // Initialize when DOM is ready
0214	    function init() {
0215	        console.log('Save Playlist Fix: DOM loaded, initializing');
0216	        
0217	        if (!initSavePlaylist()) {
0218	            // If initialization fails, retry after a short delay
0219	            console.log('Save Playlist Fix: Initialization failed, will retry in 500ms');
0220	            setTimeout(initSavePlaylist, 500);
0221	        }
0222	    }
0223	    
0224	    // Initialize immediately if DOM is already loaded
0225	    if (document.readyState === 'complete' || document.readyState === 'interactive') {
0226	        init();
0227	    } else {
0228	        // Otherwise wait for DOM to be ready
0229	        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
0230	    }
0231	    
0232	    // Also try to initialize when window is fully loaded
0233	    window.addEventListener('load', function() {
0234	        console.log('Save Playlist Fix: Window load event fired');
0235	        initSavePlaylist();
0236	    });
0237	})();

0193	function startFullAnalysis() {
0194	    const analyzeBtn = document.getElementById('analyze-button');
0195	    const path = document.getElementById('music-directory').value;
0196	    const recursive = document.getElementById('recursive-scan').checked;
0197	    
0198	    console.log('Starting analysis with path:', path, 'recursive:', recursive);
0199	    
0200	    if (!path) {
0201	        showMessage('Please enter a music folder path first', 'error');
0202	        return;
0203	    }
0204	    
0205	    // Show loading state
0206	    analyzeBtn.disabled = true;
0207	    analyzeBtn.textContent = 'Starting Analysis...';
0208	    
0209	    // Make a direct request to /analyze endpoint with the correct parameter name (directory, not path)
0210	    fetch('/analyze', {
0211	        method: 'POST',
0212	        headers: {
0213	            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
0214	        },
0215	        body: JSON.stringify({
0216	            directory: path,
0217	            recursive: recursive
0218	        })
0219	    })
0220	    .then(response => {
0221	        if (!response.ok) {
0222	            return response.json().then(errData => {
0223	                throw new Error(errData.message || `HTTP error ${response.status}`);
0224	            });
0225	        }
0226	        return response.json();
0227	    })
0228	    .then(data => {
0229	        console.log('Analysis response:', data);
0230	        
0231	        if (data.status === 'success' || data.success) {
0232	            showMessage('Analysis started!', 'success');
0233	            // Start polling to update progress
0234	            updateAnalysisStatus();
0235	        } else {
0236	            showMessage('Error starting analysis: ' + (data.error || data.message || 'Unknown error'), 'error');
0237	            analyzeBtn.disabled = false;
0238	            analyzeBtn.textContent = 'Full Analysis';
0239	        }
0240	    })
0241	    .catch(error => {

0243	        showMessage('Error starting analysis: ' + error, 'error');
0244	        analyzeBtn.disabled = false;
0245	        analyzeBtn.textContent = 'Full Analysis';
0246	    });
0247	}

0259	function startQuickScan() {
0260	    const quickScanBtn = document.getElementById('quick-scan-btn');
0261	    const pathInput = document.getElementById('music-directory');
0262	    const path = pathInput ? pathInput.value.trim() : '';
0263	    const recursiveInput = document.getElementById('recursive-scan');
0264	    const recursive = recursiveInput ? recursiveInput.checked : true;
0265	    
0266	    console.log('Starting quick scan with path:', path);
0267	    
0268	    // Validate path exists
0269	    if (!path) {
0270	        showMessage('Please enter a music folder path first', 'error');
0271	        return;
0272	    }
0273	    
0274	    // Show loading state
0275	    quickScanBtn.disabled = true;
0276	    quickScanBtn.textContent = 'Scanning...';
0277	    
0278	    // First save the path to configuration
0279	    fetch('/api/settings/save_music_path', {
0280	        method: 'POST',
0281	        headers: {
0282	            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
0283	        },
0284	        body: JSON.stringify({
0285	            path: path,
0286	            recursive: recursive
0287	        })
0288	    })
0289	    .then(response => response.json())
0290	    .then(data => {
0291	        console.log('Path saved response:', data);
0292	        
0293	        // Now initiate the scan with explicit path parameter
0294	        return fetch('/api/quick-scan', {
0295	            method: 'POST',
0296	            headers: {
0297	                'Content-Type': 'application/json'
0298	            },
0299	            body: JSON.stringify({
0300	                folder_path: path,
0301	                recursive: recursive
0302	            })
0303	        });
0304	    })
0305	    .then(response => {
0306	        if (!response.ok) {
0307	            return response.json().then(errData => {
0308	                throw new Error(errData.message || `HTTP error ${response.status}`);
0309	            });
0310	        }
0311	        return response.json();
0312	    })
0313	    .then(data => {
0314	        quickScanBtn.disabled = false;
0315	        quickScanBtn.textContent = 'Quick Scan';
0316	        showMessage(`Scan complete: ${data.files_processed || 0} files processed`, 'success');
0317	        startPollingQuickScanStatus();
0318	    })
0319	    .catch(error => {
0320	        quickScanBtn.disabled = false;
0321	        quickScanBtn.textContent = 'Quick Scan';
0322	        showMessage(`Error: ${error.message}`, 'error');
0323	        console.error('Quick scan error:', error);
0324	    });
0325	}

0327	// Add the polling function for quick scan status
0328	function startPollingQuickScanStatus() {
0329	    const statusContainer = document.getElementById('analysis-status');
0330	    const quickScanBtn = document.getElementById('quick-scan-btn');
0331	    
0332	    function checkStatus() {
0333	        fetch('/api/quick-scan/status')
0334	            .then(response => response.json())
0335	            .then(status => {
0336	                if (status.running) {
0337	                    // Update status display
0338	                    let statusHtml = `
0339	                        <div class="status-progress">
0340	                            <p>Quick scanning files...</p>
0341	                            <div class="progress-bar">
0342	                                <div class="progress-fill" style="width: ${status.percent_complete}%"></div>
0343	                            </div>
0344	                            <p>${status.files_processed} files processed</p>
0345	                        </div>
0346	                    `;
0347	                    
0348	                    statusContainer.innerHTML = statusHtml;
0349	                    
0350	                    // Keep button disabled
0351	                    quickScanBtn.disabled = true;
0352	                    quickScanBtn.textContent = 'Scanning...';
0353	                    
0354	                    // Continue polling
0355	                    setTimeout(checkStatus, 1000);
0356	                } else if (status.error) {
0357	                    // Show error
0358	                    statusContainer.innerHTML = `
0359	                        <div class="status-error">
0360	                            Error: ${status.error}
0361	                        </div>
0362	                    `;
0363	                    
0364	                    // Re-enable button
0365	                    quickScanBtn.disabled = false;
0366	                    quickScanBtn.textContent = 'Quick Scan';
0367	                } else if (status.files_processed > 0) {
0368	                    // Show success
0369	                    statusContainer.innerHTML = `
0370	                        <div class="status-success">
0371	                            Scan complete! Processed ${status.files_processed} files, added ${status.tracks_added} new tracks.
0372	                        </div>
0373	                    `;
0374	                    
0375	                    // Re-enable button
0376	                    quickScanBtn.disabled = false;
0377	                    quickScanBtn.textContent = 'Quick Scan';
0378	                }
0379	            })
0380	            .catch(error => {
0381	                console.error('Error checking quick scan status:', error);
0382	                // Continue polling even on error
0383	                setTimeout(checkStatus, 3000);
0384	            });
0385	    }
0386	    
0387	    // Start checking immediately
0388	    checkStatus();
0389	}

0410	    // Create form data with proper content type
0411	    const formData = new FormData();
0412	    formData.append('skip_existing', skipExisting ? 'true' : 'false');
0413	    
0414	    fetch('/api/update-metadata', {
0415	        method: 'POST',
0416	        body: formData  // Changed from JSON to FormData
0417	    })
0418	    .then(response => response.json())
0419	    .then(data => {
0420	        console.log('Metadata update response:', data);
0421	        if (data.status === 'started') {
0422	            showMessage('Metadata update started in background', 'success');
0423	            
0424	            // Start polling with minimal UI updates
0425	            startPollingMetadataStatus();
0426	            
0427	            // Trigger global status check right away
0428	            if (window.checkBackgroundTasks) {
0429	                window.checkBackgroundTasks();
0430	            }
0431	        } else {
0432	            showMessage(`Error: ${data.message || data.error || 'Unknown error'}`, 'error');
0433	            if (statusElem) {
0434	                statusElem.textContent = `Error: ${data.message || data.error || 'Unknown error'}`;
0435	            }
0436	            updateBtn.disabled = false;
0437	            updateBtn.textContent = 'Update Metadata';
0438	        }
0439	    })
0440	    .catch(error => {
0441	        console.error('Error:', error);
0442	        showMessage('Failed to start metadata update', 'error');
0443	        if (statusElem) {
0444	            statusElem.textContent = 'Failed to start metadata update';
0445	        }
0446	        updateBtn.disabled = false;
0447	        updateBtn.textContent = 'Update Metadata';
0448	    });
0449	}
0450	
0451	// Modify the startPollingMetadataStatus to work with shorter polling intervals
0452	// but only while on the settings page
0453	function startPollingMetadataStatus() {
0454	    const updateBtn = document.getElementById('update-metadata-btn');
0455	    const statusElem = document.getElementById('metadata-status-text');
0456	    
0457	    // Create a progress bar if it doesn't exist
0458	    let progressBar = document.getElementById('metadata-progress-bar');
0459	    let progressFill = document.getElementById('metadata-progress-fill');
0460	    
0461	    if (!progressBar) {
0462	        const statusContainer = statusElem.parentElement;
0463	        progressBar = document.createElement('div');
0464	        progressBar.id = 'metadata-progress-bar';
0465	        progressBar.className = 'progress-bar';
0466	        progressFill = document.createElement('div');
0467	        progressFill.id = 'metadata-progress-fill';
0468	        progressFill.className = 'progress-fill';
0469	        progressBar.appendChild(progressFill);
0470	        statusContainer.insertBefore(progressBar, statusElem);
0471	    }
0472	    
0473	    // We'll use a shorter polling interval for the settings page since it's the focused UI
0474	    const pollInterval = 1000; // 1 second
0475	    let timer = null;
0476	    
0477	    function checkStatus() {
0478	        fetch('/api/metadata-update/status')
0479	            .then(response => response.json())
0480	            .then(data => {
0481	                // Update progress bar
0482	                progressFill.style.width = `${data.percent_complete}%`;
0483	                
0484	                if (data.running) {
0485	                    const processed = data.processed_tracks || 0;
0486	                    const total = data.total_tracks || 0;
0487	                    const updated = data.updated_tracks || 0;
0488	                    
0489	                    statusElem.textContent = `Updating metadata: ${processed}/${total} tracks (${updated} updated)`;
0490	                    
0491	                    // Only continue polling if we're still on the settings page
0492	                    if (document.getElementById('update-metadata-btn')) {
0493	                        timer = setTimeout(checkStatus, pollInterval);
0494	                    }
0495	                } else if (data.error) {
0496	                    statusElem.textContent = `Error updating metadata: ${data.error}`;
0497	                    updateBtn.disabled = false;
0498	                    updateBtn.textContent = 'Update Metadata';
0499	                } else if (data.processed_tracks > 0) {
0500	                    // Update completed
0501	                    progressFill.style.width = '100%';
0502	                    statusElem.textContent = `Metadata update complete! ${data.updated_tracks} of ${data.total_tracks} tracks updated.`;
0503	                    updateBtn.disabled = false;
0504	                    updateBtn.textContent = 'Update Metadata';
0505	                }
0506	            })
0507	            .catch(error => {
0508	                console.error('Error checking metadata status:', error);
0509	                statusElem.textContent = 'Error checking update status';
0510	                updateBtn.disabled = false;
0511	                updateBtn.textContent = 'Update Metadata';
0512	            });
0513	    }
0514	    
0515	    // Start checking status with initial frequency
0516	    checkStatus();
0517	    
0518	    // Clean up function to stop polling
0519	    return function stopPolling() {
0520	        if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
0521	    };
0522	}
