0524	// Update artist images
0525	function updateArtistImages(service) {
0526	    const updateBtn = service === 'lastfm' ? 
0527	        document.getElementById('update-lastfm-btn') : 
0528	        document.getElementById('update-spotify-btn');
0529	    
0530	    const statusElem = document.getElementById('metadata-status-text');
0531	    
0532	    // Show loading state
0533	    updateBtn.disabled = true;
0534	    updateBtn.textContent = 'Updating...';
0535	    
0536	    if (statusElem) {
0537	        statusElem.textContent = `Starting artist image update via ${service}...`;
0538	        statusElem.parentElement.style.display = 'block';
0539	    }
0540	    
0541	    const endpoint = service === 'lastfm' ? 
0542	        '/api/update-artist-images' : 
0543	        '/api/update-artist-images/spotify';
0544	    
0545	    fetch(endpoint, {
0546	        method: 'POST'
0547	    })
0548	    .then(response => response.json())
0549	    .then(data => {
0550	        if (data.success) {
0551	            showMessage(`Artist images updated via ${service}! Updated ${data.updated} of ${data.total} artists.`, 'success');
0552	            if (statusElem) {
0553	                statusElem.textContent = `Artist images updated via ${service}! Updated ${data.updated} of ${data.total} artists.`;
0554	            }
0555	        } else {
0556	            showMessage(`Error: ${data.error || 'Unknown error'}`, 'error');
0557	            if (statusElem) {
0558	                statusElem.textContent = `Error: ${data.error || 'Unknown error'}`;
0559	            }
0560	        }
0561	    })
0562	    .catch(error => {
0563	        console.error('Error:', error);
0564	        showMessage(`Failed to update artist images via ${service}`, 'error');
0565	        if (statusElem) {
0566	            statusElem.textContent = `Failed to update artist images via ${service}`;
0567	        }
0568	    })
0569	    .finally(() => {
0570	        // Reset button
0571	        updateBtn.disabled = false;
0572	        updateBtn.textContent = service === 'lastfm' ? 'Update via LastFM' : 'Update via Spotify';
0573	    });
0574	}

0576	// Load cache statistics
0577	function loadCacheStats() {
0578	    const statsElem = document.querySelector('.cache-stats');
0579	    
0580	    if (!statsElem) return;
0581	    
0582	    statsElem.innerHTML = '<div class="stats-loading">Loading cache statistics...</div>';
0583	    
0584	    fetch('/cache/stats')
0585	        .then(response => response.json())
0586	        .then(data => {
0587	            if (data.status === 'success') {
0588	                let html = `
0589	                    <div class="stat-item">
0590	                        <div class="stat-label">Cache Directory:</div>
0591	                        <div class="stat-value">${data.cache_directory}</div>
0592	                    </div>
0593	                    <div class="stat-item">
0594	                        <div class="stat-label">Files Cached:</div>
0595	                        <div class="stat-value">${data.file_count}</div>
0596	                    </div>
0597	                    <div class="stat-item">
0598	                        <div class="stat-label">Total Size:</div>
0599	                        <div class="stat-value">${data.total_size_mb} MB / ${data.max_size_mb} MB</div>
0600	                    </div>
0601	                    <div class="stat-item">
0602	                        <div class="stat-label">Usage:</div>
0603	                        <div class="stat-value">
0604	                            <div class="cache-usage-bar">
0605	                                <div class="cache-usage-fill" style="width: ${data.usage_percent}%"></div>
0606	                            </div>
0607	                            <div class="cache-usage-text">${data.usage_percent}%</div>
0608	                        </div>
0609	                    </div>
0610	                `;
0611	                statsElem.innerHTML = html;
0612	            } else {
0613	                statsElem.innerHTML = '<div class="error">Failed to load cache statistics</div>';
0614	            }
0615	        })
0616	        .catch(error => {
0617	            console.error('Error:', error);
0618	            statsElem.innerHTML = '<div class="error">Error loading cache statistics</div>';
0619	        });
0620	}

0622	// Clear cache
0623	function clearCache() {
0624	    const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clear-cache-btn');
0625	    
0626	    // Show loading state
0627	    clearBtn.disabled = true;
0628	    clearBtn.textContent = 'Clearing...';
0629	    
0630	    fetch('/cache/clear', {
0631	        method: 'POST'
0632	    })
0633	    .then(response => response.json())
0634	    .then(data => {
0635	        if (data.status === 'success') {
0636	            showMessage(`Cache cleared successfully! Removed ${data.files_removed} files.`, 'success');
0637	            loadCacheStats(); // Refresh stats
0638	        } else {
0639	            showMessage(`Error: ${data.message || 'Unknown error'}`, 'error');
0640	        }
0641	    })
0642	    .catch(error => {
0643	        console.error('Error:', error);
0644	        showMessage('Failed to clear cache', 'error');
0645	    })
0646	    .finally(() => {
0647	        // Reset button
0648	        clearBtn.disabled = false;
0649	        clearBtn.textContent = 'Clear Cache';
0650	    });
0651	}

0653	// Update the analysis status with polling
0654	function updateAnalysisStatus() {
0655	    fetch('/api/analysis/status')
0656	        .then(response => response.json())
0657	        .then(status => {
0658	            const statusContainer = document.getElementById('analysis-status');
0659	            
0660	            if (!statusContainer) return;
0661	            
0662	            if (status.running) {
0663	                // Show progress
0664	                const percent = status.percent_complete || 0;
0665	                const filesProcessed = status.files_processed || 0;
0666	                
0667	                let statusHtml = '';
0668	                
0669	                if (percent < 50) {
0670	                    // Step 1: Quick scanning
0671	                    statusHtml = `
0672	                        <div class="status-progress">
0673	                            <p>Step 1/2: Quick scanning files...</p>
0674	                            <div class="progress-bar">
0675	                                <div class="progress-fill" style="width: ${percent*2}%"></div>
0676	                            </div>
0677	                            <p>${filesProcessed} files processed</p>
0678	                        </div>
0679	                    `;
0680	                } else {
0681	                    // Step 2: Feature analysis
0682	                    statusHtml = `
0683	                        <div class="status-progress">
0684	                            <p>Step 2/2: Analyzing audio features...</p>
0685	                            <div class="progress-bar">
0686	                                <div class="progress-fill" style="width: ${(percent-50)*2}%"></div>
0687	                            </div>
0688	                            <p>${filesProcessed} files processed</p>
0689	                        </div>
0690	                    `;
0691	                }
0692	                
0693	                statusContainer.innerHTML = statusHtml;
0694	                
0695	                // Poll again in 1 second
0696	                setTimeout(updateAnalysisStatus, 1000);
0697	            } else if (status.error) {
0698	                // Show error
0699	                statusContainer.innerHTML = `
0700	                    <div class="status-error">
0701	                        Error: ${status.error}
0702	                    </div>
0703	                `;
0704	            } else if (status.files_processed > 0) {
0705	                // Show success
0706	                statusContainer.innerHTML = `
0707	                    <div class="status-success">
0708	                        Analysis complete! ${status.files_processed} files processed.
0709	                    </div>
0710	                `;
0711	                
0712	                // Re-enable the analyze button
0713	                const analyzeBtn = document.getElementById('analyze-button');
0714	                if (analyzeBtn) {
0715	                    analyzeBtn.disabled = false;
0716	                    analyzeBtn.textContent = 'Full Analysis';
0717	                }
0718	            }
0719	        })
0720	        .catch(error => {
0721	            console.error('Error checking analysis status:', error);
0722	            // Poll again in 5 seconds
0723	            setTimeout(updateAnalysisStatus, 5000);
0724	        });
0725	}

0727	// Helper function to show messages
0728	function showMessage(message, type) {
0729	    const toast = document.createElement('div');
0730	    toast.className = `toast ${type}`;
0731	    toast.textContent = message;
0732	    
0733	    const container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
0734	    if (container) {
0735	        container.appendChild(toast);
0736	        
0737	        // Remove after 3 seconds
0738	        setTimeout(() => {
0739	            toast.classList.add('fade-out');
0740	            setTimeout(() => {
0741	                container.removeChild(toast);
0742	            }, 500);
0743	        }, 3000);
0744	    }
0745	}

0765	// Library Statistics
0766	function updateLibraryStats() {
0767	    const totalTracks = document.getElementById('total-tracks');
0768	    const tracksWithMetadata = document.getElementById('tracks-with-metadata');
0769	    const analyzedTracks = document.getElementById('analyzed-tracks');
0770	    const dbSize = document.getElementById('db-size');
0771	    const cacheSize = document.getElementById('cache-size');
0772	    
0773	    if (!totalTracks || !tracksWithMetadata || !analyzedTracks || !dbSize || !cacheSize) {
0774	        console.error('Library stats elements not found in DOM');
0775	        return;
0776	    }
0777	    
0778	    fetch('/api/library/stats')
0779	        .then(response => response.json())
0780	        .then(data => {
0781	            if (data.status === 'success') {
0782	                const stats = data.stats;
0783	                totalTracks.textContent = stats.total_tracks.toLocaleString();
0784	                
0785	                // Show metadata as percentage of total
0786	                const metadataPercent = stats.total_tracks > 0 
0787	                    ? Math.round((stats.tracks_with_metadata / stats.total_tracks) * 100) 
0788	                    : 0;
0789	                tracksWithMetadata.textContent = `${stats.tracks_with_metadata.toLocaleString()} (${metadataPercent}%)`;
0790	                
0791	                // Show analyzed as percentage of total
0792	                const analyzedPercent = stats.total_tracks > 0 
0793	                    ? Math.round((stats.analyzed_tracks / stats.total_tracks) * 100) 
0794	                    : 0;
0795	                analyzedTracks.textContent = `${stats.analyzed_tracks.toLocaleString()} (${analyzedPercent}%)`;
0796	                
0797	                // Show sizes in MB
0798	                dbSize.textContent = `${stats.db_size_mb} MB`;
0799	                cacheSize.textContent = `${stats.cache_size_mb} MB`;
0800	            } else {
0801	                console.error('Error fetching library stats:', data.message || 'Unknown error');
0802	            }
0803	        })
0804	        .catch(error => {
0805	            console.error('Error fetching library stats:', error);
0806	        });
0807	}

0809	// Add this event listener to clear current page on unload
0810	window.addEventListener('beforeunload', function() {
0811	    // Let the server know we're leaving the settings page
0812	    if (sessionStorage.getItem('currentPage') === 'settings') {
0813	        sessionStorage.removeItem('currentPage');
0814	        
0815	        // Stop any active polls
0816	        if (window.metadataPoller) {
0817	            window.metadataPoller();
0818	        }
0819	    }
0820	});
0821	
0822	// Add a function to handle the page visibility changes
0823	document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function() {
0824	    if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
0825	        // Page is hidden, slow down or pause polling
0826	        if (window.metadataPoller) {
0827	            window.metadataPoller();
0828	            window.metadataPoller = null;
0829	        }
0830	    } else if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && 
0831	              sessionStorage.getItem('currentPage') === 'settings') {
0832	        // Page is visible again, restart polling if we're on the settings page
0833	        window.metadataPoller = startPollingMetadataStatus();
0834	    }
0835	});

0870	// Update the next scheduled run display
0871	function updateNextRunDisplay() {
0872	    const scheduleFrequency = document.getElementById('schedule_frequency');
0873	    const nextRunElement = document.getElementById('next-run-time');
0874	    const nextRunContainer = document.getElementById('next-scheduled-run-info');
0875	    
0876	    if (scheduleFrequency && nextRunElement && nextRunContainer) {
0877	        const frequency = scheduleFrequency.value;
0878	        
0879	        if (frequency === 'never') {
0880	            nextRunElement.textContent = 'Not scheduled';
0881	            nextRunContainer.style.display = 'none';
0882	        } else {
0883	            nextRunContainer.style.display = 'block';
0884	            
0885	            // Request updated next run time from server
0886	            fetch('/api/next-scheduled-run')
0887	                .then(response => response.json())
0888	                .then(data => {
0889	                    if (data.next_run) {
0890	                        nextRunElement.textContent = data.next_run;
0891	                    }
0892	                })
0893	                .catch(error => {
0894	                    console.error('Error fetching next run time:', error);
0895	                });
0896	        }
0897	    }
0898	}

0944	// Add this function to improve analysis status display
0945	function updateAnalysisDisplay() {
0946	    // Fetch both statuses
0947	    Promise.all([
0948	        fetch('/api/analysis/status').then(r => r.json()),
0949	        fetch('/api/analysis/database-status').then(r => r.json())
0950	    ])
0951	    .then(([status, dbStatus]) => {
0952	        console.log('Status API:', status);
0953	        console.log('Database status:', dbStatus);
0954	        
0955	        // Use database numbers if API returns lower counts
0956	        const totalFiles = Math.max(status.total_files || 0, dbStatus.total || 0);
0957	        const processedFiles = Math.max(status.files_processed || 0, dbStatus.analyzed || 0);
0958	        
0959	        // Safely update UI elements - check if they exist first
0960	        const totalElem = document.getElementById('analysis-status-total');
0961	        const processedElem = document.getElementById('analysis-status-processed');
0962	        const pendingElem = document.getElementById('analysis-status-pending');
0963	        const analysisStatusElem = document.getElementById('analysis-status');
0964	        
0965	        // Only update elements if they exist
0966	        if (totalElem) totalElem.textContent = totalFiles;
0967	        if (processedElem) processedElem.textContent = processedFiles;
0968	        if (pendingElem) pendingElem.textContent = totalFiles - processedFiles;
0969	        
0970	        // Update progress bar if it exists
0971	        const progressBar = document.getElementById('analysis-progress-bar');
0972	        if (progressBar) {
0973	            const percent = totalFiles > 0 ? (processedFiles / totalFiles) * 100 : 0;
0974	            progressBar.style.width = `${percent}%`;
0975	            progressBar.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', percent);
0976	        }
0977	        
0978	        // Update running status
0979	        if (status.running && analysisStatusElem) {
0980	            analysisStatusElem.textContent = 'Running';
0981	            // Schedule next update
0982	            setTimeout(updateAnalysisDisplay, 2000);
0983	        } else if (analysisStatusElem) {
0984	            analysisStatusElem.textContent = 'Idle';
0985	        }
0986	    })
0987	    .catch(error => {
0988	        console.error('Error updating analysis display:', error);
0989	    });
0990	}

1000	// Make sure these event listeners are being attached properly
1001	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
1002	    // Quick Scan button
1003	    const quickScanBtn = document.getElementById('quick-scan-btn');
1004	    if (quickScanBtn) {
1005	        quickScanBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
1006	            const musicPath = document.getElementById('music-directory').value;
1007	            const recursive = document.getElementById('recursive-scan').checked;
1008	            
1009	            // Show status before making request
1010	            const statusText = document.getElementById('analysis-status-text');
1011	            if (statusText) {
1012	                statusText.textContent = "Starting quick scan...";
1013	            }
1014	            
1015	            // Make AJAX request to scan endpoint
1016	            fetch('/api/quick-scan', {
1017	                method: 'POST',
1018	                headers: {
1019	                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
1020	                },
1021	                body: JSON.stringify({ 
1022	                    folder_path: musicPath,
1023	                    recursive: recursive
1024	                }),
1025	            })
1026	            .then(response => response.json())
1027	            .then(data => {

1176	function updateAnalysisDisplay() {
1177	    // Fetch both statuses
1178	    Promise.all([
1179	        fetch('/api/analysis/status').then(r => r.json()),
1180	        fetch('/api/analysis/database-status').then(r => r.json())
1181	    ])
1182	    .then(([status, dbStatus]) => {
1183	        console.log('Status API:', status);
1184	        console.log('Database status:', dbStatus);
1185	        
1186	        // Use database numbers if API returns lower counts
1187	        const totalFiles = Math.max(status.total_files || 0, dbStatus.total || 0);
1188	        const processedFiles = Math.max(status.files_processed || 0, dbStatus.analyzed || 0);
1189	        
1190	        // Safely update UI elements - check if they exist first
1191	        const totalElem = document.getElementById('analysis-status-total');
1192	        const processedElem = document.getElementById('analysis-status-processed');
1193	        const pendingElem = document.getElementById('analysis-status-pending');
1194	        const analysisStatusElem = document.getElementById('analysis-status');
1195	        
1196	        // Only update elements if they exist
1197	        if (totalElem) totalElem.textContent = totalFiles;
1198	        if (processedElem) processedElem.textContent = processedFiles;
1199	        if (pendingElem) pendingElem.textContent = totalFiles - processedFiles;
1200	        
1201	        // Update progress bar if it exists
1202	        const progressBar = document.getElementById('analysis-progress-bar');
1203	        if (progressBar) {
1204	            const percent = totalFiles > 0 ? (processedFiles / totalFiles) * 100 : 0;
1205	            progressBar.style.width = `${percent}%`;
1206	            progressBar.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', percent);
1207	        }
1208	        
1209	        // Update running status
1210	        if (status.running && analysisStatusElem) {
1211	            analysisStatusElem.textContent = 'Running';
1212	            // Schedule next update
1213	            setTimeout(updateAnalysisDisplay, 2000);
1214	        } else if (analysisStatusElem) {
1215	            analysisStatusElem.textContent = 'Idle';
1216	        }
1217	    })
1218	    .catch(error => {
1219	        console.error('Error updating analysis display:', error);
1220	    });
1221	}

1309	        console.log('Added click handler to stop analysis button');

1317	// Add a function to stop analysis
1318	function stopAnalysis() {
1319	    console.log('Stopping analysis');
1320	    
1321	    fetch('/api/analysis/stop', {
1322	        method: 'POST'
1323	    })
1324	    .then(response => response.json())
1325	    .then(data => {
1326	        if (data.status === 'success') {
1327	            showMessage('Analysis stopped successfully', 'success');
1328	        } else {
1329	            showMessage('Failed to stop analysis: ' + data.message, 'error');
1330	        }
1331	    })
1332	    .catch(error => {
1333	        console.error('Error stopping analysis:', error);
1334	        showMessage('Error stopping analysis', 'error');
1335	    });
1336	}

0043	    function initializeStatusChecking() {
0044	        // Check analysis status periodically
0045	        function checkAnalysisStatus() {
0046	            fetch('/api/analysis/status')
0047	                .then(response => response.json())
0048	                .then(data => {
0049	                    console.log('Analysis status update:', data);
0050	                    // Update UI with status - show progress if either running is true OR files are being processed
0051	                    if (data.running || (data.files_processed > 0 && data.files_processed < data.total_files)) {
0052	                        // Calculate percent complete - either use the API value or calculate it
0053	                        let percentComplete = data.percent_complete;
0054	                        if (!percentComplete && data.total_files > 0) {
0055	                            percentComplete = Math.round((data.files_processed / data.total_files) * 100);
0056	                        }
0057	                        
0058	                        // Make the indicator visible - force display with !important
0059	                        analysisStatusIndicator.style.cssText = 'display: block !important; visibility: visible !important; opacity: 1 !important; background-color: rgba(0,0,0,0.3); padding: 10px; margin: 10px 0; border-radius: 4px;';
0060	                        analysisStatusIndicator.classList.add('active');
0061	                        analysisStatusIndicator.innerHTML = `
0062	                            <div class="progress-bar">
0063	                                <div class="progress-fill" style="width: ${percentComplete}%; background-color: #4caf50;"></div>
0064	                            </div>
0065	                            <div class="progress-status-text" style="color: white; font-size: 12px; text-align: center; margin-top: 5px;">
0066	                                Analyzing: ${data.files_processed}/${data.total_files} files (${percentComplete}%)
0067	                            </div>
0068	                        `;
0069	                        console.log('Analysis progress shown:', percentComplete + '%');
0070	                    } else {
0071	                        analysisStatusIndicator.classList.remove('active');
0072	                        analysisStatusIndicator.style.cssText = 'display: none;';
0073	                        analysisStatusIndicator.innerHTML = `
0074	                            <div class="progress-bar">
0075	                                <div class="progress-fill" style="width: 0%"></div>
0076	                            </div>
0077	                            <div class="progress-status-text">Analysis idle</div>
0078	                        `;
0079	                    }
0080	                })
0081	                .catch(error => {
0082	                    console.error('Error fetching analysis status:', error);
0083	                });
0084	        }
0085

0154	    function initializeSidebarNavigation() {
0155	        // Add active class to current page link
0156	        const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
0157	        
0158	        document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-nav a, .nav-button').forEach(link => {
0159	            const href = link.getAttribute('href');
0160	            if (href === currentPath || 
0161	                (currentPath === '/' && href === '#') ||
0162	                (href !== '#' && currentPath.startsWith(href))) {
0163	                link.classList.add('active');
0164	            }
0165	        });
0166	    }

0168	    // Retry loading playlists after a delay in case the server is still initializing
0169	    function retryLoadPlaylists() {
0170	        setTimeout(() => {
0171	            // Only retry loading if playlists haven't been loaded successfully yet
0172	            console.log('Retrying playlist load...');
0173	            window.loadSidebarPlaylists();
0174	        }, 10000); // Try again after 10 seconds
0175	    }
0176	    
0177	    // Set up a retry mechanism
0178	    retryLoadPlaylists();
0179	    
0180	    // Make loadSidebarPlaylists globally accessible for other pages to call
0181	    window.loadSidebarPlaylists = loadSidebarPlaylists;
0182	});

0184	// Function to load playlists into the sidebar
0185	window.loadSidebarPlaylists = function() {
0186	    console.log('Loading playlists from API');
0187	    
0188	    const playlistsContainer = document.getElementById('playlist-list');
0189	    if (!playlistsContainer) {
0190	        console.error('Playlists container not found in sidebar.');
0191	        return;
0192	    }
0193	
0194	    playlistsContainer.innerHTML = '<li class="loading">Loading playlists...</li>';
0195	    
0196	    fetch('/api/playlists')
0197	        .then(response => {
0198	            console.log('Playlists response status:', response.status, 'ok:', response.ok);
0199	            if (!response.ok) {
0200	                // Try to get error message from response body
0201	                return response.json().then(errData => {
0202	                    throw new Error(`Playlist endpoint not available: ${response.status} - ${errData.error || 'Unknown server error'}`);
0203	                }).catch(() => {
0204	                    // If parsing error body fails
0205	                    throw new Error(`Playlist endpoint not available: ${response.status}`);
0206	                });
0207	            }
0208	            return response.json();
0209	        })
0210	        .then(data => {
0211	            console.log('Playlists data received (raw from server):', JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
0212	            console.log('Type of received data:', typeof data, 'Is Array:', Array.isArray(data));
0213	            
0214	            playlistsContainer.innerHTML = ''; 
0215	            
0216	            if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
0217	                console.error('Playlists data is not an array:', data);
0218	                playlistsContainer.innerHTML = '<li class="error-playlists">Error: Invalid playlist data format from server</li>';
0219	                return;
0220	            }
0221	
0222	            if (data.length === 0) {
0223	                playlistsContainer.innerHTML = '<li class="empty-playlists">No playlists found</li>';
0224	                return;
0225	            }
0226	            
0227	            let playlistsAdded = 0;
0228	            data.forEach(playlist => {
0229	                console.log('Processing playlist:', playlist, 'Type:', typeof playlist);
0230	
0231	                if (typeof playlist !== 'object' || playlist === null) {
0232	                    console.error('Playlist item is not an object:', playlist);
0233	                    return; // Skip this non-object item
0234	                }
0235	
0236	                const playlistId = playlist.id || playlist.playlist_id || playlist._id; 
0237	
0238	                if (playlistId === undefined || playlistId === null) { // Added null check
0239	                    console.error('Playlist ID is undefined or null for playlist:', playlist);
0240	                    // Do not return; allow other playlists to be processed.
0241	                    // This item will be skipped for rendering.
0242	                } else {
0243	                    const playlistItem = document.createElement('li');
0244	                    playlistItem.className = 'playlist-item';
0245	
0246	                    const playlistLink = document.createElement('a');
0247	                    playlistLink.href = `/?playlist=${playlistId}`; 
0248	                    playlistLink.className = 'playlist-link';
0249	
0250	                    let playlistName = 'Untitled Playlist';
0251	                    if (playlist.name) {
0252	                        playlistName = playlist.name;
0253	                    } else if (playlist.playlist_name) {
0254	                        playlistName = playlist.playlist_name;
0255	                    } else if (typeof playlist === 'object' && playlist !== null) {
0256	                        if (Array.isArray(playlist) && playlist.length > 1 && typeof playlist[1] === 'string') {
0257	                            playlistName = playlist[1];
0258	                        }
0259	                    }
0260	                    
0261	                    playlistLink.textContent = playlistName;
0262	                    playlistLink.title = playlist.description || '';
0263	                    
0264	                    playlistLink.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
0265	                        e.preventDefault();
0266	                        
0267	                        // Re-check ID just in case, though it should be valid here
0268	                        const currentId = playlist.id || playlist.playlist_id || playlist._id;
0269	                        if (currentId === undefined || currentId === null) {
0270	                            console.error("Cannot load playlist: ID is undefined or null at click time.");
0271	                            return;
0272	                        }
0273	                        const newUrl = `/?playlist=${currentId}`;
0274	                        window.history.pushState({ url: newUrl }, '', newUrl);
0275	                        
0276	                        if (typeof window.loadPlaylist === 'function') {
0277	                            window.loadPlaylist(currentId); 
0278	                        } else {
0279	                            console.error('loadPlaylist function not available');
0280	                        }
0281	                        
0282	                        document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar a').forEach(link => {
0283	                            link.classList.remove('active');
0284	                        });
0285	                        // this.classList.add('active'); // Active class should be set by navigation logic or after load
0286	                    });
0287	                    
0288	                    playlistItem.appendChild(playlistLink);
0289	                    playlistsContainer.appendChild(playlistItem);
0290	                    playlistsAdded++;
0291	                }
0292	            });
0293	            
0294	            if (playlistsAdded === 0 && data.length > 0) {
0295	                playlistsContainer.innerHTML = '<li class="empty-playlists">No valid playlists to display (all items lacked a valid ID).</li>';
0296	            } else if (playlistsAdded === 0 && data.length === 0) {
0297	                // This case is already handled by "No playlists found"
0298	            } else if (playlistsAdded > 0) {
0299	                console.log(`Playlists loaded successfully, count: ${playlistsAdded}`);
0300	            }
0301	        })
0302	        .catch(error => {
0303	            console.error('Error loading playlists:', error.message);
0304	            if (playlistsContainer) {
0305	                playlistsContainer.innerHTML = `<li class="error-playlists">Failed to load playlists: ${error.message}</li>`;
0306	            }
0307	        });
0308	};
0309	
0310	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0311	    // Only call loadSidebarPlaylists if it hasn't been loaded already
0312	    if (typeof window.loadSidebarPlaylists === 'function') {
0313	        window.loadSidebarPlaylists();
0314	    } else if (!window.loadSidebarPlaylists) {
0315	        console.error("loadSidebarPlaylists is not defined globally when DOMContentLoaded fires.");
0316	    }
0317	
0318	    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .nav-buttons > a, .sidebar .sub-nav-buttons > a');
0319	    navLinks.forEach(link => {
0320	        link.addEventListener('click', function() {
0321	            document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar a').forEach(lnk => {
0322	                lnk.classList.remove('active');
0323	            });
0324	            this.classList.add('active');
0325	        });
0326	    });
0327	});

0071	        // Global analysis status checking
0072	        const globalStatusBar = document.getElementById('global-status-bar');
0073	        let globalStatusInterval = null;
0074	        
0075	        function checkGlobalAnalysisStatus() {
0076	            fetch('/api/analysis/status')
0077	                .then(response => response.json())
0078	                .then(data => {
0079	                    // Only show this for errors, hide during normal analysis (sidebar will handle that)
0080	                    if (data.running) {
0081	                        // Hide the global status bar during analysis
0082	                        globalStatusBar.style.display = 'none';
0083	                    } else if (data.error) {
0084	                        // Show errors in the global bar
0085	                        const statusHtml = `
0086	                            <div class="status-content">
0087	                                <div class="status-icon error"></div>
0088	                                <div class="status-text">
0089	                                    <strong>Analysis Error:</strong> 
0090	                                    ${data.error}
0091	                                </div>
0092	                                <div class="status-dismiss" onclick="dismissGlobalStatus()">Ã—</div>
0093	                            </div>
0094	                        `;
0095	                        
0096	                        globalStatusBar.innerHTML = statusHtml;
0097	                        globalStatusBar.style.display = 'block';
0098	                        globalStatusBar.classList.add('status-error');
0099	                        globalStatusBar.classList.remove('status-success');
0100	                    }
0101	                })
0102	                .catch(error => console.error('Error checking analysis status:', error));
0103	        }
0104	        
0105	        function dismissGlobalStatus() {
0106	            if (globalStatusBar) {
0107	                globalStatusBar.style.display = 'none';
0108	                globalStatusBar.classList.remove('status-error', 'status-success');
0109	            }
0110	        }
0111	        
0112	        // Make functions available globally
0113	        window.dismissGlobalStatus = dismissGlobalStatus;
0114	        window.globalStatusBar = globalStatusBar;
0115	        window.checkGlobalAnalysisStatus = checkGlobalAnalysisStatus;
0116	        
0117	        // Ensure this runs first before any page-specific scripts
0118	        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
0119	            // Initial check on page load - run immediately
0120	            checkGlobalAnalysisStatus();
0121	            
0122	            // Also start polling right away if no interval exists
0123	            if (!globalStatusInterval) {
0124	                globalStatusInterval = setInterval(checkGlobalAnalysisStatus, 2000);
0125	            }
0126	        }, { once: true });  // Using 'once: true' to ensure it only runs once
0127	    </script>
